## Summary

Snapback 4 is one of two parallel candidates for the successor to Snapback 2,
and the sibling of Snapback 3, not its successor. It builds
[LLP 2000.000](./2000.000-keep-the-core-flip-two-substrates.rfc.md) as
written: the Snapback 2 core kept verbatim, two substrates flipped up front —
resources metered at runtime instead of priced statically, and a client that
replicates the viewer's declared partition instead of caching receipted
results — proved first on the hub fixture, with a deterministic simulator as
the primary harness. Snapback 3 ([LLP 2000](./2000-snapback3.rfc.md), led by
Codex / GPT-6 Astra) keeps the same two questions open and discovers their
answers in its first slice. Snapback 2 ([LLP 1000](./1000-snapback2.explainer.md))
continues as the incumbent and the control. All three are judged by one
pre-registered comparison on one clock.

This document records the author's decisions of 2026-09-12 that created the
track, restates the bet on one page so a reader can orient without the brief,
fixes the bakeoff protocol, draws the fence, and plans the first four weeks.
The design argument and its evidence stay in LLP 2000.000. Only running code
will bind (`rules/RULES.md`).

## 1. Decisions (author, 2026-09-12)

On 2026-09-12 Charlie Cheever asked whether Claude Fable 5.1 should lead a
Snapback 4 while Codex / GPT-6 Astra leads Snapback 3 and Snapback 2
continues, "and see which one turns out best." The lead answered yes with
conditions; the author accepted them ("Sounds good") and asked for this
document. The decisions below are therefore the lead's proposal as accepted
by the author. Where the author has not separately ruled, the text says so.

1. **Three tracks, one comparison.** Snapback 2 continues as the incumbent
   and the control. Snapback 3 builds LLP 2000; Snapback 4 builds LLP
   2000.000. This is a bakeoff of *bets* — discover the substrates in the
   slice versus decide them first — not of models. The result will say more
   about the bets than about the leads, and §3 is written so that it can.
2. **The name is Snapback 4.** Siblings, not a sequence: "4" does not mean
   "after 3." A separate number gives the track its own corpus root,
   directory, package identity and data directory with no rename when the
   bakeoff resolves, and files never move in LLP. If Snapback 4 loses, it is
   a number that never shipped. "Snapback 3b" was considered and rejected:
   it reads as a variant of 3, and this is a different bet.
3. **Snapback 4 builds LLP 2000.000 as written.** Its §14 open questions are
   the lead's to decide inside the track, recorded in §7 as they are
   decided. The author rules on the bar, the comparison and promotion (§3).
4. **One owner on the core.** The lead writes the engine, the store and the
   client contract. Codex lanes build the fixture generator, the reference
   applications, the device witnesses and the periphery. Blind reviews from
   two model families are reserved for the two disclosure-sensitive
   mechanisms: partition inversion of read rules (LLP 2000.000 §7.1) and
   the meter's cut behaviour on non-uniform scans (LLP 2000.000 §6). This
   departs from the standing rule that Codex codes and Claude orchestrates,
   deliberately, for the core only.
5. **The clock is four weeks** from the first commit under `snapback4/` to
   the first slice running on the hub fixture. At the clock the author kills
   the track, merges its substrates into a survivor, or continues it. There
   is no third month without a ruling.
6. **Shared and not shared.** Shared with Snapback 3: the LLP corpus, the
   Clique harness and scorers, the fixtures, the briefs, and the simulator
   once either track has one. Not shared: code, except the Snapback 2 pieces
   both are expected to copy (LLP 2000.000 §5.1).
7. **Snapback 2 keeps shipping and freezes new substrate work for the
   duration.** Fix-forward, the loop bar, acquisition and papercuts continue;
   no new certificate axes, client custody mechanisms or ceiling changes.
   This is accepted here as a condition of the bakeoff. Making it binding on
   concurrent Snapback 2 sessions needs one line in `rules/NOT-DOING.md`'s
   Moves ledger, which this document does not write.
8. **Reading load.** This root, updated in place, and one append-only
   journal, `snapback4/JOURNAL.md`. Sub-LLPs only for a decision that must
   outlive the journal. No panels beyond decision 4's two reviews.

## 2. The bet on one page

**Keep verbatim from Snapback 2** (LLP 2000.000 §5.1): the rule language
with its uniformity and hoist analysis, `decided_true` and the
structural-fact refusals; one data language, Quarry, with `--` comments,
`require … else CODE`, `shape`, `merge`, `walk`, `chunk`; the value model
and its conformance corpus; typed read and write states with no silent state
and the refusal envelope `{code, family, site, message, rewrite, guide}`; the
successor classifier (widening additive, checked narrowing, exact projection
ownership); `deliver`'s engine-computed audience, tombstones and
`bootstrap`; `schedule`, `job`, `chunk` and rehearsal; personas; `init`,
`dev`, `check`, `doctor`, `run`, `test`, `why`, `trace`, `guide`; H0
`deploy`, `rollback`, `secret`; channels; assets; the word-prefix index.

**Flip 1 — certify authorization, meter resources** (LLP 2000.000 §6). The
certificate keeps every read site's shape, uniformity, completeness class
and prediction eligibility, and stops pricing bytes and steps. A
deterministic meter charges examined rows, index entries, rule probes,
steps, heap, output and rows written as the program runs, on the server and
in the client interpreter alike. A query cut by the meter returns `capped`
with no continuation past the cut, the same observable outcome as a static
ceiling cut today; a mutation aborts with `E_BOUND` naming actual work; a
job is chunked. `check --cost` prints the structural cost class; `test`
prints measured meters on the hub fixture.

**Flip 2 — the viewer's partition is the unit of sync** (LLP 2000.000 §7).
`sync to <audience> last N by <index>` on a table makes the engine maintain,
per principal, the rows that principal may read, as a change log with a
horizon; a table without the word is online-only, and `check` says which
rule term prevented inversion. The client holds a replica of its partitions,
one watermark each, a durable outbox with client-minted ids, and the
interpreter; queries run locally and are `complete` inside the horizon.
Revocation is a scope tombstone; `until-revoked` and `delivered-history`
survive as the client's leave policy. Two client tiers — online-only
without a store, local-first with one — share one language and one server.

**The vocabulary is fixed before the mechanism** (LLP 2000.000 §8):

```ts
type Read<T> = { data: T; complete: boolean; fresh: boolean; since?: number; next?: Cursor }
            | { loading: true } | { denied: Refusal };
row.pending: boolean;
type Write = { state: "pending" } | { state: "sent"; seq: number } | { state: "failed"; why: Refusal };
type Link = "online" | "offline" | "signed-out";
```

A screen that needs a word not on that card is a defect in the mechanism,
not a gap in the card.

**The hub comes first** (LLP 2000.000 §11): the Clique generator's `u0001`
with 1,000 memberships, `u0500` with 500 follows, and about 100,000
messages are the first fixture; day-two changes are designed before the
day-one screen; the native client is in slice one; physical floors are
measured before targets; the simulator is the primary harness.

## 3. The bakeoff protocol

Shared with Snapback 3; LLP 2000 or a sibling record should adopt the same
text so that the two tracks are measured by one instrument.

**Brief.** [`benchmarks/briefs/social-messenger.md`](../benchmarks/briefs/social-messenger.md)
(Clique) M1–M8, plus
[`benchmarks/briefs/messaging-day2-evolution.md`](../benchmarks/briefs/messaging-day2-evolution.md).
The field-work application (LLP 2000 §4) tests generality after the first
slice, on whichever tracks survive the clock.

**Fixture.** The Clique generator at the instrument freeze LLP 1023 and
LLP 1024 record (`f40559f37722c79178be664a11f8a6485f1508fd`): hub `u0001`
with 100 groups and 900 DMs, `u0500` with 500 follows, and the 100,015

     let pure_domain = checked(&format!(r#"{schema}
 fn sameOwner(owner: principal):
     1	//! Exact non-empty facts established by successful count guards.
     2	
     3	use super::*;
     4	use std::collections::BTreeSet;
     5	
     6	pub(super) const NONEMPTY: &str = "$aggregateNonempty";
     7	/// Capture marker for a local whose current value is an exact `count(domain)`
     8	/// observation. It lives on the result and every domain root: ordinary
     9	/// rebinding, a write, an authority edge, or a control-flow meet therefore
    10	/// destroys stale provenance without repurposing diagnostic `origins`.
    11	pub(super) const COUNT_CAPTURE: &str = "$aggregateCountCapture";
    12	
    13	impl Binder<'_> {
    14	    /// A non-empty observation survives evaluation of its guard only when the
    15	    /// complete condition is write-free. Unlike point facts, aggregate facts
    16	    /// deliberately admit filters and seeks because those are part of the
    17	    /// exact domain identity recorded below.
    18	    pub(super) fn aggregate_guard_safe(&self, value: &A) -> bool {
    19	        match value {
    20	            A::Name { .. } | A::Focal { .. } | A::Literal { .. } => true,
    21	            A::Array { items, .. } => items.iter().all(|item| self.aggregate_guard_safe(item)),
    22	            A::Object { fields, .. } => fields.iter().all(|field| field.value.as_ref().is_none_or(|value| self.aggregate_guard_safe(value))),
    23	            A::Unary { value, .. } | A::Assert { value, .. } | A::ShapeUse { base: value, .. } => self.aggregate_guard_safe(value),
    24	            A::Binary { left, right, .. } => self.aggregate_guard_safe(left) && self.aggregate_guard_safe(right),
    25	            A::Member { base, .. } => self.aggregate_guard_safe(base),
    26	            A::Call { name, args, .. } => self.catalog.functions.get(name).is_none_or(|function|
    27	                self.pure_functions.get(&function.name) == Some(&true))
    28	                && args.iter().all(|arg| self.aggregate_guard_safe(arg)),
    29	            A::Read(read) | A::Exists { read, .. } | A::Created { read, .. } => {
    30	                read.computed.values().all(|value| self.aggregate_guard_safe(value))
    31	                    && read.keys.iter().all(|key| self.aggregate_guard_safe(&key.value))
    32	                    && read.seeks.iter().all(|seek| self.aggregate_guard_safe(&seek.value))
    33	                    && read.filter.as_deref().is_none_or(|filter| self.aggregate_guard_safe(filter))
    34	                    && read.matching.as_deref().is_none_or(|matching| self.aggregate_guard_safe(matching))
    35	                    && read.range.as_ref().and_then(|range| range.cursor.as_deref()).is_none_or(|cursor| self.aggregate_guard_safe(cursor))
    36	            }
    37	            A::Shape { base, fields, .. } => self.aggregate_guard_safe(base)
    38	                && fields.iter().all(|field| match field {
    39	                    ShapeField::Field { value, .. } => self.aggregate_guard_safe(value),
    40	                    ShapeField::Spread { .. } | ShapeField::NamedSpread { .. } | ShapeField::Bare { .. } => true,
    41	                }),
    42	            A::If { then, condition, otherwise, .. } => self.aggregate_guard_safe(then)
    43	                && self.aggregate_guard_safe(condition)
    44	                && self.aggregate_guard_safe(otherwise),
    45	            A::Merge(_) | A::Walk { .. } | A::Write { .. } => false,
    46	        }
    47	    }
    48	
    49	    /// Record only implications that follow from count's nonnegative range.
    50	    /// The common authored form is `require n > 0`, where `n` was bound from
    51	    /// `count(domain)`; direct count calls use the same identity.
    52	    // @ref LLP 3000.013.001#4-non-empty-domain-proof — the fact belongs to the exact domain roots and transaction state.
    53	    pub(super) fn record_aggregate_nonempty(&mut self, condition: &A, truth: bool) {
    54	        if !truth { return; }
    55	        let A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = condition else { return };
    56	        let (candidate, threshold, op) = if let Some(n) = literal_int(right) {
    57	            (left.as_ref(), n, op.as_str())
    58	        } else if let Some(n) = literal_int(left) {
    59	            let reversed = match op.as_str() { "<" => ">", "<=" => ">=", ">" => "<", ">=" => "<=", other => other };
    60	            (right.as_ref(), n, reversed)
    61	        } else { return };
    62	        let proves_positive = match op {
    63	            ">" => threshold >= 0,
    64	            ">=" | "=" => threshold >= 1,
    65	            "!=" => threshold == 0,
    66	            _ => false,
    67	        };
    68	        if !proves_positive { return; }
    69	        let Some((key, roots)) = self.count_key(candidate) else { return };
    70	        for root in roots {
    71	            let Some(mut binding) = self.binding(&root).cloned() else { return };
    72	            binding.fields.insert(key.clone(), Ty::Bool);
    73	            self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(root, binding);
    74	        }
    75	    }
    76	
    77	    pub(super) fn aggregate_nonempty(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
    78	        self.aggregate_key(read).is_some_and(|(key, roots)| roots.iter().all(|root|
    79	            self.binding(root).is_some_and(|binding| binding.fields.get(&key) == Some(&Ty::Bool))))
    80	    }
    81	
    82	    /// Resolve count provenance before rebinding `name`: a count domain may
    83	    /// itself refer to the old value of that local. Such a self-dependent
    84	    /// capture cannot be represented by the post-assignment binding and is
    85	    /// therefore deliberately not narrowed.
    86	    pub(super) fn count_capture(&self, name: &str, value: &A) -> Option<(String, BTreeSet<String>)> {
    87	        let A::Call { name: call, args, .. } = value else { return None };
    88	        if call != "count" || args.len() != 1 || self.catalog.functions.contains_key(call)
    89	            || !self.aggregate_guard_safe(value) { return None; }
    90	        let read = args.first().and_then(|arg| self.aggregate_domain(arg))?;
    91	        let (aggregate, roots) = self.aggregate_key(&read)?;
    92	        if roots.contains(name) { return None; }
    93	        Some((aggregate[1].clone(), roots))
    94	    }
    95	
    96	    /// Attach pre-resolved capture-time provenance after assigning `name`.
    97	    /// `origins` remains diagnostic history; this field fact is the reaching
    98	    /// definition proof used by a later `require name > 0`.
    99	    pub(super) fn record_count_capture(&mut self, name: &str, capture: Option<(String, BTreeSet<String>)>) {
   100	        let Some((aggregate, mut owners)) = capture else { return };
   101	        let Some(result) = self.binding(name) else { return };
   102	        // The reaching definition is part of the marker. A later count over
   103	        // the same textual domain may restore a root marker after rebinding,
   104	        // but it must never make an older captured result current again.
   105	        let capture = vec![COUNT_CAPTURE.into(), aggregate,
   106	            format!("{}:{}", result.runtime, result.revision)];
   107	        owners.insert(name.to_owned());
   108	        for owner in owners {
   109	            let Some(mut binding) = self.binding(&owner).cloned() else { return };
   110	            binding.fields.insert(capture.clone(), Ty::Bool);
   111	            self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(owner, binding);
   112	        }
   113	    }
   114	
   115	    fn count_key(&self, value: &A) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {
   116	        let (count_arg, captured_name) = match value {
   117	            A::Call { name, args, .. } if name == "count" && args.len() == 1
   118	                && !self.catalog.functions.contains_key(name) => args.first(),
   119	            A::Name { name, .. } => self.binding(name)?.origins.iter().rev().find_map(|(_, origin)| match origin {
   120	                A::Call { name, args, .. } if name == "count" && args.len() == 1
   121	                    && !self.catalog.functions.contains_key(name) => args.first(),
   122	                _ => None,
   123	            }),
   124	            _ => None,
   125	        }.map(|arg| (arg, match value { A::Name { name, .. } => Some(name.as_str()), _ => None }))?;
   126	        let result = self.aggregate_key(&self.aggregate_domain(count_arg)?)?;
   127	        if let Some(name) = captured_name {
   128	            let binding = self.binding(name)?;
   129	            let definition = format!("{}:{}", binding.runtime, binding.revision);
   130	            let capture = binding.fields.keys().find(|path| path.len() == 3
   131	                && path[0] == COUNT_CAPTURE && path[1] == result.0[1]
   132	                && path[2] == definition)?.clone();
   133	            if binding.fields.get(&capture) != Some(&Ty::Bool)
   134	                || !result.1.iter().all(|root| self.binding(root)
   135	                    .is_some_and(|binding| binding.fields.get(&capture) == Some(&Ty::Bool)))
   136	            {
   137	                return None;
   138	            }
   139	        }
   140	        Some(result)
   141	    }
   142	
   143	    fn aggregate_key(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {
   144	        if read.range.is_some() || read.matching.is_some() { return None; }
   145	        let mut roots = BTreeSet::new();
   146	        let keys = read.keys.iter().map(|key| Some(format!("{:?}:{}", key.column,
   147	            self.aggregate_value(&key.value, &mut roots)?))).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?;
   148	        let seeks = read.seeks.iter().map(|seek| Some(format!("{}:{}:{}", seek.column, seek.op,
   149	            self.aggregate_value(&seek.value, &mut roots)?))).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?;
   150	        let filter = match read.filter.as_deref() {
   151	            Some(value) => Some(self.aggregate_value(value, &mut roots)?),
   152	            None => None,
   153	        };
   154	        if roots.is_empty() { roots.insert(pure_calls::CONSTANTS.into()); }
   155	        let identity = format!("{:?}:{:?}:{:?}:{keys:?}:{seeks:?}:{filter:?}", read.table, read.index, read.order_keys);
   156	        Some((vec![NONEMPTY.into(), identity], roots))
   157	    }
   158	
   159	    fn aggregate_value(&self, value: &A, roots: &mut BTreeSet<String>) -> Option<String> {
   160	        match value {
   161	            A::Focal { .. } => Some("focal".into()),
   162	            A::Member { base, name, .. } if matches!(base.as_ref(), A::Focal { .. }) => Some(format!("focal.{name}")),
   163	            // The operation clock is one immutable input. `new` is excluded:
   164	            // evaluating the same source again would mint a different id.
   165	            A::Name { name, .. } if matches!(name.as_str(), "now" | "commit") => Some(format!("clock:{name}")),
   166	            A::Literal { .. } | A::Name { .. } | A::Member { .. } => self.stable_value(value, roots).map(|value| value.key()),
   167	            A::Unary { op, value, .. } => Some(format!("unary:{op}:{}", self.aggregate_value(value, roots)?)),
   168	            A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } => Some(format!("binary:{op}:{}:{}", self.aggregate_value(left, roots)?, self.aggregate_value(right, roots)?)),
   169	            A::Call { name, args, .. } => {
   170	                if let Some(function) = self.catalog.functions.get(name) {
   171	                    if self.pure_functions.get(&function.name) != Some(&true) { return None; }
   172	                    return Some(format!("call:{name}:{:?}", args.iter().map(|arg| self.aggregate_value(arg, roots)).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?));
   173	                }
   174	                if !stable_builtin(name, args.len()) { return None; }
   175	                Some(format!("builtin:{name}:{:?}", args.iter().map(|arg| self.aggregate_value(arg, roots)).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?))
   176	            }
   177	            A::Array { items, .. } => Some(format!("array:{:?}", items.iter().map(|item| self.aggregate_value(item, roots)).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?)),
   178	            _ => None,
   179	        }
   180	    }
   181	}
   182	
   183	/// Source-level builtins whose result is a deterministic function of stable
   184	/// arguments. State-reading operations and allocation never enter this list;
   185	/// malformed calls still receive their ordinary binding error.
   186	fn stable_builtin(name: &str, arity: usize) -> bool {
   187	    match name {
   188	        "count" | "first" | "last" | "lower" | "trim" | "sha256" | "tokens"
   189	        | "month" | "year" | "monthStart" | "yearStart" => arity == 1,
   190	        "at" | "group" | "join" | "min" | "max" => arity == 2,
   191	        "slice" | "share" => arity == 3,
   192	        "sum" => matches!(arity, 1 | 2),
   193	        "day" | "hour" => matches!(arity, 1 | 2),
   194	        "bucket" => matches!(arity, 2 | 3),
   195	        "divide" => matches!(arity, 3 | 4),
   196	        "sort" => arity >= 2,
   197	        _ => false,
   198	    }
   199	}
     1	//! Lowering one Quarry program to the IR: a scope of typed names, one
     2	//! statement list that expressions may hoist temporaries into, reads that
     3	//! choose their index, writes that read their target once, and shapes that
     4	//! become bounded loops. Refusals carry verified repairs or prose guidance.
     5	
     6	use super::catalog::{Catalog, TableInfo};
     7	use crate::ast::{self, Expr as A, Literal, ObjectField, ProgramKind, ReadExpr, ShapeField, Stmt as S, WriteOp};
     8	use crate::diag::{Diagnostic, Fallible, Site, ambiguous, name_rewrite, placement, type_error, unbound_loop, unknown, unsupported};
     9	use crate::print;
    10	use crate::types::{Ty, scalar_to_arg};
    11	use snapback4_core::ir::{self, BinaryOp, Bound, Builtin, Expr, IndexKeyExpr, Input, KeyProvenance, LoopDomain, Merge, MergeLane, Order, Program, Read, Recurse, Scan, SortKey, Stmt, UnaryOp};
    12	use snapback4_core::value::Value;
    13	use std::collections::BTreeMap;
    14

    16	mod job_reads;
    17	mod pure_calls;

    41	/// A bound operation: the IR plus the types the client sees.
    42	#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
    43	pub struct BoundProgram {
    44	    /// Source statement ownership stays beside flat IR, like diagnostic sites.
    45	    pub(crate) statement_ends: Vec<usize>,
    46	    pub(crate) maintenance_scopes: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    47	    pub view_types: BTreeMap<String, Ty>,
    48	    pub(crate) filters: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    49	    /// Whole-domain consumers for diagnostics only; absent from IR/wire.
    50	    pub(crate) constraint_domains: std::collections::BTreeSet<String>,
    51	    pub(crate) sources: BTreeMap<String, (Site, String)>,
    52	    pub(crate) receipt_sources: Vec<(Expr, Site)>,
    53	    pub(crate) receipt_returns: Vec<(String, Site)>,
    54	    pub(crate) implicit_write_results: Vec<Expr>,
    55	    /// Source-table pages before binding filters, for executable horizon repairs only.
    56	    pub(crate) page_sources: BTreeMap<String, ReadExpr>,
    57	    pub(crate) canonical_sources: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    58	    pub(crate) selections: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    59	    /// Scan sites the compiler generated for a timeline read: the only scans whose
    60	    /// filter is exactly the platform's retirement check.
    61	    pub(crate) timeline_scans: std::collections::BTreeSet<String>,
    62	    pub program: Program,
    63	    pub(crate) presence: Option<snapback4_core::schema::Execution>,
    64	    pub module: String,
    65	    pub args: Vec<(String, Ty)>,
    66	    pub result: Ty,
    67	    pub notes: Vec<Diagnostic>,
    68	    pub site: Site,
    69	    /// Job helpers with reads that need a non-uniform meter in a query too; never IR/wire.
    70	    pub job_bound_helpers: std::collections::BTreeSet<String>,
    71	}
    72	
    73	impl BoundProgram {
    74	    pub fn filter_source(&self, site: &str) -> Option<&str> { self.filters.get(site).map(String::as_str) }
    75	    pub fn domain_source(&self, site: &str) -> Option<&str> { self.sources.get(&format!("domain/{site}")).map(|(_, text)| text.as_str()) }
    76	    pub fn loop_source(&self, binding: &str) -> Option<&str> { self.sources.get(&format!("for/{binding}")).map(|(_, text)| text.as_str()) }
    77	    pub fn selections(&self) -> &BTreeMap<String, String> { &self.selections }
    78	    /// Whether the scan at `site` is a timeline read's own expansion, not an authored scan.
    79	    pub fn timeline_scan(&self, site: &str) -> bool { self.timeline_scans.contains(site) }
    80	    /// The actual binder choice, shared by `why` and structural cost reports.
    81	    pub fn selection(&self, site: &str) -> Option<&str> { self.selections.get(site).map(String::as_str) }
    82	}
    83	
    84	#[derive(Clone)]
    85	pub struct Binder<'a> {
    86	    viewer_depth: Option<u32>,
    87	    pure_functions: BTreeMap<String, bool>,
    88	    maintenance_scopes: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    89	    call_types: BTreeMap<Vec<String>, Ty>,
    90	    filters: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    91	    view_types: BTreeMap<String, Ty>,
    92	    constraint_domains: std::collections::BTreeSet<String>,
    93	    sources: BTreeMap<String, (Site, String)>,
    94	    page_sources: BTreeMap<String, ReadExpr>,
    95	    canonical_sources: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    96	    selections: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    97	    presence_sources: Vec<(Expr, Site, String)>,
    98	    receipt_sources: Vec<(Expr, Site)>,
    99	    receipt_returns: Vec<(String, Site)>,
   100	    implicit_write_results: Vec<Expr>,
   101	    catalog: &'a Catalog,
   102	    schema: &'a snapback4_core::schema::Schema,
   103	    name: String,
   104	    kind: ProgramKind,
   105	    caller_kind: ProgramKind,
   106	    /// Diagnostic-only caller path; helpers never change execution authority.
   107	    call_chain: Vec<(String, Site)>,
   108	    job_bound_helpers: std::collections::BTreeSet<String>,
   109	    scopes: Vec<BTreeMap<String, Binding>>,
   110	    focal: Option<(String, Ty)>,
   111	    /// Only direct scanned-row columns get false-on-null ordering in where.
   112	    in_predicate: bool,
   113	    returned_data: bool,
   114	    shape_field: Option<(String, Site)>,
   115	    variants: Vec<(String, bool)>,
   116	    temps: u32,
   117	    sites: u32,
   118	    returns: Vec<(Ty, A)>,
   119	    /// Depth of helper expansion, to bound recursion.
   120	    depth: u32,
   121	    /// Enclosing `for` and `chunk` bodies: where `break` may stand.
   122	    loops: u32,
   123	    /// Binding facts carried by the current loop's transfer edges. Ordinary
   124	    /// fallthrough remains in the lexical scope; these edges meet their own
   125	    /// destinations at the loop header or exit.
   126	    loop_transfers: Vec<LoopTransfers>,
   127	    /// Enclosing caller loops affect placement, but never helper-local jumps.
   128	    caller_loops: u32,
   129	    in_prelude: bool,
   130	    /// The total being bound (`count`, or `sum` and its column), for E_QUARRY_BOUND's view recipe.
   131	    total: Option<(&'static str, Option<String>)>,
   132	    record_ownership: bool,
   133	    /// The feed table and source site of the timeline read being expanded, while
   134	    /// it is: the generated scan carries that read's own site.
   135	    expanding_feed: Option<(String, Site)>,
   136	    timeline_scans: std::collections::BTreeSet<String>,
   137	    notes: Vec<Diagnostic>,
   138	    errors: Vec<Diagnostic>,
   139	    /// Totals over partly readable rows that a readable grant may decide:
   140	    /// refused only if the finished program does not guard the scan with it.
   141	    pending_totals: Vec<(String, Diagnostic)>,
   142	}
   143	
   144	/// Source names are lexical; the IR's flat environment needs a distinct
   145	/// runtime name for each declaration and each helper expansion.
   146	#[derive(Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
   147	struct Binding {
   148	    runtime: String,
   149	    ty: Ty,
   150	    declared: Ty,
   151	    parameter: bool,
   152	    revision: u32,
   153	    fields: BTreeMap<Vec<String>, Ty>,
   154	    /// An invalid binding retains its original cause; its unknown type never escapes compilation.
   155	    error: Option<Diagnostic>,
   156	    /// Original operands for a diagnostic rewrite, never type or runtime provenance.
   157	    origins: Vec<(Ty, A)>,
   158	}
   159	
   160	#[derive(Clone, Default)]
   161	struct LoopTransfers {
   162	    /// Binding state is keyed by runtime identity so a same-named loop item
   163	    /// cannot hide an outer local from the header or exit meet.
   164	    breaks: Vec<BTreeMap<String, Binding>>,
   165	    continues: Vec<BTreeMap<String, Binding>>,
   166	}
   167	
   168	const MAX_EXPANSION_DEPTH: u32 = 8;
   169	const MENTION_MAX: u32 = 64;
   170	
   171	impl<'a> Binder<'a> {
   172	    pub fn bind(catalog: &'a Catalog, schema: &'a snapback4_core::schema::Schema, module: &str, decl: &ast::ProgramDecl, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) -> Option<BoundProgram> {
   173	        Self::bind_with_ownership(catalog, schema, module, decl, diagnostics, true)
   174	    }
   175	
   176	    /// Differential-test control: lowering is identical with the side map disabled.
   177	    #[doc(hidden)]
   178	    pub fn bind_with_ownership(catalog: &'a Catalog, schema: &'a snapback4_core::schema::Schema, module: &str, decl: &ast::ProgramDecl, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>, record_ownership: bool) -> Option<BoundProgram> {
   179	        let mut binder = Binder { viewer_depth: None, maintenance_scopes: BTreeMap::new(), pure_functions: BTreeMap::new(), call_types: BTreeMap::new(), page_sources: BTreeMap::new(), canonical_sources: BTreeMap::new(), constraint_domains: Default::default(), filters: BTreeMap::new(), variants: Vec::new(), view_types: BTreeMap::new(), sources: BTreeMap::new(), selections: BTreeMap::new(), presence_sources: Vec::new(), receipt_sources: Vec::new(), receipt_returns: Vec::new(), implicit_write_results: Vec::new(), catalog, schema, name: decl.name.clone(), kind: decl.kind, caller_kind: decl.kind, call_chain: Vec::new(), job_bound_helpers: Default::default(), scopes: vec![BTreeMap::new()], focal: None, in_predicate: false, returned_data: false, shape_field: None, temps: 0, sites: 0, returns: Vec::new(), depth: 0, loops: 0, loop_transfers: Vec::new(), caller_loops: 0, in_prelude: false, total: None, record_ownership, expanding_feed: None, timeline_scans: Default::default(), notes: Vec::new(), errors: Vec::new(), pending_totals: Vec::new() };
   180	        binder.call_chain.push((super::operation_name(module, &decl.name), decl.site.clone()));
   181	        binder.declare(pure_calls::CONSTANTS, Ty::Null);
   182	        let mut args = BTreeMap::new();
   183	        let mut arg_types = Vec::new();
   184	        for arg in &decl.args {
   185	            if args.contains_key(&arg.name) {
   186	                diagnostics.push(unknown(&arg.site, format!("duplicate argument {}", arg.name), "rename one argument"));
   187	                return None;
   188	            }
   189	            if let ast::TypeKind::Ref { table } = &arg.type_.kind
   190	                && !catalog.tables.contains_key(table)
   191	            {
   192	                diagnostics.push(type_error(&arg.site, format!("argument {} references unknown table {table}", arg.name), name_rewrite(table, catalog.tables.keys().map(String::as_str), None)));
   193	                return None;
   194	            }
   195	            let ty = Ty::from_scalar(&arg.type_);
   196	            binder.scopes[0].insert(arg.name.clone(), Binding { runtime: arg.name.clone(), ty: ty.clone(), declared: ty.clone(), parameter: true, revision: 0, fields: BTreeMap::new(), error: None, origins: Vec::new() });
   197	            args.insert(arg.name.clone(), scalar_to_arg(&arg.type_));
   198	            arg_types.push((arg.name.clone(), ty));
   199	        }
   200	        if decl.native {
   201	            // The body is the app's Rust (LLP 3000.005): nothing to bind, the
   202	            // module is checked against the declaration by `check`.
   203	            let full_name = super::operation_name(module, &decl.name);
   204	            let (kind, result) = match decl.kind {
   205	                ProgramKind::Query => (ir::ProgramKind::Query, Ty::Json),
   206	                _ => (ir::ProgramKind::Job, Ty::Null),
   207	            };

   142	
   143	    fn aggregate_key(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {
   144	        if read.range.is_some() || read.matching.is_some() { return None; }
   145	        let mut roots = BTreeSet::new();
   146	        let keys = read.keys.iter().map(|key| Some(format!("{:?}:{}", key.column,
   147	            self.aggregate_value(&key.value, &mut roots)?))).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?;
   148	        let seeks = read.seeks.iter().map(|seek| Some(format!("{}:{}:{}", seek.column, seek.op,
   149	            self.aggregate_value(&seek.value, &mut roots)?))).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?;
   150	        let filter = match read.filter.as_deref() {
   151	            Some(value) => Some(self.aggregate_value(value, &mut roots)?),
   152	            None => None,
   153	        };
   154	        if roots.is_empty() { roots.insert(pure_calls::CONSTANTS.into()); }
   155	        let identity = format!("{:?}:{:?}:{:?}:{keys:?}:{seeks:?}:{filter:?}", read.table, read.index, read.order_keys);
   156	        Some((vec![NONEMPTY.into(), identity], roots))
   157	    }
   158	
   159	    fn aggregate_value(&self, value: &A, roots: &mut BTreeSet<String>) -> Option<String> {
   160	        match value {
   161	            A::Focal { .. } => Some("focal".into()),
   162	            A::Member { base, name, .. } if matches!(base.as_ref(), A::Focal { .. }) => Some(format!("focal.{name}")),
   163	            // The operation clock is one immutable input. `new` is excluded:
   164	            // evaluating the same source again would mint a different id.
   165	            A::Name { name, .. } if matches!(name.as_str(), "now" | "commit") => Some(format!("clock:{name}")),
   166	            A::Literal { .. } | A::Name { .. } | A::Member { .. } => self.stable_value(value, roots).map(|value| value.key()),
   167	            A::Unary { op, value, .. } => Some(format!("unary:{op}:{}", self.aggregate_value(value, roots)?)),
   168	            A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } => Some(format!("binary:{op}:{}:{}", self.aggregate_value(left, roots)?, self.aggregate_value(right, roots)?)),
   169	            A::Call { name, args, .. } => {
   170	                if let Some(function) = self.catalog.functions.get(name) {
   171	                    if self.pure_functions.get(&function.name) != Some(&true) { return None; }
   172	                    return Some(format!("call:{name}:{:?}", args.iter().map(|arg| self.aggregate_value(arg, roots)).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?));
   173	                }
   174	                if !stable_builtin(name, args.len()) { return None; }
   175	                Some(format!("builtin:{name}:{:?}", args.iter().map(|arg| self.aggregate_value(arg, roots)).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?))
   176	            }
   177	            A::Array { items, .. } => Some(format!("array:{:?}", items.iter().map(|item| self.aggregate_value(item, roots)).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?)),
   178	            _ => None,
   179	        }
   180	    }
   181	}
   182	
   183	/// Source-level builtins whose result is a deterministic function of stable
   184	/// arguments. State-reading operations and allocation never enter this list;
   185	/// malformed calls still receive their ordinary binding error.
   186	fn stable_builtin(name: &str, arity: usize) -> bool {
   187	    match name {
   188	        "count" | "first" | "last" | "lower" | "trim" | "sha256" | "tokens"
   189	        | "month" | "year" | "monthStart" | "yearStart" => arity == 1,
   190	        "at" | "group" | "join" | "min" | "max" => arity == 2,
   191	        "slice" | "share" => arity == 3,
   192	        "sum" => matches!(arity, 1 | 2),
   193	        "day" | "hour" => matches!(arity, 1 | 2),
   194	        "bucket" => matches!(arity, 2 | 3),
   195	        "divide" => matches!(arity, 3 | 4),
   196	        "sort" => arity >= 2,
   197	        _ => false,
   198	    }
   199	}

    14	}
    15	
    16	#[test]
    17	fn global_count_is_zero_then_tracks_inserts_and_deletes() {
    18	    let source = r#"
    19	table entries:
    20	  public
    21	  insert <- allow
    22	  delete <- allow
    23	  online only
    24	table moderators:
    25	  principal: principal
    26	  unique principal
    27	  read <- .principal = viewer
    28	  online only
    29	view total: count entries
    30	  read <- allow
    31	  online only
    32	view privateTotal: count entries
    33	  read <- exists moderators[viewer]
    34	  online only
    35	mutation add(id: id):
    36	  return insert entries { id }
    37	mutation remove(id: entries):
    38	  delete entries[id]
    39	  return null
    40	query totalEntries():
    41	  return total
    42	query privateEntries():
    43	  return privateTotal
    44	"#;
    45	    let bound = checked(source);
    46	    let generated = snapback4_lang::emit::typescript(&bound);
    47	    let native = snapback4_lang::emit::rust::rust(&bound);
    48	    assert!(!generated.contains("$global") && !native.contains("$global"),
    49	        "the physical discriminator leaked into generated application types");
    50	    for expression in ["total first 1 by .id", "total[true]"] {
    51	        let bound = compile(&[("bad.q".into(), format!("{source}\nquery bad(c: cursor ?):\n  return {expression}\n"))]);
    52	        assert!(bound.diagnostics.iter().any(|diagnostic| diagnostic.code == "E_QUARRY_TYPE"
    53	            && diagnostic.message.contains("global maintained scalar")), "{expression}: {:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
    54	    }
    55	    for domain in ["total", "total first 1 by .id"] {
    56	        let bound = compile(&[("bad.q".into(), format!("{source}\nquery badLoop():\n  for row in {domain}:\n    return row\n  return null\n"))]);
    57	        assert!(bound.diagnostics.iter().any(|diagnostic| diagnostic.code == "E_QUARRY_TYPE"
    58	            && diagnostic.message.contains("global maintained scalar")), "{domain}: {:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
    59	    }
    60	    for expression in ["count(total)", "exists total"] {
    61	        let bound = compile(&[("bad.q".into(), format!("{source}\nquery badAggregate():\n  return {expression}\n"))]);
    62	        assert!(bound.diagnostics.iter().any(|diagnostic| diagnostic.code == "E_QUARRY_TYPE"),
    63	            "{expression}: {:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
    64	    }
    65	    for expression in ["min((total where true by byId), .value + 0)", "max((total where true by byId), .value + 0)"] {
    66	        let bound = compile(&[("bad.q".into(), format!("{source}\nquery badExtremum():\n  return {expression}\n"))]);
    67	        assert!(bound.diagnostics.iter().any(|diagnostic| diagnostic.code == "E_QUARRY_TYPE"
    68	            && diagnostic.message.contains("global maintained scalar")), "{expression}: {:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
    69	    }
    70	    let mut engine = engine(source);
    71	    let query = |engine: &mut Engine| engine.query("totalEntries", Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::new())).unwrap().data;
    72	    let private = |engine: &mut Engine, viewer| engine.query("privateEntries", Context::new(viewer, BTreeMap::new())).unwrap().data;
    73	    assert_eq!(query(&mut engine), Value::Int(0));
    74	    assert_eq!(private(&mut engine, "alice"), Value::Null, "an unauthorized empty global stays hidden");
    75	    engine.seed([("moderators".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([
    76	        ("id", Value::Id("mod".into())), ("principal", Value::Id("alice".into())),
    77	    ]))]).unwrap();
    78	    assert_eq!(private(&mut engine, "alice"), Value::Int(0), "authorized absence is the count identity");
    79	    assert_eq!(private(&mut engine, "bob"), Value::Null, "a hidden absence is not zero");
    80	    for id in ["a", "b"] {
    81	        let outcome = engine.mutate("add", None, Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::from([("id".into(), Value::Id(id.into()))]))).unwrap();
    82	        assert!(matches!(outcome.state, WriteState::Sent { .. }), "{outcome:?}");
    83	    }
    84	    assert_eq!(query(&mut engine), Value::Int(2));
    85	    assert_eq!(private(&mut engine, "alice"), Value::Int(2));
    86	    assert_eq!(private(&mut engine, "bob"), Value::Null, "a hidden stored scalar remains null");
    87	    engine.mutate("remove", None, Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::from([("id".into(), Value::Id("a".into()))]))).unwrap();
    88	    assert_eq!(query(&mut engine), Value::Int(1));
    89	    engine.mutate("remove", None, Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::from([("id".into(), Value::Id("b".into()))]))).unwrap();
    90	    assert_eq!(query(&mut engine), Value::Int(0));
    91	    assert!(engine.store().rows("total").unwrap().is_empty(), "zero deletes the physical aggregate row");
    92	    assert!(engine.store().rows("privateTotal").unwrap().is_empty(), "private zero also has no physical row");
    93	}

   108	fn matching_count_guard_narrows_only_the_same_required_projection_until_a_write() {
   109	    let schema = r#"
   110	table entries:
   111	  owner: principal
   112	  score: int
   113	  optionalScore: int ?
   114	  by byOwner: owner, id

   116	  insert <- allow
   117	  delete <- allow
   118	  online only
   119	"#;
   120	    let sound = checked(&format!(r#"{schema}
   121	query stats(owner: principal):

   123	  require n > 0 else EMPTY
   124	  return {{ low: min(entries[owner] by byOwner, .score), high: max(entries[owner] by byOwner, .score), total: sum(entries[owner] by byOwner, .score) }}
   125	query filtered(owner: principal):
   126	  require count(entries where .owner = owner) > 0 else EMPTY
   127	  return min(entries where .owner = owner, .score) + 1
   128	mutation freshAfterWrite(owner: principal, victim: entries):
   129	  delete entries[victim]
   130	  n = count(entries[owner] by byOwner)
   131	  require n > 0 else EMPTY
   132	  return min(entries[owner] by byOwner, .score) + 1

   136	    assert_eq!(sound.programs.iter().find(|program| program.program.name == "filtered").unwrap().result, Ty::Int);
   137	
   138	    for (name, body) in [
   139	        ("different", "n = count(entries[owner] by byOwner)\n  require n > 0 else EMPTY\n  return min(entries['other'] by byOwner, .score) + 1"),
   140	        ("optional", "n = count(entries[owner] by byOwner)\n  require n > 0 else EMPTY\n  return min(entries[owner] by byOwner, .optionalScore) + 1"),
   141	        ("written", "n = count(entries[owner] by byOwner)\n  require n > 0 else EMPTY\n  delete entries[victim]\n  return min(entries[owner] by byOwner, .score) + 1"),
   142	        ("stale_binding", "n = count(entries[owner] by byOwner)\n  delete entries[victim]\n  require n > 0 else EMPTY\n  return min(entries[owner] by byOwner, .score) + 1"),
   143	        ("rebound", "n = count(entries[owner] by byOwner)\n  n = 1\n  require n > 0 else EMPTY\n  return min(entries[owner] by byOwner, .score) + 1"),
   144	        ("joined", "n = count(entries[owner] by byOwner)\n  if flag:\n    n = 1\n  require n > 0 else EMPTY\n  return min(entries[owner] by byOwner, .score) + 1"),
   145	        ("root_rebound", "who = owner\n  n = count(entries[who] by byOwner)\n  who = next\n  require n > 0 else EMPTY\n  return min(entries[who] by byOwner, .score) + 1"),
   146	        ("root_recaptured", "who = owner\n  n = count(entries[who] by byOwner)\n  who = next\n  m = count(entries[who] by byOwner)\n  require n > 0 else EMPTY\n  return min(entries[who] by byOwner, .score) + 1"),

   154	        let kind = if matches!(name, "written" | "stale_binding") { "mutation" } else { "query" };
   155	        let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), format!("{schema}{kind} {name}({args}):\n  {body}\n"))]);
   156	        assert!(bound.diagnostics.iter().any(|d| d.code == "E_QUARRY_TYPE" && d.message.contains("int ?")), "{name}: {:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
   157	    }
   158	
   159	    let helper = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), format!(r#"{schema}
   160	fn remove(victim: entries):

codex
The combined commit hash matches the brief. The code now ties count markers to the guarded binding’s runtime identity and revision, and calls the global-source admission check from `scan_at`. I’m tracing callers and invalidation paths to check whether those fixes cover the full contract.

     /// After `require x != null` or inside `if x != null:`, `x` is present.
     pub(super) fn narrow(&mut self, condition: &A, then_branch: bool) {
+        // Aggregate facts may describe filtered domains, so they have a
+        // slightly wider write-free admission check than point facts. Record
+        // them once for the whole, already-evaluated condition: recursively
+        // recording a safe left operand would be stale when a later operand
+        // calls a writeful helper.
+        if self.aggregate_guard_safe(condition) {
+            self.record_aggregate_nonempty(condition, then_branch);
+        }
         self.narrow_inner(condition, then_branch, self.point_guard_safe(condition));
     }

diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/program/control.rs b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/program/control.rs
index 1a7593a08c..d139676dac 100644
--- a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/program/control.rs
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/program/control.rs
@@ -527,6 +527,16 @@ impl Binder<'_> {
     }
 
     pub(super) fn loop_domain(&mut self, domain: &A, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(LoopDomain, Ty)> {
+        let global = match domain {
+            A::Name { name, site } if self.binding(name).is_none() => Some((name, site)),
+            A::Read(read) => Some((&read.table, &read.site)),
+            _ => None,
+        }.filter(|(name, _)| self.catalog.tables.get(*name).and_then(|table| table.maintained.as_ref())
+            .is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty()));
+        if let Some((name, site)) = global {
+            return Err(type_error(site, format!("{name} is one global maintained scalar, not a loop domain"),
+                format!("use `{name}` as a scalar value rather than iterating it")).guide("maintains"));
+        }
         if let A::Read(read) = domain && let Some(shaped) = self.page_shape(read) { return self.loop_domain(&shaped, out); }
         if let A::Name { name, site } = domain && self.binding(name).is_none() && self.catalog.tables.contains_key(name) {
             let read = ReadExpr { cursor_prefix: None, order_keys: Vec::new(), computed: Default::default(), table: name.clone(), keys: Vec::new(), index: Some("byId".into()), range: None, matching: None, filter: None, seeks: Vec::new(), site: site.clone() };

     /// names keep their lexical meaning and ordinary pages keep their bounds.

         match value {
-            A::Read(read) if read.range.is_none() && read.matching.is_none() => Some(read.clone()),
-            A::Name { name, site } if self.binding(name).is_none() && self.catalog.tables.contains_key(name) =>
+            A::Read(read) if read.range.is_none() && read.matching.is_none()
+                && !self.catalog.tables.get(&read.table).and_then(|table| table.maintained.as_ref()).is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty()) => Some(read.clone()),
+            A::Name { name, site } if self.binding(name).is_none() && self.catalog.tables.contains_key(name)
+                && !self.catalog.tables.get(name).and_then(|table| table.maintained.as_ref()).is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty()) =>
                 Some(ReadExpr { cursor_prefix: None, order_keys: Vec::new(), computed: Default::default(), table: name.clone(), keys: Vec::new(), index: Some("byId".into()), range: None, matching: None, filter: None, seeks: Vec::new(), site: site.clone() }),
             _ => None,
         }

index ffa1d039dc..77b843e43b 100644

@@ -183,6 +183,12 @@ impl Binder<'_> {
 
     // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.22 — ruling 25: program existence over a prefix is a top-1 scan below page ownership; rule probes retain their exact-key path.
     pub(super) fn exists_read(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
+        if self.catalog.tables.get(&read.table).and_then(|table| table.maintained.as_ref())
+            .is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty())
+        {
+            return Err(type_error(&read.site, format!("{} is one global maintained scalar, not an existence probe", read.table),
+                format!("compare the bare scalar instead: `{} > 0`", read.table)).guide("maintains"));
+        }
         self.exists_probe(read, out).map_err(|error| super::super::bracket_ranges::refusal(read, false, error))
     }

   468	            if !info.total_cursor(&index) {
   469	                let columns = index.columns.iter().map(|column| index.computed.get(column)
   470	                    .map(print::expression).unwrap_or_else(|| column.clone())).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
   471	                if index.unique {
   472	                    // Do not add id to the unique constraint: that would
   473	                    // weaken it. Plan a separate pageable index from the read.
   474	                    let mut planned = read.clone();
   475	                    let rewrite = if index.computed.is_empty() {
   476	                        planned.index = None;
   477	                        for (key, column) in planned.keys.iter_mut().zip(&index.columns) { key.column.get_or_insert_with(|| column.clone()); }
   478	                        planned.order_keys = index.columns.iter().skip(prefix.len()).cloned().collect();
   479	                        print::read_expr(&planned)
   480	                    } else {
   481	                        let mut name = format!("{}Page", index.name);
   482	                        while info.indexes.iter().any(|index| index.name == name) { name.push('_'); }
   483	                        planned.index = Some(name.clone());
   484	                        format!("add `by {name}: {columns}, id` to {}; read `{}`", read.table, print::read_expr(&planned))
   485	                    };
   486	                    return Err(Diagnostic::new(&range.site, "E_CURSOR_INDEX", format!("index {}.{} cannot carry a cursor: its nullable unique key allows tied null rows without an id tiebreak", read.table, index.name), rewrite).guide("indexes"));
   487	                }
   488	                return Err(Diagnostic::new(&range.site, "E_CURSOR_INDEX", format!("index {}.{} cannot carry a cursor: it has no declared id or total unique key", read.table, index.name), format!("by {}: {columns}, id", index.name)).guide("indexes").mechanical());
   489	            }
   490	            after = Some(Box::new(e));
   491	        }
   492	        let mut prefix: Vec<IndexKeyExpr> = prefix.into_iter().map(IndexKeyExpr::Scalar).collect();
   493	        if let Some(matching) = &read.matching {
   494	            // The search text follows the keys as the word term; the index
   495	            // was chosen for its words component in exactly that position.
   496	            let (e, t) = self.expr(matching, out)?;
   497	            if !matches!(t, Ty::Text(_)) {
   498	                let rewrite = if matches!(t, Ty::Optional(ref inner) if matches!(**inner, Ty::Text(_))) { format!("{} ?? ''", print::expression(matching)) } else { "matching q  -- with q: text <=100".to_owned() };
   499	                return Err(type_error(matching.site(), format!("matching takes present text, got {}", t.name()), rewrite));
   500	            }
   501	            prefix.push(IndexKeyExpr::WordPrefix(e));
   502	        }
   503	        let scan_site = self.site();
   504	        self.page_sources.insert(scan_site.clone(), page_source);
   505	        let pins = read.keys.iter().enumerate().map(|(position, key)| {
   506	            let column = key.column.as_ref().unwrap_or(&index.columns[position]);
   507	            format!(".{column} = {}", self.catalog.source.operand(&key.value))
   508	        }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" and ");
   509	        let domain = if pins.is_empty() { read.table.clone() } else { format!("{} where {pins}", read.table) };
   510	        self.sources.insert(format!("domain/{scan_site}"), (read.site.clone(), domain));
   511	        if filter.is_some() && let Some(text) = filter_text { self.filters.insert(scan_site.clone(), text); }
   512	        // Only the generated scan itself, never an authored scan bound inside its keys, cursor or helpers.
   513	        if self.expanding_feed.as_ref().is_some_and(|(feed, site)| *feed == read.table && *site == read.site) { self.timeline_scans.insert(scan_site.clone()); }
   514	        self.record_selection(&scan_site, read, &index, if position == prefix::Position::Exists { "exists" } else { "page" }, &selection);
   515	        // Authored spelling serves diagnostics; inferred-index residency sites
   516	        // keep canonical bytes. Explicit-index search alternatives retain their
   517	        // authored origin even when presence planning synthesizes a browse index.
   518	        if !read.order_keys.is_empty() && let Some(text) = self.catalog.canonical_read_origins.get(&read.site.to_string()) {
   519	            self.canonical_sources.insert(scan_site.clone(), text.clone());
   520	        }
   521	        let spelling = if !read.order_keys.is_empty() { self.catalog.read_origins.get(&read.site.to_string()).cloned() } else { None };
   522	        self.sources.insert(scan_site.clone(), (read.site.clone(), spelling.unwrap_or_else(|| print::read_expr(&ReadExpr { index: None, range: None, matching: None, filter: None, seeks: Vec::new(), ..read.clone() }))));
   523	        let mut scan = Scan { cursor_pins: read.cursor_prefix.map_or(0, |old| (read.keys.len() - old) as u32),
   524	            variant: self.variant(),
   525	            site: scan_site,
   526	            table: read.table.clone(),
   527	            index: index.name,
   528	            prefix,
   529	            order: if range.order == ast::Order::Asc { Order::Asc } else { Order::Desc },
   530	            take: bound,
   531	            existence: position == prefix::Position::Exists,
   532	            after,
   533	            before: None,
   534	            range: seek,
   535	            filter,
   536	            opaque_cursor: range.cursor.is_some(),
   537	            merge_site: None,
   538	            provenance: KeyProvenance::Shape,
   539	        };
   540	        if position == prefix::Position::Aggregate && snapback4_core::engine::rules::scan_visibility(self.schema, &scan).uniform {
   541	            scan.take = Bound::Meter;
   542	        }
   543	        if matches!(position, prefix::Position::Exists | prefix::Position::Aggregate) {
   544	            self.constraint_domains.insert(scan.site.clone());
   545	        }
     1	//! Bind a planned view's source lanes independently, preserving source rules.
     2	use super::*;
     3	
     4	impl Binder<'_> {
     5	    pub(super) fn planned_view(&mut self, merge: &ast::MergeExpr, out: &mut setup::Body, position: prefix::Position) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
     6	        let site = self.site();
     7	        let cursor = merge.cursor.as_ref().map(|value| self.expr(value, out)).transpose()?;
     8	        if let Some((_, ty)) = &cursor && ty.required() != Ty::Cursor {
     9	            return Err(type_error(&merge.site, "after expects a cursor", "declare c: cursor ? and pass after c"));
    10	        }
    11	        let mut lanes = Vec::new();
    12	        let mut result: Option<BTreeMap<String, Ty>> = None;
    13	        let mut first_site = None;
    14	        let mut field_sources: BTreeMap<String, Vec<(Ty, A)>> = BTreeMap::new();
    15	        // Queries retain the view's shared cursor identity. A non-query with
    16	        // one source is an ordinary admitted scan followed by a row shape;
    17	        // representing it as a merge would reject safe capped mutations.
    18	        let plain = self.caller_kind != ProgramKind::Query && merge.lanes.len() == 1
    19	            && merge.lanes[0].project.is_some() && merge.lanes[0].loop_.is_none();
    20	        for lane in &merge.lanes {
    21	            let mut read = *lane.planned.clone().expect("planned lane");
    22	            // @ref LLP 3000.013#2.2 — planning cannot erase point-key admission.
    23	            // Validate the authored predicate in the caller's lexical scope,
    24	            // before binding the proposed lane and its generated selectors.
    25	            if let Some(predicate) = &lane.admission {
    26	                self.clone().row_predicate(&read.table, predicate)?;
    27	            }
    28	            if let Some(range) = &mut read.range { range.cursor = None; }
    29	            self.push_scope();
    30	            let (source, binding) = if let Some((name, domain)) = &lane.loop_ {
    31	                let (source, ty) = self.lane_domain(domain, out)?;
    32	                (Some(source), self.declare(name, ty))
    33	            } else { (None, String::new()) };
    34	            if let Some((_, domain)) = &lane.loop_ {
    35	                self.sources.insert(format!("for/{binding}"), (domain.site().clone(), self.catalog.source.expression(domain)));
    36	            }
    37	            let distinct = lane.distinct.as_ref().map(|value| self.expr(value, out).map(|(value, _)| value)).transpose()?;
    38	            let mut setup = setup::Body::default();
    39	            // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12 — as with authored merges, a lane-dependent
    40	            // residual needs its own selector in eager setup. Deduplication
    41	            // above still reads the original discovery binding.
    42	            if let Some((name, _)) = &lane.loop_
    43	                && read.filter.as_ref().is_some_and(|filter| lanes::filter_uses_lane(filter, name)) {
    44	                let selector = self.temp("selector");
    45	                self.scopes.last_mut().expect("lane scope").get_mut(name).expect("lane binding").runtime = selector.clone();
    46	                setup.push(Stmt::Let { name: selector, value: ir::var(&binding) });
    47	            }
    48	            let (mut scan, _) = self.scan_at(&read, &mut setup, position)?;
    49	            scan.after = cursor.as_ref().map(|(value, _)| Box::new(value.clone()));
    50	            scan.opaque_cursor = cursor.is_some();
    51	            scan.merge_site = Some(site.clone());
    52	            if lane.project.is_none() {
    53	                // Admission was checked against the original domain cap above.
    54	                scan.take = Bound::Literal(merge.take);
    55	                self.pop_scope();
    56	                self.push_value_lane(&mut lanes, lane, MergeLane { guard: None, distinct, branch: lane.branch.clone(), setup: setup.code, scan, source, binding, project: None }, position)?;
    57	                continue;
    58	            }
    59	            scan.after = None;
    60	            let mut conjuncts = Vec::new();
    61	            if let Some(filter) = &read.filter { super::super::intent::split(*filter.clone(), &mut conjuncts); }
    62	            let guard = super::super::intent::conjunction(conjuncts.into_iter().filter(super::super::intent::independent).collect());
    63	            let guard = guard.map(|guard| self.expr(&guard, out).map(|(value, _)| value)).transpose()?;
    64	            let mut projection_setup = setup::Body::default();
    65	            let project_binding = if plain { self.temp("view_row") } else { "$project".into() };
    66	            let (project, ty) = self.shape_object(&project_binding, Ty::Row(read.table.clone()), lane.project.as_ref().expect("view projection"), &lane.site, &mut projection_setup)?;
    67	            if plain {
    68	                scan.merge_site = None;
    69	                scan.after = cursor.as_ref().map(|(value, _)| Box::new(value.clone()));
    70	                let max = scan.take.maximum() as u32;
    71	                let target = self.temp("view_rows");
    72	                out.extend(setup);
    73	                out.push(Stmt::Let { name: target.clone(), value: Expr::Array(Vec::new()) });
    74	                projection_setup.push(Stmt::Push { target: target.clone(), value: project });
    75	                out.push_for(project_binding, LoopDomain::Page(scan), projection_setup);
    76	                self.pop_scope();
    77	                return Ok((ir::var(&target), Ty::Collection(Box::new(ty), max)));
    78	            }
    79	            let fields = match ty { Ty::RowShape(fields) | Ty::RowObject(fields) | Ty::Object(fields) => fields, _ => unreachable!("shape object") };
    80	            if let Some(prior) = &mut result {
    81	                if prior.keys().ne(fields.keys()) {
    82	                    return Err(type_error(&lane.site, format!("view {} branch fields differ from branch at {}", merge.table, first_site.as_ref().unwrap()), "project the same named fields in every view branch").guide("reads"));
    83	                }
    84	                for (name, ty) in fields {
    85	                    let old = &prior[&name];
    86	                    let current = projection_source(lane, &name);
    87	                    let origins = field_sources.get_mut(&name).unwrap();
    88	                    origins.push((ty.clone(), current));
    89	                    old.join(&ty).map_err(|error| self.join_error_many(error.at(&name), origins))?;
    90	                    let joined = view_join(old, &ty).ok_or_else(|| type_error(&lane.site,
    91	                        format!("view {} field {name}: {} conflicts with {} at {}", merge.table, ty.name(), old.name(), first_site.as_ref().unwrap()),
    92	                        format!("project compatible values for {name} in every branch")).guide("reads"))?;
    93	                    prior.insert(name, joined);
    94	                }
    95	            } else {
    96	                first_site = Some(lane.site.clone());
    97	                field_sources = fields.keys().map(|name| (name.clone(), vec![(fields[name].clone(), projection_source(lane, name))])).collect();
    98	                result = Some(fields);
    99	            }
   100	            let project = if projection_setup.is_empty() { project } else { Expr::Block { body: projection_setup.code, value: Box::new(project) } };
   101	            self.pop_scope();
   102	            self.push_value_lane(&mut lanes, lane, MergeLane { guard, distinct, branch: lane.branch.clone(), setup: setup.code, scan, source, binding, project: Some(project) }, position)?;
   103	        }
   104	        if result.is_none() && !lanes.is_empty() {
   105	            let planned = Merge { variant: self.variant(), position: None, view: None, order_keys: Vec::new(), site, lanes };
   106	            // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#3 — the binding check: the runtime's own analysis must agree that every
   107	            // lane is decided (a grant lane by a grant covering the rule) and that the lanes share one
   108	            // position. Otherwise a page with a grant branch reads as written, the grant branch alone.
   109	            if planned.branches() && !snapback4_core::engine::rules::branch_merge_lazy(self.schema, &planned)
   110	                && let Some(read) = merge.lanes.iter().find(|lane| matches!(lane.branch.as_deref(), Some(ir::GRANT_LANE | ir::SYSTEM_LANE))).and_then(|lane| lane.planned.as_deref()) {
   111	                return self.read(read, out);
   112	            }
   113	            return Ok((Expr::Merge(planned), Ty::Page(merge.table.clone(), merge.take)));
   114	        }
   115	        let fields = match self.catalog.view_types.get(&merge.table) {
   116	            Some(Ty::RowShape(fields)) => fields.clone(), _ => result.unwrap_or_default(),
   117	        };
   118	        let mut names = merge.view_order.clone();
   119	        // Keep the projected order aligned with each branch's source scan,
   120	        // including an explicitly ordered id before another field.
   121	        if !names.iter().any(|name| name == "id") { names.push("id".into()); }
   122	        let mut suffix = names.into_iter().map(|name| {
   123	            let kind = fields.get(&name).and_then(cursor_kind).ok_or_else(|| type_error(&merge.site,
   124	                format!("view order .{name} has no scalar column type"), "order by a field projecting a scalar source column in every branch"))?;
   125	            Ok(snapback4_core::cursor::CursorColumn { name, kind })
   126	        }).collect::<Fallible<Vec<_>>>()?;
   127	        suffix.push(snapback4_core::cursor::CursorColumn { name: "$branch".into(), kind: snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Text { max: 1024 } });
   128	        let position = ir::ViewPosition { take: merge.take, order: if merge.order == ast::Order::Asc { Order::Asc } else { Order::Desc }, after: cursor.map(|(value, _)| Box::new(value)), suffix };
   129	        self.view_types.insert(merge.table.clone(), Ty::RowShape(fields.clone()));
   130	        Ok((Expr::Merge(Merge { variant: self.variant(), position: Some(position), view: Some(merge.table.clone()), order_keys: merge.view_order.clone(), site, lanes }), Ty::Collection(Box::new(Ty::RowShape(fields)), merge.take)))
   131	    }
   132	
   133	    // @ref LLP 3000.013#2.1 — nullable membership is an ordinary filtered lane,
   134	    // below source authorization; no null value is used as an index prefix.
   135	    fn push_value_lane(&mut self, lanes: &mut Vec<MergeLane>, ast: &ast::MergeLane, mut lane: MergeLane, position: prefix::Position) -> Fallible<()> {
   136	        if let Some((_, read)) = &ast.nulls
   137	            && !matches!(&lane.source, Some(ir::LaneSource::Range { .. })) {
   138	            let Some(ir::LaneSource::List { value, max }) = lane.source.take() else {
   139	                return Err(type_error(&ast.site, "nullable in needs a bounded list", "use a bounded list argument or expression after in"));
   140	            };
   141	            let values = self.temp("lane_values");
   142	            let nulls = self.temp("null_lane");
   143	            let item = self.temp("lane_value");
   144	            // Evaluate the list once, inside lane discovery so a cut/cap keeps
   145	            // its existing incompleteness latch. Record at most one null lane.
   146	            lane.source = Some(ir::LaneSource::List { max, value: Expr::Block { body: vec![
   147	                Stmt::Let { name: values.clone(), value },
   148	                Stmt::Let { name: nulls.clone(), value: Expr::Array(Vec::new()) },
   149	                Stmt::For { binding: item.clone(), domain: LoopDomain::ArgList { name: values.clone(), max }, body: vec![
   150	                    Stmt::If { condition: Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Eq, left: Box::new(ir::var(&item)), right: Box::new(ir::lit(Value::Null)) },
   151	                        then_body: vec![Stmt::Push { target: nulls.clone(), value: ir::lit(Value::Bool(true)) }, Stmt::Break], else_body: Vec::new() },
   152	                ] },
   153	            ], value: Box::new(ir::var(&values)) } });
   154	            let mut setup = setup::Body::default();
   155	            let mut read = *read.clone();
   156	            if let Some(range) = &mut read.range { range.cursor = None; }
   157	            let (mut scan, _) = self.scan_at(&read, &mut setup, position)?;
   158	            scan.after = lane.scan.after.clone();
   159	            scan.opaque_cursor = lane.scan.opaque_cursor;
   160	            scan.merge_site = lane.scan.merge_site.clone();
   161	            scan.take = lane.scan.take.clone();
   162	            let null_lane = MergeLane { guard: lane.guard.clone(), distinct: Some(ir::lit(Value::Bool(true))),
   163	                branch: lane.branch.clone(), project: lane.project.clone(), setup: setup.code, scan,
   164	                source: Some(ir::LaneSource::List { value: ir::var(&nulls), max: 1 }), binding: item };
   165	            lanes.push(lane);
   166	            lanes.push(null_lane);
   167	        } else { lanes.push(lane); }
   168	        Ok(())
   169	    }
   170	}
   171	
   172	fn view_join(a: &Ty, b: &Ty) -> Option<Ty> {
   173	    let optional = a.is_optional() || b.is_optional();
   174	    let a = a.required();
   175	    let b = b.required();
   176	    let joined = if matches!(a, Ty::Id | Ty::Ref(_)) && matches!(b, Ty::Id | Ty::Ref(_)) { Ty::Id }
   177	        else if a == Ty::Null { b }
   178	        else if b == Ty::Null { a }
   179	        else if a.comparable(&b) { a.join(&b).ok()? }
   180	        else { return None; };
   181	    Some(if optional { joined.optional() } else { joined })
   182	}
   183	
   184	fn cursor_kind(ty: &Ty) -> Option<snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType> {
   185	    use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType as C;
   186	    Some(match ty {
   187	        Ty::Id | Ty::Ref(_) => C::Id, Ty::Principal => C::Principal,
   188	        Ty::Int => C::Int, Ty::Bool => C::Bool, Ty::Time => C::Time,
   189	        Ty::Text(max) => C::Text { max: *max }, Ty::Bytes => C::Bytes { max: u32::MAX },
   190	        Ty::Enum(variants) => C::Enum { variants: variants.clone() },
   191	        Ty::Decimal(scale) => C::Decimal { scale: *scale },
   192	        Ty::Money(currency, scale) => C::Money { currency: currency.clone(), scale: *scale },
   193	        Ty::Optional(inner) => C::Optional(Box::new(cursor_kind(inner)?)),
   194	        _ => return None,
   195	    })
   196	}
   197	
   198	fn projection_source(lane: &ast::MergeLane, name: &str) -> A {
   199	    lane.project.as_ref().and_then(|fields| fields.iter().find_map(|field| match field {
   200	        ShapeField::Field { name: field, value, .. } if field == name => Some(value.clone()),
   201	        _ => None,
   202	    })).unwrap_or_else(|| A::Member { base: Box::new(A::Focal { site: lane.site.clone() }), name: name.into(), site: lane.site.clone() })
   203	}
     1	//! Merge sources share the statement loop's type checking and runtime domain.
     2	use super::*;
     3	use snapback4_core::ir::LaneSource;
     4	
     5	impl Binder<'_> {
     6	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#21.6 — query lanes take statement-for domains except walk; the meter bounds discovery.
     7	    pub(super) fn lane_domain(&mut self, domain: &A, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(LaneSource, Ty)> {
     8	        if self.caller_kind != ProgramKind::Query && !matches!(domain, A::Read(_)) {
     9	            return Err(crate::diag::syntax_with(domain.site(), "range and list merge lanes belong in queries", "read the merge in a query; keep the job or mutation's existing page loop").guide("reads"));
    10	        }
    11	        let (bound, ty) = self.loop_domain(domain, out).map_err(|error| {
    12	            if matches!(error.code.as_str(), "E_UNBOUND_LOOP") || error.message.starts_with("for cannot iterate") || error.message.contains("static bound") {
    13	                crate::diag::syntax_with(domain.site(), "a merge for lane requires a prefix page, range(a, b), or a bounded list", "for h in range(hour(now) - 24, hour(now) + 1), for a in authors (authors: [principal] <=50), or for row in table[prefix] by index").guide("reads")
    14	            } else { error }
    15	        })?;
    16	        let source = match bound {
    17	            LoopDomain::Page(scan) => LaneSource::Scan(scan),
    18	            LoopDomain::LiteralRange { start, end_exclusive } => LaneSource::Range { start: ir::lit(Value::Int(start)), end_exclusive: ir::lit(Value::Int(end_exclusive)), max: Some(width(start, end_exclusive)) },
    19	            LoopDomain::ExpressionRange { start, end_exclusive } => LaneSource::Range { start, end_exclusive, max: range_width(domain) },
    20	            LoopDomain::ArgList { name, max } => LaneSource::List { value: ir::var(&name), max },
    21	            LoopDomain::DerivedArray { value, max, .. } => LaneSource::List { value, max },
    22	            LoopDomain::Recurse(_) => return Err(crate::diag::syntax_with(domain.site(), "a merge for lane requires a prefix page, range(a, b), or a bounded list; walk is not a lane domain", "for h in range(hour(now) - 24, hour(now) + 1), for a in authors (authors: [principal] <=50), or for row in table[prefix] by index").guide("reads")),
    23	        };
    24	        Ok((source, ty))
    25	    }
    26	}
    27	
    28	fn width(start: i64, end: i64) -> u64 { (i128::from(end) - i128::from(start)).max(0) as u64 }
    29	
    30	fn range_width(domain: &A) -> Option<u64> {
    31	    fn affine(value: &A) -> Option<(Option<&A>, i64)> {
    32	        if let Some(value) = literal_int(value) { return Some((None, value)); }
    33	        if let A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = value {
    34	            if matches!(op.as_str(), "+" | "-") && let Some(n) = literal_int(right) {
    35	                let (base, offset) = affine(left)?;
    36	                return Some((base, if op == "+" { offset.checked_add(n)? } else { offset.checked_sub(n)? }));
    37	            }
    38	        }
    39	        // This comparison is for stable scalar expressions only, never reads,
    40	        // helper calls or new ids whose repeated evaluation may differ.
    41	        fn stable(value: &A) -> bool {
    42	            match value {
    43	                A::Name { .. } | A::Literal { .. } => true,
    44	                A::Unary { value, .. } => stable(value),
    45	                A::Binary { left, right, .. } => stable(left) && stable(right),
    46	                A::Call { name, args, .. } if matches!(name.as_str(), "day" | "hour" | "bucket" | "month" | "year" | "monthStart" | "yearStart") => args.iter().all(stable),
    47	                _ => false,
    48	            }
    49	        }
    50	        stable(value).then_some((Some(value), 0))
    51	    }
    52	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#14 — ruling 28's dynamic widths stay meter-bounded; only structurally equal stable bases admit offset folding.
    53	    // Source locations do not matter, but precedence does: the printer is not
    54	    // an identity for expressions such as (a + b) * 2 and a + b * 2.
    55	    fn same(a: &A, b: &A) -> bool {
    56	        match (a, b) {
    57	            (A::Name { name: a, .. }, A::Name { name: b, .. }) => a == b,
    58	            (A::Literal { value: a, .. }, A::Literal { value: b, .. }) => a == b,
    59	            (A::Unary { op: a, value: av, .. }, A::Unary { op: b, value: bv, .. }) => a == b && same(av, bv),
    60	            (A::Binary { op: a, left: al, right: ar, .. }, A::Binary { op: b, left: bl, right: br, .. }) => a == b && same(al, bl) && same(ar, br),
    61	            (A::Call { name: a, args: av, .. }, A::Call { name: b, args: bv, .. }) => a == b && av.len() == bv.len() && av.iter().zip(bv).all(|(a, b)| same(a, b)),
    62	            _ => false,
    63	        }
    64	    }
    65	    let A::Call { args, .. } = domain else { return None };
    66	    let (a, start) = affine(&args[0])?;
    67	    let (b, end) = affine(&args[1])?;
    68	    match (a, b) {
    69	        (None, None) => Some(width(start, end)),
    70	        (Some(a), Some(b)) if same(a, b) => Some(width(start, end)),
    71	        _ => None,
    72	    }
    73	}
    74	
    75	// A lane-dependent predicate needs the eager binding snapshot. Plain row
    76	// predicates can keep the same head strategy as explicitly written lanes.
    77	pub(super) fn filter_uses_lane(value: &A, name: &str) -> bool {
    78	    let read_uses_lane = |read: &ast::ReadExpr| {
    79	        read.keys.iter().any(|key| filter_uses_lane(&key.value, name))
    80	            || read.seeks.iter().any(|seek| filter_uses_lane(&seek.value, name))
    81	            || read.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(|filter| filter_uses_lane(filter, name))
    82	            || read.matching.as_deref().is_some_and(|matching| filter_uses_lane(matching, name))
    83	            || read.range.as_ref().and_then(|range| range.cursor.as_deref())
    84	                .is_some_and(|cursor| filter_uses_lane(cursor, name))
    85	            || read.computed.values().any(|value| filter_uses_lane(value, name))
    86	    };
    87	    match value {
    88	        A::Name { name: used, .. } => used == name,
    89	        A::Focal { .. } | A::Literal { .. } => false,
    90	        A::Member { base, .. } | A::Unary { value: base, .. } => filter_uses_lane(base, name),
    91	        A::Binary { left, right, .. } => filter_uses_lane(left, name) || filter_uses_lane(right, name),
    92	        A::Call { args, .. } => args.iter().any(|arg| filter_uses_lane(arg, name)),
    93	        // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — an independent fixed point guard does
    94	        // not need a snapshot of the varying membership row.
    95	        A::Exists { read, .. } | A::Created { read, .. } | A::Read(read) => read_uses_lane(read),
    96	        _ => true,
    97	    }
    98	}

    38	    pub(super) fn prefix_bound(&self, read: &ReadExpr, index: &ast::IndexDecl, position: Position) -> Fallible<(ast::RangeSpec, Bound)> {
    39	        if let Some(range) = &read.range { return Ok((range.clone(), Bound::Literal(range.take))); }
    40	        let ceiling = if self.caller_kind == ProgramKind::Job { snapback4_core::Ceilings::job() } else { snapback4_core::Ceilings::default() }.examined;

    50	            if read.seeks.len() == 2 && low.len() == 1 && low == high { equal.push(next.clone()); }

    53	            let m = self.schema.maintain_for(&read.table)?;
    54	            let parent = m.parent_key.as_ref()?;
    55	            pinned.contains(&parent.reference).then(|| self.schema.group_bound(&m.source, &[parent.reference.clone()])).flatten()

    73	            if let Some(Some(by)) = &window

    80	            let suggestion = format!("page with `{} N` and a cursor: `{}` (declare `c: cursor ?`)", if window.is_some() { "last" } else { "first" }, self.catalog.source.expression(&A::Read(page.clone())));

   148	    /// A maintained view that keeps this total, rendered from the author's own
