   150	    // @ref LLP 3000.036#13.10 — a total over partly readable rows: the view discloses the total, never the rows.
   151	    fn total_view_recipe(&self, read: &ReadExpr, scan: &Scan) -> String {
   152	        use crate::bind::intent::{column, split};
   153	        let table = &scan.table;
   154	        let (kind, of) = self.total.clone().unwrap_or(("count", None));
   155	        // The group: the read's equalities, in its index order.
   156	        let mut groups: Vec<(String, String)> = read.keys.iter().filter_map(|key| key.column.clone().map(|column| (column, print::expression(&key.value)))).collect();
   157	        let mut filters = Vec::new();
   158	        if let Some(filter) = read.filter.clone() { split(*filter, &mut filters); }
   159	        for filter in &filters {
   160	            if let A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = filter && op == "=" && let Some(col) = column(left)
   161	                && super::super::intent::independent(right) && !groups.iter().any(|(c, _)| c == col) {
   162	                groups.push((col.to_owned(), print::expression(right)));
   163	            }
   164	        }
   165	        let suffix = if kind == "count" { "Count" } else { "Total" };
   166	        let name = format!("{table}{suffix}");
   167	        let aggregate = match &of { Some(column) => format!("sum {table} of {column}"), None => format!("count {table}") };
   168	        let (readers, who) = match snapback4_core::engine::rules::grant_decision(self.schema, scan) {
   169	            Some(grant) => (grant.quarry(), "the callers that grant admits"),
   170	            None => ("allow".to_owned(), "everyone; narrow it to the readers the total is for"),
   171	        };
   172	        if groups.is_empty() {
   173	            // @ref LLP 3000.036#17.2 — a view needs a group key (the core refuses an empty `by`): a whole-table
   174	            // total groups by a column every row holds the same value of (lane 020 pasted `count t` and got E_QUARRY_SYNTAX).
   175	            let key = (1..).map(|n| if n == 1 { "totalKey".to_owned() } else { format!("totalKey{n}") })
   176	                .find(|key| !table_columns(self.schema, table).contains(key)).unwrap();
   177	            return format!("or keep the total in a view, which discloses the total, never the rows; a view groups by a key, so give every {table} row the same one: add `{key}: enum('all') default 'all'` and `immutable {key}` to {table}, declare `view {name}: {aggregate} by .{key}` with `read <- {readers}` ({who}), then read `{name}['all']`");
   178	        }
   179	        let by = format!(" by {}", groups.iter().map(|(c, _)| format!(".{c}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "));
   180	        let lookup = format!("{name}[{}]", groups.iter().map(|(_, v)| v.clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "));
   181	        format!("or keep the total in a view, which discloses the total, never the rows: `view {name}: {aggregate}{by}` with `read <- {readers}` ({who}), then read `{lookup}`")
   182	    }

   184	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.22 — ruling 25: program existence over a prefix is a top-1 scan below page ownership; rule probes retain their exact-key path.
   185	    pub(super) fn exists_read(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
   186	        if self.catalog.tables.get(&read.table).and_then(|table| table.maintained.as_ref())
   187	            .is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty())
   188	        {
   189	            return Err(type_error(&read.site, format!("{} is one global maintained scalar, not an existence probe", read.table),
   190	                format!("compare the bare scalar instead: `{} > 0`", read.table)).guide("maintains"));
   191	        }
   192	        self.exists_probe(read, out).map_err(|error| super::super::bracket_ranges::refusal(read, false, error))
   193	    }

   195	    fn exists_probe(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
   196	        if !self.is_page(read, ReadMode::Exists)? { return Ok((self.read_value(read, ReadMode::Exists, out)?, Ty::Bool)); }
   197	        let (scan, _) = self.scan_at(read, out, Position::Exists)?;
   198	        Ok((Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Gt, left: Box::new(Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::Count, args: vec![Expr::Scan(scan)] }), right: Box::new(Expr::Literal(Value::Int(0))) }, Ty::Bool))
   199	    }

   204	}
     1	//! Seeks and row-local scan predicates.
     2	use super::*;
     3	
     4	impl<'a> Binder<'a> {
     5	    // @ref LLP 3000.011.000.001#4 — retain the binder's actual choice for why, never infer a new lookup or rewrite.
     6	    pub(super) fn record_selection(&mut self, site: &str, read: &ReadExpr, index: &ast::IndexDecl, result: &str, reason: &str) {
     7	        let named = read.keys.iter().all(|key| key.column.is_some());
     8	        let bindings = index.columns.iter().enumerate().filter_map(|(position, column)| {
     9	            let key = if named { read.keys.iter().find(|key| key.column.as_ref() == Some(column)) } else { read.keys.get(position) }?;
    10	            Some(format!("{column} = {}", self.catalog.source.operand(&key.value)))
    11	        }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
    12	        let origin = if self.catalog.synthesized.contains(&(read.table.clone(), index.name.clone())) { "synthesized; " } else { "" };
    13	        let source = if origin.is_empty() { String::new() } else { format!("; read: {}", self.catalog.read_origins.get(&read.site.to_string()).cloned().unwrap_or_else(|| print::read_expr(read))) };
    14	        let mut selection = format!("selected {} ({bindings}); result: {result}; {origin}{reason}{source}", index.name);
    15	        if !self.variants.is_empty() {
    16	            let when = self.variants.iter().map(|(name, present)| format!("{} {}", name.trim_start_matches("$intentPresent:").trim_start_matches("$intentSearch:"), if *present { "present" } else { "absent" })).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" and ");
    17	            selection = format!("when {when}: {selection}");
    18	        }
    19	        self.selections.entry(site.into()).and_modify(|prior| { if !prior.contains(&selection) { prior.push_str("; "); prior.push_str(&selection); } }).or_insert(selection);
    20	    }
    21	
    22	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#10 — a covered total unique key reads one row; additional named equalities check that row.
    23	    pub(super) fn read_value(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, mode: ReadMode, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Expr> {
    24	        let info = self.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
    25	        for key in &read.keys { if let Some(field) = &key.column { super::super::row_views::predicate_field(info, field, &read.site)?; } }
    26	        if !read.seeks.is_empty() { return Err(type_error(&read.site, "a seek needs a bounded page", "t[prefix, col >= start] first 50 by index")); }

    28	        if point.keys.is_empty() && info.maintained.as_ref().is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty()) {

    82	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.3 — a seek constrains the first index column after the pinned prefix.
    83	    pub(super) fn seek(&mut self, info: &'a TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, index: &ast::IndexDecl, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Option<ir::Range>> {
    84	        if read.seeks.is_empty() { return Ok(None); }
    85	        let mut range = ir::Range { gt: None, gte: None, lt: None, lte: None };
    86	        for bound in &read.seeks {
    87	            if index.columns.get(read.keys.len()) != Some(&bound.column) || read.matching.is_some() {
    88	                return Err(type_error(&bound.site, "a seek compares the first unpinned column of a plain index", "t[prefix, col >= start, col < end] first 50 by index"));
    89	            }
    90	            let expected = self.key_ty(info, index, &bound.column);
    91	            let (value, ty) = self.field_value(&ObjectField { spread: false, name: bound.column.clone(), value: Some(bound.value.clone()), site: bound.site.clone() }, &expected, out)?;
    92	            if !ty.comparable(&expected) || ty.is_optional() {
    93	                let endpoint = print::expression(&bound.value);
    94	                let rewrite = if ty.is_optional() {
    95	                    format!("put the read inside `if {endpoint} != null:`, then `{} where .{} {} {endpoint}`", read.table, bound.column, bound.op)
    96	                } else {
    97	                    format!("use a present {} endpoint in `{} where .{} {} {endpoint}`", expected.required().name(), read.table, bound.column, bound.op)
    98	                };
    99	                return Err(type_error(&bound.site, format!("seek on {}.{} expects present {}, got {}", read.table, bound.column, expected.required().name(), ty.name()), rewrite));
   100	            }
   101	            let slot = match bound.op.as_str() { ">" => &mut range.gt, ">=" => &mut range.gte, "<" => &mut range.lt, "<=" => &mut range.lte, _ => unreachable!("parser seek comparison") };
   102	            if slot.is_some() { return Err(type_error(&bound.site, "a seek bound is repeated", "write one lower and one upper bound")); }
   103	            *slot = Some(Box::new(self.snapshot(value, out)));
   104	        }
   105	        if (range.gt.is_some() && range.gte.is_some()) || (range.lt.is_some() && range.lte.is_some()) {
   106	            return Err(type_error(&read.site, "a seek has at most one lower and one upper bound", "t[prefix, col >= start, col < end] first 50 by index"));
   107	        }
   108	        // Null sorts below every present value. An upper-only nullable seek
   109	        // must skip the entire null segment, including its id suffixes.
   110	        if range.gt.is_none() && range.gte.is_none()
   111	            && read.seeks.first().is_some_and(|bound| self.key_ty(info, index, &bound.column).is_optional()) {
   112	            range.gt = Some(Box::new(ir::lit(Value::Null)));
   113	        }
   114	        Ok(Some(range))
   115	    }
   116	}

   119	    pub(super) fn variant(&self) -> Option<ir::VariantId> {
   120	        (!self.variants.is_empty()).then(|| {
   121	            let hash = snapback4_core::value::digest(&self.variants);
   122	            std::array::from_fn(|i| u32::from_str_radix(&hash[i * 8..i * 8 + 8], 16).expect("hex digest"))
   123	        })
   124	    }

   126	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#18.5 — a declared page shape after a scalar predicate needs no wrapper or correction loop.
   127	    pub(super) fn page_shape(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<A> {
   128	        let predicate = read.filter.as_ref()?;
   129	        let (base, name, site) = trailing_shape(predicate)?;
   130	        self.catalog.shapes.get(name)?;

   132	        // escape. A valid row/object shape inside the predicate keeps its meaning.
   133	        let mut probe = self.clone();
   134	        probe.focal = Some(("$filter".into(), Ty::Row(read.table.clone())));
   135	        probe.kind = ProgramKind::Query;
   136	        probe.in_predicate = true;
   137	        let (_, ty) = probe.expr(base, &mut setup::Body::default()).ok()?;
   138	        if matches!(ty.required(), Ty::Row(_) | Ty::Object(_) | Ty::RowShape(_) | Ty::RowObject(_) | Ty::Page(_, _) | Ty::Collection(_, _) | Ty::List(_, _)) { return None; }

   140	        remove_trailing_shape(page.filter.as_mut()?);
   141	        Some(A::ShapeUse { base: Box::new(A::Read(page)), name: name.into(), site: site.clone() })
   142	    }

   144	    pub(super) fn scan_filter(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, index: &ast::IndexDecl) -> Fallible<(ReadExpr, Option<Box<Expr>>)> {
   145	        let mut read = read.clone();
   146	        let Some(mut predicate) = read.filter.take() else { return Ok((read, None)); };
   147	        // Planning knows parameter declarations; binding also knows helper,
   148	        // local and lane selectors. Only present, infallible endpoints may
   149	        // leave the short-circuit predicate in either pass.
   150	        let required = self.scopes.iter().flat_map(|scope| scope.keys())
   151	            .filter(|name| self.binding(name).is_some_and(|binding| !binding.ty.is_optional()))
   152	            .cloned().collect();

   154	            let info = self.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
   155	            let mut parts = Vec::new(); super::super::intent::split(*predicate, &mut parts);
   156	            let mut residual = Vec::new();
   157	            for part in parts {
   158	                let candidate = super::super::intent::comparison(&part).map(|(column, op, value)| (column, vec![op], value))
   159	                    .or_else(|| super::super::intent::equality(&part, info)
   160	                        .filter(|(column, _)| !self.key_ty(info, index, column).is_optional())

   167	                } else { residual.push(part); }
   168	            }
   169	            let Some(remaining) = super::super::intent::conjunction(residual) else { return Ok((read, None)); };
   170	            predicate = remaining;
   171	        }
   172	        // A single next-column comparison is the same seek as its key spelling.
   173	        if read.seeks.is_empty() && read.matching.is_none()
   174	            && let A::Binary { op, left, right, site } = predicate.as_ref()
   175	            && matches!(op.as_str(), ">" | ">=" | "<" | "<=")
   176	            && let A::Member { base, name, .. } = left.as_ref()
   177	            && matches!(base.as_ref(), A::Focal { .. })
   178	            && index.columns.get(read.keys.len()) == Some(name)
   179	            && !has_focal(right)
   180	            && super::super::intent_aggregates::safe_endpoint(right, &required)

     5	#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
     6	pub(super) enum Position { Page, Domain, Aggregate, Exists }

     8	impl Binder<'_> {
     9	    pub(super) fn is_page(&self, read: &ReadExpr, point: ReadMode) -> Fallible<bool> {
    10	        let global = read.keys.is_empty() && self.catalog.tables.get(&read.table)
    11	            .and_then(|table| table.maintained.as_ref()).is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty());
    12	        if !read.order_keys.is_empty() || read.range.is_some() || read.matching.is_some() || read.filter.is_some() || !read.seeks.is_empty() || read.keys.is_empty() && !global { return Ok(true); }
    13	        if global { return Ok(false); }
    14	        // Speculation cannot emit setup or consume compiler names. The selected
    15	        // form binds its key expressions exactly once in the caller's body.
    16	        let mut probe = self.clone();
    17	        let info = probe.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
    18	        let mut lookup = read.clone();
    19	        if point == ReadMode::FilteredPoint && info.maintained.is_some() && lookup.index.is_none() { lookup.index = Some("byGroup".into()); }
    20	        match probe.select_index(info, &lookup, point, &mut setup::Body::default()) {
    21	            Ok(_) => Ok(false),
    22	            Err(error) if error.code == "E_QUARRY_AMBIGUOUS" => Err(error),
    23	            Err(point_error) => {
    24	                // Only an actual index prefix changes the form. A mistyped
    25	                // full key or an uncovered named equality keeps its point
    26	                // diagnostic, including the established repair.
    27	                let mut probe = self.clone();
    28	                match probe.select_index(info, read, ReadMode::Range, &mut setup::Body::default()) {
    29	                    Ok(_) => Ok(true),
    30	                    Err(error) if error.code == "E_QUARRY_AMBIGUOUS" => Err(error),
    31	                    Err(error) if error.code == "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN" => Err(error),
    32	                    Err(_) => Err(point_error),
    33	                }
    34	            },
    35	        }
    36	    }

    38	    pub(super) fn prefix_bound(&self, read: &ReadExpr, index: &ast::IndexDecl, position: Position) -> Fallible<(ast::RangeSpec, Bound)> {
    39	        if let Some(range) = &read.range { return Ok((range.clone(), Bound::Literal(range.take))); }
    40	        let ceiling = if self.caller_kind == ProgramKind::Job { snapback4_core::Ceilings::job() } else { snapback4_core::Ceilings::default() }.examined;
    41	        let pinned = &index.columns[..read.keys.len()];
    42	        // One group per distinct source key: a parent-scoped source cap bounds
    43	        // its aggregate rows too, without copying a constraint onto the view.
    44	        // A pinned unique key bounds the group as a cap does (wave 3 #7); `.id = x`
    45	        // plans as the closed seek `.id >= x and .id <= x` on the next column.
    46	        let mut equal = pinned.to_vec();
    47	        if let Some(next) = index.columns.get(read.keys.len()) {
    48	            let printed = |op: &str| read.seeks.iter().filter(|seek| &seek.column == next && seek.op == op).map(|seek| crate::print::expression(&seek.value)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    49	            let (low, high) = (printed(">="), printed("<="));
    50	            if read.seeks.len() == 2 && low.len() == 1 && low == high { equal.push(next.clone()); }
    51	        }
    52	        let cap = self.schema.group_bound(&read.table, pinned).into_iter().chain(crate::bind::unique_bound::unique_bound(self.schema, &read.table, &equal)).min().or_else(|| {
    53	            let m = self.schema.maintain_for(&read.table)?;
    54	            let parent = m.parent_key.as_ref()?;
    55	            pinned.contains(&parent.reference).then(|| self.schema.group_bound(&m.source, &[parent.reference.clone()])).flatten()
    56	        });
    57	        let bound = if position == Position::Exists { Bound::Literal(1) }

    67	            let columns = if pinned.is_empty() { String::new() } else { format!(" by {}", pinned.join(", ")) };

    70	            // @ref LLP 3000.036#13.3 — a synced table's horizon keeps the newest rows, so its page reads newest first:
    71	            // the page rewrite and N_NO_HORIZON's window then agree, and applying both draws no N_SYNC_ORDER.
    72	            let window = self.schema.table(&read.table).and_then(|table| table.sync.as_ref()).map(|sync| sync.horizon.as_ref().map(|window| window.by.clone()));
    73	            if let Some(Some(by)) = &window
    74	                && let Some(horizon) = self.catalog.tables.get(&read.table).and_then(|info| info.indexes.iter().find(|candidate| &candidate.name == by))
    75	                && horizon.words.is_none() && horizon.columns.len() > pinned.len() && horizon.columns[..pinned.len()] == *pinned {
    76	                page.index = Some(horizon.name.clone());
    77	            }
    78	            let order = if window.is_some() { ast::Order::Desc } else { ast::Order::Asc };
    79	            page.range = Some(ast::RangeSpec { order, take: 50, cursor: Some(Box::new(A::Name { name: "c".into(), site: read.site.clone() })), site: read.site.clone() });
    80	            let suggestion = format!("page with `{} N` and a cursor: `{}` (declare `c: cursor ?`)", if window.is_some() { "last" } else { "first" }, self.catalog.source.expression(&A::Read(page.clone())));

   102	    pub(super) fn check_meter_prefix(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, scan: &Scan, aggregate: bool) -> Fallible<()> {
   103	        if scan.take != Bound::Meter || snapback4_core::engine::rules::scan_visibility(self.schema, scan).uniform { return Ok(()); }

   113	        // @ref LLP 3000.028#4-put-the-rules-where-the-builder-meets-them — preserve the loop, callers and truncation cost in the reason.
   114	        let jobs = self.call_chain.last().filter(|_| self.call_chain.len() > 1)
   115	            .map(|(helper, _)| self.helper_job_callers(helper)).unwrap_or_default();
   116	        let query_site = &self.call_chain[0].1;
   117	        let declaration = self.catalog.source.declarations.get(&query_site.to_string());
   118	        if aggregate && (self.caller_kind != ProgramKind::Query || jobs.is_empty() || declaration.is_none()) {
   119	            // @ref LLP 3000.036#11 — a total has no first-N repair (app-farm lane 087).
   120	            let deciding = if missing.is_empty() { leading.join(", ") } else { missing.iter().map(|c| format!(".{c}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ") };
   121	            let grant = snapback4_core::engine::rules::grant_decision(self.schema, scan);
   122	            // @ref LLP 3000.036#17.8 — a readable grant the program requires before the total (`require exists staff[viewer]`)
   123	            // makes every row readable for every caller who reaches it, which the server decides once (read.rs `granted`).
   124	            let guard = grant.as_ref().map_or_else(String::new, |grant| format!("; or guard it with the grant, so every caller who reaches it reads every row: `require {} else …` before it (or `if {}:` around it)", grant.quarry(), grant.quarry()));

   126	                format!("pin {deciding} with where equalities so one verdict covers every row (the total then covers only that scope){guard}; {}. If the maintained count keeps the source's private read rule, its group keys must carry every field that rule reads (including {deciding}); a public total instead deliberately discloses the count across hidden rows; or return a `first N` page with a cursor and fold it in the client", self.total_view_recipe(read, scan))).guide("bounds");

   129	                return Ok(());
   130	            }
   131	            return Err(diagnostic);
   132	        }
   133	        if self.caller_kind != ProgramKind::Query || jobs.is_empty() || declaration.is_none() {
   134	            return Err(Diagnostic::new(&read.site, "E_QUARRY_BOUND", format!("{} by {}: a meter-bounded prefix needs a read rule uniformly decided by its pinned columns", scan.table, scan.index),
   135	                format!("use `first N`: `{}` (declare `c: cursor ?` and follow its cursor); for a complete read, scan by an index leading with {} and pin the deciding columns (N_NEVER_COMPLETE)", self.catalog.source.expression(&A::Read(page)), leading.join(", "))).guide("bounds"));
   136	        }
   137	        let chain = self.call_chain.iter().map(|(name, site)| format!("{name} (at {site})")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" -> ");

   139	        let authored = self.catalog.read_origins.get(&read.site.to_string()).cloned().unwrap_or_else(|| self.catalog.source.expression(&A::Read(read.clone())));
   140	        let rule = if missing.is_empty() { "read rule".into() } else { format!("{}-based read rule", missing.join(", ")) };
   141	        // @ref LLP 3000.007#23 — a whole-query replacement points to that declaration, never its shared helper.
   142	        let end = query_site.line + declaration.unwrap().lines().count() as u32 - 1;
   143	        let context = format!("; viewer query caller chain: {chain}. `{authored}` (at {}) does not decide its {rule}; a helper grants no system authority.      1	//! Resolve row extrema without changing the binary min/max overload.
     2	use super::*;
     3	
     4	impl Binder<'_> {
     5	    // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.2 — literal columns retain their encoding and seek;
     6	    // other scalar values bind to the aggregate row and fold the whole scan.
     7	    pub(super) fn extremum(&mut self, name: &str, args: &[A], out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Option<(Expr, Ty)>> {
     8	        if matches!(&args[1], A::Member { base, .. } if matches!(base.as_ref(), A::Focal { .. })) {
     9	            return self.column_extremum(name, args, out);
    10	        }
    11	        let candidate = self.aggregate_domain(&args[0]).or_else(|| {
    12	            self.catalog.extremum_domains.get(&args[0].site().to_string()).filter(|read|
    13	                !self.catalog.tables.get(&read.table).and_then(|table| table.maintained.as_ref())
    14	                    .is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty())).cloned()
    15	        });
    16	        let domain = if let Some(read) = candidate {
    17	            self.is_page(&read, ReadMode::FilteredPoint)?.then_some(read)
    18	        } else { None };
    19	        let nonempty = domain.as_ref().is_some_and(|read| self.aggregate_nonempty(read));
    20	        let mut probe = self.clone();
    21	        let source_ty = match probe.expr(&args[0], &mut setup::Body::default()) {
    22	            Ok((_, ty)) => {
    23	                if ty.sequence().is_none() { return Ok(None); }
    24	                Some(ty)
    25	            }
    26	            Err(error) if domain.is_none() => return Err(error),
    27	            Err(_) => None,
    28	        };
    29	        let outer = probe.expr(&args[1], &mut setup::Body::default());
    30	        if let Ok((_, other)) = &outer {
    31	            if !other.is_optional() && source_ty.as_ref().is_some_and(|ty| ty.comparable(other)) {
    32	                return Ok(None); // the existing binary collection overload
    33	            }
    34	            // An expression which needs the enclosing focal row cannot silently
    35	            // switch to the aggregate row. Removing just that focal binding also
    36	            // distinguishes nested shapes/aggregates that own their own `.`.
    37	            probe.focal = None;
    38	            if self.focal.is_some() && probe.expr(&args[1], &mut setup::Body::default()).is_err() {
    39	                return Err(ambiguous(args[1].site(), format!("{name}'s value expression can bind the enclosing row"),
    40	                    format!("move `{name}({}, {})` into a helper outside the enclosing shape; pass enclosing values as named arguments", print::expression(&args[0]), print::expression(&args[1]))).guide("reads"));
    41	            }
    42	        }
    43	        let (source, item, domain) = if let Some(read) = domain {
    44	            let (scan, _) = self.scan_at(&read, out, prefix::Position::Aggregate)?;
    45	            self.shape_meter_note(&scan, &read);
    46	            (Expr::Scan(scan), Ty::Row(read.table), true)
    47	        } else {
    48	            let (source, ty) = self.expr(&args[0], out)?;
    49	            (source, ty.sequence().expect("probed sequence").0, false)
    50	        };
    51	        let saved = self.focal.replace(("$filter".into(), item.clone()));
    52	        let predicate = std::mem::replace(&mut self.in_predicate, false);
    53	        let kind = std::mem::replace(&mut self.kind, ProgramKind::Query);
    54	        self.push_scope();
    55	        self.declare("$filter", item);
    56	        let mut body = setup::Body::default();
    57	        let projected = self.expr(&args[1], &mut body);
    58	        self.pop_scope();
    59	        self.kind = kind;
    60	        self.in_predicate = predicate;
    61	        self.focal = saved;
    62	        let (value, ty) = projected?;
    63	        if matches!(ty.required(), Ty::List(..) | Ty::Collection(..) | Ty::Page(..) | Ty::Object(..) | Ty::RowShape(..) | Ty::RowObject(..) | Ty::Row(..) | Ty::WriteRow(..)) {
    64	            return Err(type_error(args[1].site(), format!("{name} needs a scalar row expression, got {}", ty.name()),
    65	                format!("{name}({}, .column + 1)", print::expression(&args[0]))));
    66	        }
    67	        let value = if body.is_empty() { value } else { Expr::Block { body: body.code, value: Box::new(value) } };
    68	        let result = if nonempty && !ty.is_optional() { ty } else { ty.optional() };
    69	        Ok(Some((Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::ExtremumOf { max: name == "max", domain }, args: vec![source, value] }, result)))
    70	    }
    71	
    72	    // @ref LLP 3000.013#5.1 — domains retain aggregate admission and coverage.
    73	    fn column_extremum(&mut self, name: &str, args: &[A], out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Option<(Expr, Ty)>> {
    74	        let A::Member { base, name: field, .. } = &args[1] else { return Ok(None); };
    75	        if !matches!(base.as_ref(), A::Focal { .. }) { return Ok(None); }
    76	        // Speculation must not emit setup or consume fresh names for a scalar
    77	        // binary call such as min(value, .column) inside an existing shape.
    78	        let mut probe = self.clone();
    79	        let domain = if let Some(read) = self.aggregate_domain(&args[0]) {
    80	            self.is_page(&read, ReadMode::FilteredPoint)?.then_some(read)
    81	        } else { None };
    82	        let probed = match probe.expr(&args[0], &mut setup::Body::default()) {
    83	            Ok(value) => Some(value),
    84	            Err(error) if domain.is_none() => return Err(error),
    85	            // A streaming domain need not be a valid materialized page.
    86	            Err(_) => None,
    87	        };
    88	        if let Some((_, ty)) = probed {
    89	            let Some((item, _)) = ty.sequence() else { return Ok(None); };
    90	            if let Ok((_, other)) = probe.expr(&args[1], &mut setup::Body::default())
    91	                && !other.is_optional() && ty.comparable(&other) {
    92	                if self.member(Expr::Literal(Value::Null), item, field, args[1].site()).is_ok() {
    93	                    return Err(ambiguous(args[1].site(), format!("{name} can compare two collections or aggregate .{field} from the first"),
    94	                        format!("bind `other = .{field}` before this call and write `{name}({}, other)` for the binary comparison; for an aggregate, call `{name}(rows, .{field})` outside the enclosing shape", print::expression(&args[0]))).guide("reads"));
    95	                }
    96	                return Ok(None);
    97	            }
    98	        }
    99	        let nonempty = domain.as_ref().is_some_and(|read| self.aggregate_nonempty(read));
   100	        let (source, item, domain) = if let Some(mut read) = domain {
   101	            // Bare table names reach the binder without intent planning.
   102	            if matches!(&args[0], A::Name { .. }) {
   103	                let info = self.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
   104	                if let Some(index) = info.indexes.iter().filter(|index| index.is_plain() && index.columns.first() == Some(field)).min_by_key(|index| &index.name) {
   105	                    read.index = Some(index.name.clone());
   106	                }
   107	            }
   108	            let (mut scan, _) = self.scan_at(&read, out, prefix::Position::Aggregate)?;
   109	            if scan.orders_field(self.schema, field) {
   110	                scan.order = if name == "max" { Order::Desc } else { Order::Asc };
   111	                // Nulls contribute no extremum. Seek past their entire index
   112	                // segment, including when there are no present values.
   113	                if self.schema.tables[&read.table].columns.get(field).is_some_and(|column| matches!(column, snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Optional(_))) {
   114	                    let range = scan.range.get_or_insert(ir::Range { gt: None, gte: None, lt: None, lte: None });
   115	                    if range.gt.is_none() && range.gte.is_none() { range.gt = Some(Box::new(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))); }
   116	                }
   117	            }
   118	            // The runtime seeks the first qualifying ordered value. Price that
   119	            // candidate limit for advice only; the aggregate IR keeps its domain.
   120	            // Expired rows are charged and skipped before a value qualifies.
   121	            let mut advice = scan.clone();
   122	            if scan.orders_field(self.schema, field) && !self.schema.tables[&read.table].is_expiring() { advice.take = Bound::Literal(1); }
   123	            self.shape_meter_note(&advice, &read);
   124	            (Expr::Scan(scan), Ty::Row(read.table), true)
   125	        } else {
   126	            let (source, ty) = self.expr(&args[0], out)?;
   127	            (source, ty.sequence().expect("probed sequence").0, false)
   128	        };
   129	        let (_, ty) = self.member(Expr::Literal(Value::Null), item, field, args[1].site())?;
   130	        // Column ordering uses the same total order as binary min/max. Nulls
   131	        // contribute no value, including when every row's column is null.
   132	        let result = if nonempty && !ty.is_optional() { ty } else { ty.optional() };
   133	        Ok(Some((Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::Extremum { field: field.clone(), max: name == "max", domain }, args: vec![source] }, result)))
   134	    }
   135	}

   170	                    return Err(Diagnostic::new(site, "E_EMIT_IN_QUERY", "queries cannot emit effects", "change query to mutation before emitting an effect"));
   171	                }

   173	                    return Err(unknown(site, format!("unknown effect {effect}"), format!("declare `effect {effect}` at the top level; a worker with a service token claims what is emitted (guide effects)")));
   174	                }

   179	                    let A::Array { items, .. } = key else {
   180	                        return Err(type_error(key.site(), "once requires an array of key values", "emit notify { ... } once [recipient, periodEnd]"));
     1	//! Null facts for repeated, effect-free helper calls over lexical snapshots.
     2	use super::*;
     3	use std::collections::BTreeSet;
     4	
     5	pub(super) const CONSTANTS: &str = "$pureCallConstants";
     6	
     7	#[derive(Debug)]
     8	pub(super) enum Stable {
     9	    Literal(String),
    10	    Snapshot(String, Vec<String>),
    11	    Viewer,
    12	    Call(String, Vec<Stable>),
    13	}
    14	
    15	impl Binder<'_> {
    16	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.2 — a repeated pure call narrows like a snapshot field.
    17	    // Facts live on *every* argument root so the existing branch intersections,
    18	    // reassignment invalidation and loop fixed point apply to them as to fields.
    19	    fn stable_call(&self, value: &A) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {
    20	        if !matches!(value, A::Call { .. }) { return None; }
    21	        let mut roots = BTreeSet::new();
    22	        let stable = self.stable_value(value, &mut roots)?;
    23	        if roots.is_empty() { roots.insert(CONSTANTS.into()); }
    24	        Some((vec!["$pureCall".into(), stable.key()], roots))
    25	    }
    26	
    27	    pub(super) fn stable_value(&self, value: &A, roots: &mut BTreeSet<String>) -> Option<Stable> {
    28	        match value {
    29	            A::Literal { .. } => Some(Stable::Literal(print::expression(value))),
    30	            A::Name { name, .. } if name == "viewer" => Some(Stable::Viewer),
    31	            A::Name { name, .. } if matches!(name.as_str(), "now" | "commit" | "new") => None,
    32	            A::Name { .. } | A::Member { .. } | A::Focal { .. } => {
    33	                let (root, path) = control::field_path(value, self.focal.as_ref().map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()))?;
    34	                let runtime = if let Some(binding) = self.binding(&root) {
    35	                    roots.insert(root.clone());
    36	                    binding.runtime.clone()
    37	                } else {
    38	                    // A shape's implicit row is an immutable lexical snapshot.
    39	                    // Its unique runtime identity separates projection scopes;
    40	                    // with no mutable argument roots, use the constant anchor.
    41	                    self.focal.as_ref().filter(|(name, _)| name == &root)?.0.clone()
    42	                };
    43	                Some(Stable::Snapshot(runtime, path))
    44	            }
    45	            A::Call { name, args, .. } => {
    46	                let function = self.catalog.functions.get(name)?;
    47	                if !self.pure_functions.get(&function.name).copied().unwrap_or(false) { return None; }
    48	                Some(Stable::Call(function.name.clone(), args.iter().map(|arg| self.stable_value(arg, roots)).collect::<Option<_>>()?))
    49	            }
    50	            _ => None,
    51	        }
    52	    }
    53	
    54	    pub(super) fn narrow_call(&mut self, value: &A, present: bool) {
    55	        let Some((key, roots)) = self.stable_call(value) else { return; };
    56	        let Some(ty) = self.call_types.get(&key) else { return; };
    57	        let ty = if present { ty.required() } else { Ty::Null };
    58	        for root in roots {
    59	            let mut binding = self.binding(&root).expect("a stable root").clone();
    60	            binding.fields.insert(key.clone(), ty.clone());
    61	            self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(root, binding);
    62	        }
    63	    }
    64	
    65	    pub(super) fn record_call(&mut self, value: &A, ty: &Ty) {
    66	        if let Some((key, _)) = self.stable_call(value) { self.call_types.insert(key, ty.clone()); }
    67	    }
    68	
    69	    pub(super) fn call_refinement(&self, value: &A) -> Option<Ty> {
    70	        let (key, roots) = self.stable_call(value)?;
    71	        let mut result = None;
    72	        for root in roots {
    73	            let ty = self.binding(&root)?.fields.get(&key)?;
    74	            if result.as_ref().is_some_and(|other| other != ty) { return None; }
    75	            result = Some(ty.clone());
    76	        }
    77	        result
    78	    }
    79	}
    80	
    81	impl Stable {
    82	    // Escaped serialization of the structured identity, never source
    83	    // sites or helper temporaries. Argument order and snapshot identity matter.
    84	    pub(super) fn key(&self) -> String {
    85	        match self {
    86	            Self::Literal(value) => format!("literal:{value:?}"),
    87	            Self::Snapshot(root, path) => format!("snapshot:{root:?}:{path:?}"),
    88	            Self::Viewer => "viewer".into(),
    89	            Self::Call(name, args) => format!("call:{name:?}:{:?}", args.iter().map(Self::key).collect::<Vec<_>>()),
    90	        }
    91	    }
    92	}
    93	
    94	// Inspect expanded IR, after argument setup: this covers transitive helpers,
    95	// named shapes, aggregates and reads hidden in blocks without a second AST
    96	// effect model. It only classifies effects; calls still execute and are metered.
    97	pub(super) fn expression(value: &Expr) -> bool {
    98	    match value {
    99	        Expr::Literal(_) | Expr::Variable(_) | Expr::Input(Input::Viewer) => true,
   100	        Expr::Input(Input::Now | Input::ReadTime | Input::NewId) => false,
   101	        Expr::Get(_) | Expr::Exists(_) | Expr::Scan(_) | Expr::Merge(_) => false,
   102	        Expr::Field { base, .. } | Expr::Unary { value: base, .. } => expression(base),
   103	        Expr::Array(values) => values.iter().all(expression),
   104	        Expr::Object(fields) => fields.values().all(expression),
   105	        Expr::Shape { base, fields } => expression(base) && fields.values().all(expression),
   106	        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => expression(left) && expression(right),
   107	        Expr::Builtin { builtin, args } => builtin_pure(builtin) && args.iter().all(expression),
   108	        Expr::Block { body, value } => statements(body) && expression(value),
   109	    }
   110	}
   111	
   112	fn builtin_pure(builtin: &Builtin) -> bool {
   113	    match builtin {
   114	        Builtin::RequireUncapped => false,
   115	        Builtin::JobAs | Builtin::JobQuery { .. } => false,
   116	        Builtin::Connected | Builtin::ReceiptSettled { .. } => false,
   117	        Builtin::ProjectFields { .. } | Builtin::OptionalField { .. } | Builtin::Calendar { .. } | Builtin::Civil(_)
   118	        | Builtin::Len | Builtin::Slice | Builtin::Concat | Builtin::Lower | Builtin::Trim
   119	        | Builtin::Sha256 | Builtin::Join | Builtin::Group { .. } | Builtin::First | Builtin::Last
   120	        | Builtin::At | Builtin::Min | Builtin::Max | Builtin::Extremum { .. }
   121	        | Builtin::ExtremumOf { .. } | Builtin::Divmod { .. } | Builtin::Divide { .. } | Builtin::Sort { .. }
   122	        | Builtin::MentionTokens | Builtin::SearchPresent | Builtin::DeterministicShare
   123	        | Builtin::Count | Builtin::Sum | Builtin::CountCapped => true,
   124	    }
   125	}
   126	
   127	pub(super) fn statements(body: &[Stmt]) -> bool {
   128	    body.iter().all(|statement| match statement {
   129	        Stmt::Let { value, .. } | Stmt::Const { value, .. } | Stmt::Assign { value, .. }
   130	        | Stmt::Push { value, .. } | Stmt::Return(value) => expression(value),
   131	        Stmt::Require { condition, .. } => expression(condition),
   132	        Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body } => expression(condition) && statements(then_body) && statements(else_body),
   133	        Stmt::For { domain, body, .. } => {
   134	            let pure = match domain {
   135	                LoopDomain::LiteralRange { .. } | LoopDomain::ArgList { .. } => true,
   136	                LoopDomain::ExpressionRange { start, end_exclusive } => expression(start) && expression(end_exclusive),
   137	                LoopDomain::DerivedArray { value, .. } => expression(value),
   138	                LoopDomain::Page(_) | LoopDomain::Recurse(_) => false,
   139	            };
   140	            pure && statements(body)
   141	        }
   142	        Stmt::Break | Stmt::Continue => true,
   143	        Stmt::Check { .. } | Stmt::InsertIfAbsent { .. } | Stmt::Insert { .. }
   144	        | Stmt::Update { .. } | Stmt::Upsert { .. } | Stmt::WriteTarget { .. }
   145	        | Stmt::Delete { .. } | Stmt::DeleteTarget { .. } | Stmt::Emit { .. } => false,
     1	//! Presence facts for identical point reads across proved stable transaction states.
     2	use super::*;
     3	use std::collections::BTreeSet;
     4	const POINT: &str = "$point";
     5	
     6	impl Binder<'_> {
     7	    // A compound condition cannot prove an earlier read after a later effect.
     8	    // Unknown/table-reading helpers are conservative here; direct guards need none.
     9	    pub(super) fn point_guard_safe(&self, value: &A) -> bool {
    10	        match value {
    11	            A::Name { .. } | A::Focal { .. } | A::Literal { .. } => true,
    12	            A::Member { base, .. } | A::Unary { value: base, .. } | A::Assert { value: base, .. } => self.point_guard_safe(base),
    13	            A::Binary { left, right, .. } => self.point_guard_safe(left) && self.point_guard_safe(right),
    14	            A::Call { name, args, .. } => self.catalog.functions.get(name).is_none_or(|f| self.pure_functions.get(&f.name) == Some(&true))
    15	                && args.iter().all(|a| self.point_guard_safe(a)),
    16	            A::Read(read) | A::Exists { read, .. } => read.keys.iter().all(|k| self.point_guard_safe(&k.value)) && read.filter.is_none() && read.matching.is_none() && read.range.is_none(),
    17	            _ => false,
    18	        }
    19	    }
    20	    fn point_key(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {
    21	        if read.range.is_some() || read.matching.is_some() || read.filter.is_some() || !read.seeks.is_empty() { return None; }
    22	        let info = self.catalog.tables.get(&read.table)?;
    23	        if info.maintained.is_some() || info.decl.ephemeral.is_some() { return None; }
    24	        let mut binder = self.clone();
    25	        let mut setup = setup::Body::default();
    26	        let (bound, index, _) = binder.select_index(info, read, ReadMode::FilteredPoint, &mut setup).ok()?;
    27	        // Exists over a nonunique key does not prove a point lookup.
    28	        if !index.unique || bound.len() != index.columns.len() { return None; }
    29	        let mut roots = BTreeSet::from([pure_calls::CONSTANTS.to_owned()]);
    30	        let mut key = vec![POINT.into(), read.table.clone(), index.name];
    31	        for part in &read.keys {
    32	            key.push(format!("{:?}:{}", part.column, self.stable_value(&part.value, &mut roots)?.key()));
    33	        }
    34	        Some((key, roots))
    35	    }
    36	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.2 — key roots and the transaction-state anchor own
    37	    // facts, so lexical joins, rebindings and loop fixed points invalidate them.
    38	    pub(super) fn narrow_point(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr) {
    39	        let Some((key, roots)) = self.point_key(read) else { return; };
    40	        for root in roots {
    41	            let Some(mut binding) = self.binding(&root).cloned() else { return; };
    42	            binding.fields.insert(key.clone(), Ty::Bool);
    43	            self.scopes.last_mut().unwrap().insert(root, binding);
    44	        }
    45	    }
    46	    // @ref LLP 3000.013#14.7 — the runner buffers page domains before bodies.
    47	    /// A row from a freshly read, wholly write-free table domain is an exact
    48	    /// live point throughout the loop. Store it in the ordinary point-fact
    49	    /// domain; buffered or writeful loops must establish a current witness.
    50	    pub(super) fn narrow_fresh_row(&mut self, name: &str, ty: &Ty, site: &Site) {
    51	        let Ty::Row(table) = ty.required() else { return };
    52	        let value = A::Member {
    53	            base: Box::new(A::Name { name: name.into(), site: site.clone() }),
    54	            name: "id".into(),
    55	            site: site.clone(),
    56	        };
    57	        self.narrow_point(&ReadExpr {
    58	            cursor_prefix: None, computed: Default::default(), order_keys: Vec::new(), table,
    59	            keys: vec![ast::KeyPart { column: None, value, site: site.clone() }], seeks: Vec::new(), filter: None,
    60	            index: None, range: None, matching: None, site: site.clone(),
    61	        });
    62	    }
    63	    pub(super) fn source_may_write(&self, body: &[S]) -> bool {
    64	        let mut body = body.to_vec();
    65	        let mut found = false;
    66	        super::super::intent_walk::statements(&mut body, &mut |expr| match expr {
    67	            A::Write { .. } => found = true,
    68	            // Helpers and named shapes expand later. Declining to certify
    69	            // them keeps freshness conservative without duplicating lowering's
    70	            // recursive effect analysis here.
    71	            A::Call { name, .. } if self.catalog.functions.contains_key(name) => found = true,
    72	            A::ShapeUse { .. } => found = true,
    73	            A::Shape { fields, .. } if fields.iter().any(|field| matches!(field, ast::ShapeField::NamedSpread { .. })) => found = true,
    74	            _ => {}
    75	        });
    76	        found
    77	    }
    78	    pub(super) fn point_present(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
    79	        self.point_key(read).is_some_and(|(key, roots)| roots.iter().all(|root|
    80	            self.binding(root).is_some_and(|binding| binding.fields.get(&key) == Some(&Ty::Bool))))
    81	    }
    82	    pub(super) fn invalidate_points(&mut self) {
    83	        for scope in &mut self.scopes {
    84	            for binding in scope.values_mut() {
    85	                // A write invalidates these transaction facts, not the local's
    86	                // value. Clear shadowed roots too, and keep assignment revisions
    87	                // stable so a job sweep does not mistake a guard for an accumulator.
    88	                binding.fields.retain(|key, _| key.first().is_none_or(|k| {
    89	                    k != POINT && k != maintain_types::AUTHORIZATION && k != aggregate_facts::NONEMPTY
    90	                        && k != aggregate_facts::COUNT_CAPTURE
    91	                }));
    92	            }
    93	        }
    94	    }
    95	
    96	    // @ref LLP 3000.013#2.2 — post-write presence follows only a stable
    97	    // point key and a read grant whose deciding state the write cannot change.
    98	    pub(super) fn invalidate_after_write(&mut self, expr: &A) {
    99	        let A::Write { op: WriteOp::Update, table, fields, .. } = expr else {
   100	            self.invalidate_points(); return;
   101	        };
   102	        let Some(definition) = self.schema.table(table) else { self.invalidate_points(); return };
   103	        let mut deciding = BTreeSet::new();
   104	        definition.rules.read.fields(&mut deciding);
   105	        let independent = self.row_independent_read_proved(table);
   106	        let stable_read = independent || !fields.iter().any(|field| deciding.contains(&field.name));
   107	        // Maintained columns and self-probes can change indirectly. Lifetimes
   108	        // and through authority retain the conservative transaction barrier.
   109	        let safe = self.ordinary_write_lifetime(table) && stable_read
   110	            && definition.maintained.is_empty()
   111	            && !rule_probes(&definition.rules.read, table)
   112	            && rule_dependencies_stable(self.schema, &definition.rules.read);
   113	        for scope in &mut self.scopes {
   114	            for binding in scope.values_mut() {
   115	                binding.fields.retain(|key, _| match key.first().map(String::as_str) {
   116	                    Some(POINT) => safe && key.get(1) == Some(table) && key.get(2)
   117	                        .and_then(|index| definition.index(index)).is_some_and(|index|
   118	                            !fields.iter().any(|field| index.columns().contains(&field.name)
   119	                                || key.iter().skip(3).any(|part| part.starts_with(&format!("Some({:?}):", field.name))))),
   120	                    Some(maintain_types::AUTHORIZATION) => false,
   121	                    Some(aggregate_facts::NONEMPTY) => false,
   122	                    Some(aggregate_facts::COUNT_CAPTURE) => false,
   123	                    _ => true,
   124	                });
   125	            }
   126	        }
   127	    }
   128	
   129	    pub(super) fn ordinary_write_lifetime(&self, table: &str) -> bool {
   130	        self.schema.table(table).is_some_and(|table| table.expire.is_none()
   131	            && table.not_before.is_none() && table.through.is_none() && table.ephemeral.is_none())
   132	    }
   133	
   134	    pub(super) fn row_independent_read_proved(&self, table: &str) -> bool {
   135	        let Some(info) = self.catalog.tables.get(table) else { return false };
   136	        if self.schema.tables[table].rules.read.is_uniformly_readable() { return true; }
   137	        let Some(rule) = info.decl.rules.iter().find(|rule| rule.effect == ast::Effect::Read) else { return false };
   138	        fn proved(binder: &Binder<'_>, table: &str, value: &A) -> bool {
   139	            if let A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = value && op == "or" {
   140	                return proved(binder, table, left) || proved(binder, table, right);
   141	            }
   142	            let mut value = value.clone();
   143	            maintain_types::normalize_rule_constants(&mut value);
   144	            let mut row_dependent = false;
   145	            super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut value, &mut |expr| {
   146	                if matches!(expr, A::Focal { .. }) { row_dependent = true; }
   147	                if let A::Call { name, .. } = expr && binder.catalog.functions.contains_key(name) {
   148	                    row_dependent = true;
   149	                }
   150	                if let A::Read(read) | A::Exists { read, .. } | A::Created { read, .. } = expr {
   151	                    row_dependent |= read.table == table || binder.catalog.tables.get(&read.table)
   152	                        .is_none_or(|info| info.maintained.is_some())
   153	                        || binder.schema.table(&read.table).is_none_or(|target| !target.maintained.is_empty() || target.through.is_some());
   154	                }
   155	            });
   156	            !row_dependent && binder.point_guard_safe(&value) && binder.authorization_proved(&value, true)
   157	        }
   158	        proved(self, table, &rule.value)
   159	    }
   160	
   161	    pub(super) fn parent_rewrite(&self, value: &A) -> Option<String> {
   162	        let A::Member { base, name, .. } = value else { return None; };
   163	        let A::Read(read) = base.as_ref() else { return None; };
   164	        let info = self.catalog.tables.get(&read.table)?;
   165	        let field = Ty::from_scalar(info.columns.get(name)?);
   166	        if self.point_present(read) || field.is_optional() || info.maintained.is_some()
   167	            || info.decl.columns.iter().any(|c| c.name == *name && c.written_by.is_some() && !c.readable_by_audience) { return None; }
   168	        Some(format!("require exists {} else NOT_FOUND — a membership or required reference does not prove this row exists; alternatively bind {} ! else NOT_FOUND", print::read_expr(read), print::read_expr(read)))
   169	    }
   170	}
   171	
   172	pub(super) fn writes(body: &[Stmt]) -> bool {
   173	    ir::effects::Effects::writes_in(body)
   174	}
   175	
   176	fn rule_probes(rule: &snapback4_core::schema::RuleExpr, target: &str) -> bool {
   177	    use snapback4_core::schema::RuleExpr;
   178	    match rule {
   179	        RuleExpr::Exists { table, .. } | RuleExpr::Created { table, .. } => table == target,
   180	        RuleExpr::And(items) | RuleExpr::Or(items) => items.iter().any(|rule| rule_probes(rule, target)),
   181	        RuleExpr::Not(rule) => rule_probes(rule, target),
   182	        _ => false,
   183	    }
   184	}
   185	
   186	fn rule_dependencies_stable(schema: &snapback4_core::schema::Schema, rule: &snapback4_core::schema::RuleExpr) -> bool {
   187	    use snapback4_core::schema::RuleExpr;
   188	    match rule {
   189	        RuleExpr::Exists { table, .. } | RuleExpr::Created { table, .. } => schema.table(table)
   190	            .is_some_and(|target| target.maintained.is_empty() && target.through.is_none() && schema.maintain_for(table).is_none()),
   191	        RuleExpr::And(items) | RuleExpr::Or(items) => items.iter().all(|rule| rule_dependencies_stable(schema, rule)),
   192	        RuleExpr::Not(rule) => rule_dependencies_stable(schema, rule),
   193	        _ => true,
   194	    }
   195	}
     1	//! Static availability of a maintained point's scalar result.
     2	
     3	use super::*;
     4	use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
     5	use snapback4_core::schema::RuleTerm;
     6	
     7	pub(super) const AUTHORIZATION: &str = "$maintainAuthorization";
     8	const TARGET_ROW: &str = "$maintainTarget";
     9	
    10	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
    11	enum AuthorizationDomain {
    12	    /// Program `exists` observes only rows readable by the current viewer.
    13	    Program,
    14	    /// A table rule's witness tests raw row presence below the target rule.
    15	    Rule,
    16	}
    17	
    18	impl Binder<'_> {
    19	    /// A private maintained point is nullable: authorization may withhold a
    20	    /// stored value and the logical zero alike. Keep the numeric type required
    21	    /// only when the key or a surviving guard proves the view's read rule.
    22	    // @ref LLP 3000.013#14.7 — hidden maintained hits and misses are null;
    23	    // an admitted miss alone becomes the aggregate identity.
    24	    pub(super) fn maintained_result_type(
    25	        &self,
    26	        maintain: &ast::MaintainDecl,
    27	        read: &ReadExpr,
    28	        ty: Ty,
    29	    ) -> Ty {
    30	        if !self.maintained_parent_is_live(maintain, read) {
    31	            return ty.optional();
    32	        }
    33	        if (self.caller_kind == ProgramKind::Job && self.viewer_depth.is_none())
    34	            || self
    35	                .schema
    36	                .table(&read.table)
    37	                .is_some_and(|table| table.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable())
    38	        {
    39	            return ty;
    40	        }
    41	        let Some(expected) = substituted_rule(maintain, read) else {
    42	            return ty.optional();
    43	        };
    44	        if self.authorization_proved(&expected, true) {
    45	            ty
    46	        } else {
    47	            ty.optional()
    48	        }
    49	    }
    50	
    51	    /// A system job bypasses read policy, but an inherited maintained point
    52	    /// still disappears with its through parent. A dominating exact-point
    53	    /// witness or the live parent row supplying `.id` proves that lifetime.
    54	    fn maintained_parent_is_live(&self, maintain: &ast::MaintainDecl, read: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
    55	        let Some(parent) = self.schema.maintain_for(&read.table).and_then(|maintain| maintain.parent_key.as_ref()) else { return true };
    56	        let Some(key) = group_keys(maintain, read).remove(&parent.reference) else { return false };
    57	        let site = key.site().clone();
    58	        let mut parent_read = ReadExpr {

    68	    /// Store a truth fact on every lexical root it depends on. Existing branch
    69	    /// intersection and rebinding logic then keeps exactly the facts that
    70	    /// survive control flow and clears facts whose operands change.
    71	    pub(super) fn record_maintain_authorization(&mut self, condition: &A, truth: bool) {
    72	        let Some((key, roots)) = self.authorization_key(condition, truth, AuthorizationDomain::Program) else {
    73	            return;
    74	        };
    75	        for root in roots {
    76	            let Some(mut binding) = self.binding(&root).cloned() else {
    77	                return;
    78	            };
    79	            binding.fields.insert(key.clone(), Ty::Bool);
    80	            self.scopes
    81	                .last_mut()
    82	                .expect("a scope")
    83	                .insert(root, binding);
    84	        }
    85	    }
    86	
    87	    fn authorization_holds(&self, condition: &A, truth: bool) -> bool {
    88	        self.authorization_key(condition, truth, AuthorizationDomain::Rule)
    89	            .is_some_and(|(key, roots)| {
    90	                roots.iter().all(|root| {
    91	                    self.binding(root)
    92	                        .is_some_and(|binding| binding.fields.get(&key) == Some(&Ty::Bool))
    93	                })
    94	            })
    95	    }

    97	    /// Facts may prove a read rule without restating the whole expression:
    98	    /// one admitted disjunct proves an `or`, while every conjunct proves an
    99	    /// `and`. This is the same propositional narrowing used by control flow.
   100	    pub(super) fn authorization_proved(&self, condition: &A, truth: bool) -> bool {
   101	        if self.condition_known(condition, truth) || self.authorization_holds(condition, truth) {
   102	            return true;
   103	        }
   104	        match condition {
   105	            A::Unary { op, value, .. } if op == "not" => self.authorization_proved(value, !truth),
   106	            A::Binary {
   107	                op, left, right, ..
   108	            } if op == "and" && truth => {
   109	                self.authorization_proved(left, true) && self.authorization_proved(right, true)
   110	            }
   111	            A::Binary {
   112	                op, left, right, ..
   113	            } if op == "and" => {
   114	                self.authorization_proved(left, false) || self.authorization_proved(right, false)
   115	            }
   116	            A::Binary {
   117	                op, left, right, ..
   118	            } if op == "or" && truth => {
   119	                self.authorization_proved(left, true) || self.authorization_proved(right, true)
   120	            }
   121	            A::Binary {
   122	                op, left, right, ..
   123	            } if op == "or" => {
   124	                self.authorization_proved(left, false) && self.authorization_proved(right, false)
   125	            }
   126	            _ => false,
   127	        }
   128	    }
   129	
   130	    fn authorization_key(
   131	        &self,
   132	        condition: &A,
   133	        truth: bool,
   134	        domain: AuthorizationDomain,
   135	    ) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {
   136	        let mut roots = BTreeSet::new();
   137	        let key = self.condition_identity(condition, truth, domain, &mut roots)?;
   138	        if roots.is_empty() {
   139	            roots.insert(pure_calls::CONSTANTS.to_owned());
   140	        }
   141	        Some((vec![AUTHORIZATION.into(), key], roots))
   142	    }
   143	
   144	    fn condition_identity(
   145	        &self,
   146	        condition: &A,
   147	        truth: bool,
   148	        domain: AuthorizationDomain,
   149	        roots: &mut BTreeSet<String>,
   150	    ) -> Option<String> {
   151	        match condition {
   152	            A::Unary { op, value, .. } if op == "not" => {
   153	                self.condition_identity(value, !truth, domain, roots)
   154	            }
   155	            A::Exists { read, .. } => {
   156	                let raw = matches!(domain, AuthorizationDomain::Rule)
   157	                    || truth
   158	                    || self.program_absence_is_raw(read);
   159	                let identity = self.read_identity("exists", if raw { "raw" } else { "readable" }, read, roots)?;
   160	                Some(format!("truth:{truth}:{identity}"))
   161	            }
   162	            A::Created { read, .. } => {
   163	                let identity = self.read_identity("created", "raw", read, roots)?;
   164	                Some(format!("truth:{truth}:{identity}"))
   165	            }
   166	            A::Binary {
   167	                op, left, right, ..
   168	            } if matches!(op.as_str(), "=" | "!=") => {
   169	                let mut operands = [
   170	                    self.value_identity(left, domain, roots)?,
   171	                    self.value_identity(right, domain, roots)?,
   172	                ];
   173	                operands.sort();
   174	                let equal = (op == "=") == truth;
   175	                Some(format!("compare:{equal}:{:?}", operands))
   176	            }
   177	            _ => Some(format!(
   178	                "truth:{truth}:{}",
   179	                self.value_identity(condition, domain, roots)?
   180	            )),
   181	        }
   182	    }
   183	
   184	    fn value_identity(&self, value: &A, domain: AuthorizationDomain, roots: &mut BTreeSet<String>) -> Option<String> {
   185	        match value {
   186	            A::Unary { op, value, .. } => {
   187	                Some(format!("unary:{op}:{}", self.value_identity(value, domain, roots)?))
   188	            }
   189	            A::Binary {
   190	                op, left, right, ..
   191	            } => Some(format!(
   192	                "binary:{op}:{}:{}",
   193	                self.value_identity(left, domain, roots)?,
   194	                self.value_identity(right, domain, roots)?
   195	            )),
   196	            // Nested witnesses have no known polarity. Keep their observable
   197	            // program domain separate from the raw witness used by a rule.
   198	            A::Exists { read, .. } => self.read_identity("exists", match domain {
   199	                AuthorizationDomain::Program => "readable",
   200	                AuthorizationDomain::Rule => "raw",
   201	            }, read, roots),
   202	            A::Created { read, .. } => self.read_identity("created", "raw", read, roots),
   203	            _ => self.stable_value(value, roots).map(|value| value.key()),
   204	        }
   205	    }
   206	
   207	    fn read_identity(
   208	        &self,
   209	        kind: &str,
   210	        domain: &str,
   211	        read: &ReadExpr,
   212	        roots: &mut BTreeSet<String>,
   213	    ) -> Option<String> {
   214	        if read.range.is_some()
   215	            || read.matching.is_some()
   216	            || read.filter.is_some()
   217	            || !read.seeks.is_empty()
   218	        {
   219	            return None;
   220	        }
   221	        let keys = read
   222	            .keys
   223	            .iter()
   224	            .map(|key| {
   225	                Some(format!(
   226	                    "{:?}:{}",
   227	                    key.column,
   228	                    self.value_identity(&key.value, AuthorizationDomain::Rule, roots)?
   229	                ))
   230	            })
   231	            .collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?;
   232	        Some(format!("{kind}:{domain}:{:?}:{:?}:{keys:?}", read.table, read.index))
   233	    }
   234	
   235	    /// A negative program existence test establishes raw absence only when
   236	    /// every row selected by its complete point key is structurally readable.
   237	    fn program_absence_is_raw(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
   238	        if self.caller_kind == ProgramKind::Job && self.viewer_depth.is_none() {
   239	            return true;
   240	        }

   294	/// `expire at .column`: a `time` column (optional means "never" when null)
   295	/// with an index leading with it, so the sweep finds what is due without a
   296	/// walk. The rewrite is the index line to add.
   297	fn bind_expire(info: &TableInfo, column: &str, site: &Site) -> Fallible<String> {
   298	    bind_lifetime_column(info, column, site, "expire at", "ends", "expiresAt")
   299	}
   300	
   301	fn bind_lifetime_column(info: &TableInfo, column: &str, site: &Site, clause: &str, verb: &str, example: &str) -> Fallible<String> {
   302	    let table = &info.decl.name;
   303	    let Some(kind) = info.columns.get(column) else {
   304	        return Err(type_error(site, format!("{clause} .{column}: table {table} has no column {column}"), name_rewrite(column, info.columns.keys().map(String::as_str), Some(&format!("declare `{example}: time ?` and {clause} .{example}")))));
   305	    };
   306	    if kind.kind != TypeKind::Time {
   307	        return Err(type_error(site, format!("{clause} .{column}: {table}.{column} is {}, and a row's read life {verb} at a time", crate::print::scalar_type(kind)), format!("{column}: time ?")));
   308	    }
   309	    let title = title(column);
   310	    if !info.indexes.iter().any(|index| index.is_plain() && index.columns.first().map(String::as_str) == Some(column)) {
   311	        return Err(Diagnostic::new(site, "E_SCHEMA_CONSTRAINT", format!("{clause} .{column} needs an index leading with {column}, so the server finds the time boundary without a walk"), format!("by by{title}: {column}")).guide("expire"));
   312	    }
   313	    Ok(column.to_owned())
   314	}

    54	impl Runner<'_> {
    55	    // @ref LLP 3000.022#14.3 — no predicted delta enters receipt-derived state.
    56	    pub(super) fn defer_receipt_maintain(&self, m: &Maintain) -> bool {
    57	        self.device && self.schema.receipt_dependencies.contains_key(&m.target)
    58	    }
    59	
    60	    // Cache ordinary group keys once per target/query. Predicates and receipt
    61	    // fields are never evaluated; unknown/computed keys conservatively freeze
    62	    // the target. An unpinned read is incomplete if any group is dirty, while
    63	    // each returned row still decides independently whether to recount.
    64	    #[inline(never)]
    65	    pub(super) fn receipt_maintain_pending(&mut self, m: &Maintain, group: Option<&[Value]>) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    66	        if !self.defer_receipt_maintain(m) { return Ok(false); }
    67	        if !self.receipt_pending_groups.contains_key(&m.target) {
    68	            let fields = if let Some(k) = &m.row_key { vec![k.source_key.clone()] }
    69	                else { m.by.iter().map(|f| m.aliases.get(f).unwrap_or(f).clone()).collect() };
    70	            let mut groups = self.receipt_overlay_groups(&m.source, &fields, !m.computed.is_empty())?;
    71	            if let Some(k) = &m.row_key {
    72	                let member = self.receipt_overlay_groups(&k.table, std::slice::from_ref(&k.member_key), false)?;
    73	                groups = groups.zip(member).map(|(mut source, member)| { source.extend(member); source });
    74	            }
    75	            self.receipt_pending_groups.insert(m.target.clone(), groups);
    76	        }
    77	        let dirty = self.receipt_pending_groups[&m.target].as_ref().is_none_or(|keys|
    78	            group.map_or(!keys.is_empty(), |group| keys.iter().any(|key| key == group)));
    79	        if dirty {
    80	            self.value_source_incomplete = true;
    81	            self.components.note(|frame| frame.pending = true);
    82	        }
    83	        Ok(dirty)
    84	    }
    85	
    86	    fn receipt_overlay_groups(&self, table: &str, fields: &[String], computed: bool) -> Result<Option<Vec<Vec<Value>>>, RuntimeError> {
    87	        if !self.tx.has_provisional(table)? { return Ok(Some(Vec::new())); }
    88	        if computed || fields.iter().any(|f| self.schema.receipt_dependencies.get(table).is_some_and(|owned| owned.contains(f))) {
    89	            return Ok(None);
    90	        }
    91	        self.tx.provisional_groups(table, fields)
    92	    }
    93	
    94	    // Recover a read's parent/group from the pinned index. A member point uses
    95	    // one ordinary-key lookup; a scan without the parent stays conservative.
    96	    pub(super) fn receipt_read_group(&mut self, m: &Maintain, index: &str, key: &[Value]) -> Result<Option<Vec<Value>>, RuntimeError> {
    97	        if !self.defer_receipt_maintain(m) { return Ok(None); }
    98	        let columns = self.schema.tables[&m.target].indexes[index].columns();
    99	        let pinned = |field: &str| columns.iter().position(|c| c == field).and_then(|i| key.get(i)).cloned();
   100	        if let Some(k) = &m.row_key {
   101	            if let Some(parent) = pinned(&k.member_key) { return Ok(Some(vec![parent])); }
   102	            if let Some(id) = pinned("member").or_else(|| pinned("id")) {
   103	                let row = self.metered_scan(|r, scope| r.tx.get(scope, &k.table, id.as_str()?))?;
   104	                return Ok(row.and_then(|row| row.field(&k.member_key).ok().cloned()).map(|v| vec![v]));
   105	            }
   106	            return Ok(None);
   107	        }
   108	        Ok(m.by.iter().map(|f| pinned(f)).collect())
   109	    }

   111	    pub(super) fn maintain(&mut self, source: &str, old: Option<&Value>, new: Option<&Value>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   112	        self.receipt_pending_groups.clear();
   113	        // Inserts ordinarily charge their actual aggregate work. A deferred
   114	        // predicate cannot select that work on a device, so use the same public
   115	        // worst-case reservation as update/delete before skipping it. Already
   116	        // prepaid writes must not pay twice.
   117	        if self.device && !self.maintain_prepaid && self.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|
   118	            m.source == source && m.private.is_none() && m.row_key.is_none() && (self.schema.receipt_dependencies.contains_key(&m.target) || self.schema.tables[&m.target].sync.is_none())) {
   119	            self.reserve_maintains(source, &self.schema.tables[source])?;
   120	        }
   121	        self.maintain_row_views(source, old, new)?;
   122	        let maintains = self.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|maintain| maintain.source == source && maintain.row_key.is_none());
   123	        for maintain in maintains {
   124	            if self.defer_receipt_maintain(maintain) || (self.device && self.schema.tables[&maintain.target].sync.is_none()) { continue; }
   125	            let source_bytes = super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(&self.schema.tables[&maintain.source]);
   126	            let target = &self.schema.tables[&maintain.target];
   127	            let bytes = source_bytes.saturating_mul(2).saturating_add(super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(target).saturating_mul(16 + 4 * target.indexes.len() as u64));
   128	            let prepaid = self.maintain_prepaid;
   129	            if maintain.private.is_some() && self.column_prepaid { self.maintain_prepaid = true; }
   130	            let result = self.maintain_scratch(bytes, |runner| runner.maintain_one(maintain, old, new));
   131	            self.maintain_prepaid = prepaid;
   132	            result?;
   133	        }
   134	        Ok(())
   135	    }

   137	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.9 — reserve interpreted images, both keys, targets and index buffers before allocation; release transient ownership on every exit.
   138	    pub(super) fn maintain_scratch<T>(&mut self, bytes: u64, work: impl FnOnce(&mut Self) -> Result<T, RuntimeError>) -> Result<T, RuntimeError> {
   139	        if self.maintain_prepaid { return work(self); }
   140	        self.meter.heap(bytes)?;
   141	        let result = work(self);
   142	        self.meter.used.heap_bytes = self.meter.used.heap_bytes.saturating_sub(bytes);
   143	        result
   144	    }

   146	    fn maintain_one(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, old: Option<&Value>, new: Option<&Value>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   147	        let table = self.schema.table(&maintain.source).expect("validated");
   148	        let mut changes: Vec<(Vec<Value>, i128)> = Vec::new();
   149	        for (image, direction) in [(old, -1), (new, 1)] {
   150	            let Some(image) = image else { continue };
   151	            self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Steps, maintain.evaluation_steps())?;
   152	            let image = crate::schema::row_image(image, table);
   153	            if !maintain.selected(&image) { continue; }
   154	            let key = maintain.source_key(&image);
   155	            let change = delta(maintain, &image, direction)?;
   156	            if let Some(existing) = changes.iter_mut().find(|(k, _)| *k == key) { existing.1 += change; }
   157	            else { changes.push((key, change)); }
   158	        }
   159	        for (key, change) in changes {
   160	            #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   161	            if let Some(retiring) = self.retiring.as_mut() {
   162	                let entry = retiring.aggregates.entry((maintain.target.clone(), canonical_json(&key))).or_insert_with(|| (key, 0));
   163	                entry.1 = entry.1.checked_add(change).ok_or_else(|| aggregate_overflow(maintain))?;
   164	                continue;
   165	            }
   166	            if change != 0 || self.live_sum(maintain) {
   167	                self.apply_aggregate(maintain, key, change, None)?;
   168	            }
   169	        }
   170	        Ok(())
   171	    }

   173	    /// Apply a retirement's net aggregate changes, one per target row, in
   174	    /// key order. Only a total the commit really ends with can overflow.
   175	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   176	    pub(super) fn apply_retired_aggregates(&mut self, changes: BTreeMap<(String, String), (Vec<Value>, i128)>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   177	        let schema = self.schema;
   178	        for ((target, _), (key, change)) in changes {
   179	            let maintain = schema.maintain_for(&target).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing maintain for {target}")))?;
   180	            if change != 0 || self.live_sum(maintain) {
   181	                self.apply_aggregate(maintain, key, change, None)?;
   182	            }
   183	        }
   184	        Ok(())
   185	    }

   187	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   188	    pub(crate) fn rebuild_maintain_reported(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, report: &mut crate::store::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   189	        let started = std::time::Instant::now();
   190	        self.rebuilt_source_rows = 0;

   197	    /// Derive every legacy-to-digest aggregate id map to a fixed point, then
   198	    /// rewrite typed references before rebuilding the target rows. The fixed
   199	    /// point matters when one aggregate groups by a reference to another.
   200	    /// Every pass is inside the adoption writer transaction, so no reader can
   201	    /// observe a dangling intermediate reference.
   202	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   203	    pub(crate) fn rewrite_legacy_maintained_references(&mut self, maintains: &[Maintain]) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
   204	        let mut legacy = Vec::new();
   205	        for maintain in maintains.iter().filter(|maintain| maintain.private.is_none() && maintain.row_key.is_none()) {
   206	            let target = self.schema.tables[&maintain.target].clone();
   207	            let fields: Vec<_> = maintain.group_fields().into_iter().map(|field| {
   208	                let kind = target.columns.get(&field).expect("validated maintain group").clone();
   209	                (field, kind)
   210	            }).collect();
   211	            let mut rows = Vec::new();
   212	            let mut bounds = Bounds::default();
   213	            loop {
   214	                let page = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.scan(allowance, &maintain.target, "byId", &[], &bounds, Dir::Asc, 256))?;
   215	                if page.is_empty() { break; }
   216	                for (row, encoded) in page {
   217	                    bounds.after = crate::key::decode(&encoded);
   218	                    let old = row.field("id")?.as_str()?.to_owned();
   219	                    let key = fields.iter().map(|(field, kind)| Ok((kind.clone(), row.field(field)?.clone())))
   220	                        .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, RuntimeError>>()?;
   221	                    rows.push((old, key));
   222	                }
   223	            }
   224	            legacy.push((maintain.target.clone(), rows));
   225	        }
   226	
   227	        let mut ids: BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, String>> = BTreeMap::new();
   228	        let mut settled = false;
   229	        for _ in 0..=legacy.len() {
   230	            let mut next_ids: BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, String>> = BTreeMap::new();
   231	            for (target, rows) in &legacy {
   232	                let target_ids = next_ids.entry(target.clone()).or_default();
   233	                for (old, key) in rows {
   234	                    let key: Vec<_> = key.iter().map(|(kind, value)| {
   235	                        let mut value = value.clone();
   236	                        rewrite_legacy_maintained_reference(kind, &mut value, &ids);
   237	                        value
   238	                    }).collect();
   239	                    let next = maintained_id(&key);
   240	                    if old != &next { target_ids.insert(old.clone(), next); }
   241	                }
   242	            }
   243	            if next_ids == ids {
   244	                ids = next_ids;
   245	                settled = true;
   246	                break;
   247	            }
   248	            ids = next_ids;
   249	        }
   250	        if !settled {
