   252	        }
   253	
   254	        let references: Vec<_> = self.schema.tables.iter().filter_map(|(name, table)| {
   255	            let columns: Vec<_> = table.columns.iter().filter_map(|(column, kind)|
   256	                has_legacy_maintained_reference(kind, &ids).then(|| (column.clone(), kind.clone())))
   257	                .collect();
   258	            (!columns.is_empty()).then(|| (name.clone(), table.clone(), columns))
   259	        }).collect();
   260	        let mut rewritten = 0_u64;
   261	        for (name, table, columns) in references {
   262	            let mut bounds = Bounds::default();
   263	            loop {
   264	                let page = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.scan(allowance, &name, "byId", &[], &bounds, Dir::Asc, 256))?;
   265	                if page.is_empty() { break; }
   266	                for (old, encoded) in page {
   267	                    bounds.after = crate::key::decode(&encoded);
   268	                    let mut next = old.clone();
   269	                    let mut changed = false;
   270	                    if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = &mut next {
   271	                        for (column, kind) in &columns {
   272	                            let Some(value) = fields.get_mut(column) else { continue };
   273	                            changed |= rewrite_legacy_maintained_reference(kind, value, &ids);
   274	                        }
   275	                    }
   276	                    if changed {
   277	                        self.tx.put(&name, &table, &next, Some(&old))?;
   278	                        rewritten = rewritten.saturating_add(1);
   279	                    }
   280	                }
   281	            }
   282	        }
   283	        Ok(rewritten)
   284	    }
   285	
   286	    /// Build one aggregate from the rows in the store: the target's rows go,
   287	    /// then every source row contributes once. A schema change that adds or
   288	    /// redefines a `maintain` calls this, so a count over existing rows is
   289	    /// right from its first read (day two, LLP 2000.000 §11.2).
   290	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   291	    pub(crate) fn rebuild_maintain(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   292	        let heap = self.meter.used.heap_bytes;
   293	        let saved = self.column_rebuild;
   294	        self.column_rebuild |= maintain.private.is_some();
   295	        let result = self.rebuild_maintain_inner(maintain);
   296	        self.column_rebuild = saved;
   297	        self.meter.used.heap_bytes = heap;
   298	        result
   299	    }
   300	
   301	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   302	    fn rebuild_maintain_inner(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   303	        if maintain.row_key.is_some() { return self.rebuild_row_view(maintain); }
   304	        let target = self.schema.table(&maintain.target).expect("validated");
   305	        let mut bounds = Bounds::default();
   306	        let target_bytes = super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(target).saturating_mul(16 + 4 * target.indexes.len() as u64);
   307	        self.meter.heap(target_bytes.saturating_mul(256))?;
   308	        loop {
   309	            let page = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.scan(allowance, &maintain.target, "byId", &[], &bounds, Dir::Asc, 256))?;
   310	            if page.is_empty() { break; }
   311	            for (existing, key) in page {
   312	                bounds.after = crate::key::decode(&key);
   313	                self.refuse_timeline_write(&maintain.target, crate::schema::Effect::Delete)?;
   314	                let entries = self.tx.delete(&maintain.target, target, &existing)?;
   315	                self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::IndexEntries, entries)?;
   316	                self.row_changed(&maintain.target, Some(&existing), None, Origin::Maintained)?;
   317	            }
   318	        }
   319	        let source = self.schema.table(&maintain.source).expect("validated");
   320	        self.meter.heap(super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(source).saturating_mul(256))?;
   321	        let mut totals: BTreeMap<String, (Vec<Value>, i128)> = BTreeMap::new();
   322	        bounds = Bounds::default();
   323	        loop {
   324	            let page = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.scan(allowance, &maintain.source, "byId", &[], &bounds, Dir::Asc, 256))?;
   325	            if page.is_empty() { break; }
   326	            for (mut image, key) in page {
   327	                self.rebuilt_source_rows += 1;
   328	                bounds.after = crate::key::decode(&key);
   329	                materialize_optional_fields(&mut image, source);
   330	                self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Examined, 1)?;
   331	                self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Steps, maintain.evaluation_steps())?;
   332	                if !maintain.selected(&image) { continue; }
   333	                // @ref LLP 3000.028#6 — a total unique owner key has one contribution; backfill streams bounded pages instead of retaining all lifetime keys in a totals map.
   334	                if maintain.own_keys.is_some() {
   335	                    let heap = self.meter.used.heap_bytes;
   336	                    self.meter.heap(target_bytes)?;
   337	                    self.apply_aggregate(maintain, maintain.source_key(&image), 1, Some(true))?;
   338	                    self.meter.used.heap_bytes = heap;
   339	                    continue;
   340	                }
   341	                self.meter.heap(target_bytes)?;
   342	                let key = maintain.source_key(&image);
   343	                let change = delta(maintain, &image, 1)?;
   344	                let entry = match totals.entry(canonical_json(&key)) {
   345	                    std::collections::btree_map::Entry::Occupied(entry) => {
   346	                        self.meter.used.heap_bytes -= target_bytes;
   347	                        entry.into_mut()
   348	                    }
   349	                    std::collections::btree_map::Entry::Vacant(entry) => entry.insert((key, 0)),
   350	                };
   351	                entry.1 = entry.1.checked_add(change).ok_or_else(|| aggregate_overflow(maintain))?;
   352	            }
   353	        }
   354	        for (_, (key, change)) in totals {
   355	            if change != 0 || self.live_sum(maintain) {
   356	                self.apply_aggregate(maintain, key, change, Some(true))?;
   357	            }
   358	        }
   359	        Ok(())
   360	    }
   361	
   362	    fn live_sum(&self, maintain: &Maintain) -> bool {
   363	        matches!(maintain.kind, MaintainKind::Sum { .. }) && self.schema.table(&maintain.source).is_some_and(|source| source.is_expiring())
   364	    }
   365	
   366	    /// A zero sum is still a group while source rows remain: one signed
   367	    /// contribution may expire before the other. Keep its candidate row so
   368	    /// a scan, snapshot and offline replica can discover the later live sum.
   369	    fn aggregate_source_present(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, key: &[Value]) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
   370	        // One returned source image and its encoded key (MemoryRows also materializes count_prefix).
   371	        let bytes = super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(&self.schema.tables[&maintain.source]).saturating_mul(2);
   372	        self.maintain_scratch(bytes, |runner| {
   373	            let source = &runner.schema.tables[&maintain.source];
   374	            let index = maintain.contribution_index();
   375	            if !source.indexes.contains_key(&index) { return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!("maintain {} needs its contribution index", maintain.target))); }
   376	            runner.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Examined, 1)?;
   377	            let present = runner.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.count_prefix(allowance, &maintain.source, &index, key, 1))? != 0;
   378	            // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.9 — a local positive witness keeps zero; absence needs complete, currently held source coverage.
   379	            if !present && runner.device && !runner.aggregate_source_complete(maintain, key)? {
   380	                runner.unknown_fact(&maintain.source, &index, key, "zero-sum occupancy");
   381	                return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction("the zero-sum group's source occupancy is not known locally; queued without a predicted row".into()));
   382	            }
   383	            Ok(present)
   384	        })
   385	    }
   386	
   387	    fn aggregate_source_complete(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, key: &[Value]) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
   388	        let source = &self.schema.tables[&maintain.source];
   389	        if source.sync.as_ref().is_none_or(|sync| sync.horizon.is_some()) || self.schema.maintain_subset_unknown(&maintain.target) { return Ok(false); }
   390	        // The target has the same bare audience keys, but no source horizon.
   391	        if !self.upsert_absence_proven(&maintain.target, "byGroup", &target_group_key(maintain, key))? { return Ok(false); }
   392	        for (name, index) in &source.indexes {
   393	            let values = index.columns().iter().map_while(|column| maintain.by.iter().position(|field| field == column).map(|n| key[n].clone())).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   394	            if self.retained_group(&maintain.source, name, &values)? { return Ok(false); }
   395	        }
   396	        Ok(true)
   397	    }
   398	
   399	    fn apply_aggregate(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, key: Vec<Value>, change: i128, source_present: Option<bool>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   400	        if self.schema.timelines.iter().any(|t| t.owns(&maintain.target)) {
   401	            return Err(super::timeline::refusal("E_TIMELINE_OWNED", "timeline-owned tables cannot be aggregate targets; maintain a mapped application table instead"));
   402	        }
   403	        let id = maintained_id(&key);
   404	        let target = self.schema.table(&maintain.target).expect("validated");
   405	        self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Examined, 1)?;
   406	        let existing = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.get(allowance, &maintain.target, &id))?;
   407	        let target_key = target_group_key(maintain, &key);
   408	        if self.device && existing.is_none() && !self.upsert_absence_proven(&maintain.target, "byGroup", &target_key)? {
   409	            self.unknown_fact(&maintain.target, "byGroup", &target_key, "aggregate base");
   410	            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction("the affected aggregate value or its absence is not known locally; queued without a predicted row".into()));

   796	pub enum RuleExpr {
   797	    Public { reason: String },
   798	    Allow,
   799	    Deny,
   800	    Eq(RuleTerm, RuleTerm),
   801	    Ne(RuleTerm, RuleTerm),
   802	    /// Ordered comparisons are confined to named update transitions and to
   803	    /// insert rules over the row's own required int/time columns.
   804	    Compare(crate::ir::BinaryOp, RuleTerm, RuleTerm),
   805	    And(Vec<RuleExpr>),
   806	    Or(Vec<RuleExpr>),
   807	    Not(Box<RuleExpr>),
   808	    Exists {
   809	        table: String, index: String, key: Vec<RuleTerm>, provenance: KeyProvenance,
   810	        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
   811	        equalities: Vec<(String, RuleTerm)>,
   812	    },
   813	    /// True only when the indexed target row was inserted earlier in this
   814	    /// same transaction; insert rules only.
   815	    Created {
   816	        table: String, index: String, key: Vec<RuleTerm>, provenance: KeyProvenance,
   817	        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
   818	        equalities: Vec<(String, RuleTerm)>,
   819	    },
   820	}
   821	
   822	impl RuleExpr {
   823	    pub fn is_public(&self) -> bool {
   824	        matches!(self, Self::Public { .. })
   825	    }
   826	
   827	    pub fn is_uniformly_readable(&self) -> bool {
   828	        matches!(self, Self::Public { .. } | Self::Allow)
   829	    }
   830	
   831	    /// Every `.field` the rule reads, on any probe key or comparison.
   832	    pub fn fields(&self, out: &mut BTreeSet<String>) {
   833	        let term = |term: &RuleTerm, out: &mut BTreeSet<String>| {
   834	            if let RuleTerm::Field(field) | RuleTerm::Reference { column: field, .. } | RuleTerm::ReferenceChain { column: field, .. } = term {
   835	                out.insert(field.clone());
   836	            }
   837	        };
   838	        match self {
   839	            Self::Public { .. } | Self::Allow | Self::Deny => {}
   840	            Self::Eq(left, right) | Self::Ne(left, right) | Self::Compare(_, left, right) => {
   841	                term(left, out);
   842	                term(right, out);
   843	            }
   844	            Self::And(items) | Self::Or(items) => items.iter().for_each(|item| item.fields(out)),
   845	            Self::Not(inner) => inner.fields(out),
   846	            Self::Exists { key, equalities, .. } | Self::Created { key, equalities, .. } => {
   847	                key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_, term)| term)).for_each(|item| term(item, out));
   848	            }
   849	        }
   850	    }
   851	
   852	    /// The disjuncts of the rule: an `or` flattens, anything else is itself.
   853	    pub fn disjuncts(&self) -> Vec<&RuleExpr> {
   854	        match self {
   855	            Self::Or(items) => items.iter().flat_map(|item| item.disjuncts()).collect(),
   856	            other => vec![other],
   857	        }
   858	    }
   859	
   860	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.6 — service seats read on the server and never enlarge a device's audience.
   861	    pub fn service_grant(&self) -> Option<&str> {
   862	        match self {
   863	            Self::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer, RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Id(principal)))
   864	            | Self::Eq(RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Id(principal)), RuleTerm::Viewer)
   865	                if principal.strip_prefix("service:").is_some_and(|name| !name.is_empty()) => Some(principal),
   866	            _ => None,
   867	        }
   868	    }
   869	
   870	    /// Remove only entire service disjuncts; a conjunct, negation or ordinary

@@ -139,6 +139,7 @@ pub fn validate_text(label: &str, text: &str, min: u32, format: Option<TextForma
         return Err(RuntimeError::Input(match format {
             TextFormat::Handle => format!("{label} must be a handle: lowercase letters, digits and underscores"),
             TextFormat::Url => format!("{label} must be an http or https URL"),
+            TextFormat::Email => format!("{label} must be an ASCII email address with one @, a dot-safe local part, and valid domain labels"),
         }));
     }
     Ok(())
diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/ir.rs b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/ir.rs

-        _ => Err(serde::de::Error::custom("text format must be handle or url")),

 }

 impl TextFormat {
@@ -975,6 +976,24 @@ impl TextFormat {
                 .strip_prefix("https://")
                 .or_else(|| text.strip_prefix("http://"))
                 .is_some_and(|rest| !rest.is_empty() && rest.chars().all(|c| !c.is_whitespace() && !c.is_control())),
+            // @ref LLP 3000.013.001#1-decision — one deterministic ASCII mailbox grammar is shared by arguments, writes and devices.
+            Self::Email => {
+                if text.len() > 254 { return false; }
+                let Some((local, domain)) = text.split_once('@') else { return false };
+                if domain.contains('@') || local.is_empty() || local.len() > 64
+                    || domain.is_empty() || domain.len() > 253
+                    || local.starts_with('.') || local.ends_with('.') || local.contains("..")
+                {
+                    return false;
+                }
+                let local_ok = local.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric()
+                    || matches!(b, b'.' | b'!' | b'#' | b'$' | b'%' | b'&' | b'\'' | b'*'
+                        | b'+' | b'/' | b'=' | b'?' | b'^' | b'_' | b'`' | b'{' | b'|'
+                        | b'}' | b'~' | b'-'));
+                local_ok && domain.split('.').all(|label| !label.is_empty() && label.len() <= 63
+                    && !label.starts_with('-') && !label.ends_with('-')
+                    && label.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'-'))
+            }
         }
     }
 }
   399	    fn apply_aggregate(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, key: Vec<Value>, change: i128, source_present: Option<bool>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   400	        if self.schema.timelines.iter().any(|t| t.owns(&maintain.target)) {
   401	            return Err(super::timeline::refusal("E_TIMELINE_OWNED", "timeline-owned tables cannot be aggregate targets; maintain a mapped application table instead"));
   402	        }
   403	        let id = maintained_id(&key);
   404	        let target = self.schema.table(&maintain.target).expect("validated");
   405	        self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Examined, 1)?;
   406	        let existing = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.get(allowance, &maintain.target, &id))?;
   407	        let target_key = target_group_key(maintain, &key);
   408	        if self.device && existing.is_none() && !self.upsert_absence_proven(&maintain.target, "byGroup", &target_key)? {
   409	            self.unknown_fact(&maintain.target, "byGroup", &target_key, "aggregate base");
   410	            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction("the affected aggregate value or its absence is not known locally; queued without a predicted row".into()));
   411	        }
   412	        let old_value = existing.as_ref().and_then(|row| row.field("value").ok()).map(Value::as_int).transpose()?.unwrap_or(0);
   413	        let next = i128::from(old_value) + change;
   414	        if !(I53_MIN as i128..=I53_MAX as i128).contains(&next) {
   415	            return Err(aggregate_overflow(maintain));
   416	        }
   417	        let keep_zero = next == 0 && self.live_sum(maintain) && match source_present {
   418	            Some(present) => present,
   419	            None => self.aggregate_source_present(maintain, &key)?,
   420	        };
   421	        if next == 0 && !keep_zero {
   422	            if let Some(existing) = existing {
   423	                self.refuse_timeline_write(&maintain.target, crate::schema::Effect::Delete)?;
   424	                let entries = self.tx.delete(&maintain.target, target, &existing)?;
   425	                self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::IndexEntries, entries)?;
   426	                self.row_changed(&maintain.target, Some(&existing), None, Origin::Maintained)?;
   427	            }
   428	            self.mirror_column(maintain, &key, 0)?;
   429	            return Ok(());
   430	        }
   431	        let mut row = crate::small_map::collect([("id".to_owned(), Value::Id(id.clone())), ("value".to_owned(), Value::Int(next as i64))]);
   432	        for (field, value) in maintain.group_fields().iter().zip(key.iter().cloned()) {
   433	            row.insert(field.clone(), value);
   434	        }
   435	        // @ref LLP 3000.013.001#3-global-maintained-view-representation — stores index the one logical empty group by a hidden constant.
   436	        if maintain.by.is_empty() { row.insert(GLOBAL.into(), Value::Bool(true)); }
   437	        let row = Value::Row(row.into());
   438	        if existing.as_ref() == Some(&row) { return Ok(()); }
   439	        let entries = self.tx.put(&maintain.target, target, &row, existing.as_ref())?;
   440	        self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::IndexEntries, entries)?;
   441	        self.row_changed(&maintain.target, existing.as_ref(), Some(&row), Origin::Maintained)?;
   442	        self.mirror_column(maintain, &key, next as i64)
   443	    }
   444	}
   445	
   446	pub(super) fn delta(maintain: &Maintain, source: &Value, direction: i64) -> Result<i128, RuntimeError> {
   447	    Ok(match &maintain.kind {
   448	        MaintainKind::Count => i128::from(direction),
   449	        MaintainKind::Sum { of } => i128::from(direction).checked_mul(i128::from(source.field(of)?.as_int()?)).ok_or_else(|| aggregate_overflow(maintain))?,
   450	    })
   451	}
   452	
   453	// @ref LLP 3000.007#17 — an internal sum names its maintain and column, never a retained contribution or computed value.
   454	pub(super) fn aggregate_overflow(maintain: &Maintain) -> RuntimeError {
   455	    RuntimeError::AggregateOverflow { maintain: maintain.private.as_ref().map_or_else(|| maintain.target.clone(), |o|o.table.clone()), column: maintain.private.as_ref().map_or_else(|| "value".into(), |o|o.column.clone()) }

    14	fn restricted(expr: &Expr) -> Diagnostic {
    15	    refusal(expr.site(), format!("maintain cannot evaluate {}; predicates compare row-local scalar columns and literals", crate::print::expression(expr)),
    16	        "store this fact in a source field and compare .field with a literal, or use a bounded job")
    17	}
    18	
    19	/// The leading dot is optional in the intent spelling of a bare group key.
    20	pub(super) fn bare_key(expr: &Expr) -> Option<&str> {
    21	    match expr {
    22	        Expr::Name { name, .. } => Some(name),
    23	        Expr::Member { base, name, .. } if matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Focal { .. }) => Some(name),
    24	        _ => None,
    25	    }
    26	}
    27	
    28	pub(super) fn normalize_keys(maintain: &mut MaintainDecl) {
    29	    for (field, key) in maintain.by.iter_mut().zip(&mut maintain.keys) {
    30	        if let Some(name) = bare_key(key) {
    31	            *field = name.to_owned();
    32	            *key = Expr::Name { name: name.to_owned(), site: key.site().clone() };
    33	        } else if let Some(name) = path(key) {
    34	            *field = name;
    35	        }
    36	    }
    37	}
    38	
    39	// @ref LLP 3000.013#3.2 — derive an ordinary rule, then admit it on the generated table.
    40	pub(super) fn derived_read(catalog: &Catalog, maintain: &MaintainDecl) -> Fallible<RuleDecl> {
    41	    let source = &catalog.tables[&maintain.source];
    42	    let constant = |name: &str| RuleDecl { effect: Effect::Read, bindings: vec![],
    43	        value: Expr::Name { name: name.into(), site: maintain.site.clone() }, site: maintain.site.clone() };
    44	    if source.decl.public.is_some() { return Ok(constant("allow")); }
    45	    let Some(read) = source.decl.rules.iter().find(|rule| rule.effect == Effect::Read) else { return Ok(constant("deny")); };
    46	    if source.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t| maintain.by.contains(&t.reference)) { return Ok(constant("deny")); }
    47	    let bound = super::schema::bind_rule(catalog, source, read)?;
    48	    if bound.is_uniformly_readable() { return Ok(constant("allow")); }
    49	    let mut fields = BTreeSet::new();
    50	    bound.fields(&mut fields);
    51	    let aliases = inherited_aliases(source,maintain);
    52	    let bare: BTreeSet<_> = maintain.keys.iter().filter_map(bare_key).chain(aliases.values().map(String::as_str)).collect();
    53	    let missing: Vec<_> = fields.iter().filter(|field| !bare.contains(field.as_str())).map(|field| format!(".{field}")).collect();
    54	    if !missing.is_empty() {
    55	        return Err(Diagnostic::new(&maintain.site, "E_MAINTAIN_READ_RULE",
    56	            format!("maintain {} cannot derive {}'s read rule `{}`: {} are not bare group keys; calendar keys and reference paths do not preserve those fields",
    57	                maintain.name, maintain.source, crate::print::expression(&read.value), missing.join(", ")),
    58	            "read <- deny  -- replace deny with an explicit aggregate read rule over its group keys").guide("maintains"));
    59	    }
    60	    let mut read = read.clone();
    61	    super::intent_walk::expression(&mut read.value,&mut |expr| {
    62	        if let Expr::Member {base,name,..} = expr && matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Focal {..}) {
    63	            if let Some((output,_)) = aliases.iter().find(|(_,capture)| *capture == name) { *name=output.clone(); }
    64	        }
    65	    });
    66	    Ok(RuleDecl { site: maintain.site.clone(), ..read })
    67	}
    68	
    69	pub(super) fn inherited_aliases(source: &TableInfo, maintain: &MaintainDecl) -> BTreeMap<String,String> {
    70	    maintain.by.iter().zip(&maintain.keys).filter_map(|(output,key)| {
    71	        let capture=format!("${}",path(key)?);
    72	        source.through.as_ref()?.fields.contains_key(&capture).then(|| (output.clone(),capture))
    73	    }).collect()
    74	}
    75	
    76	pub(super) fn inherited_keys(source: &TableInfo, maintain: &mut MaintainDecl) {
    77	    for (output,key) in maintain.by.iter_mut().zip(&maintain.keys) {
    78	        if let Some(path)=path(key) && source.captures.contains_key(&format!("${path}")) {
    79	            *output=path.rsplit('.').next().unwrap().into();
    80	        }
    81	    }
    82	}
    83	
    84	/// Only maintain binding sees these synthetic columns; ordinary programs never do.
    85	pub(super) fn recorded_paths(tables: &BTreeMap<String, TableInfo>, maintain: &MaintainDecl) -> Fallible<BTreeMap<String, ScalarType>> {
    86	    fn visit(tables: &BTreeMap<String, TableInfo>, source: &TableInfo, expr: &Expr, out: &mut BTreeMap<String, ScalarType>) -> Fallible<()> {
    87	        match expr {
    88	            Expr::Member { base, name, .. } if !matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Focal { .. }) => {
    89	                let fail = |message: String| refusal(expr.site(), message, "use one immutable reference to a public table and an immutable target column (or id)");
    90	                let Some(reference) = bare_key(base) else { return Err(fail("maintain reference paths have exactly one hop".into())); };
    91	                let Some(ScalarType { kind: TypeKind::Ref { table }, .. }) = source.columns.get(reference) else { return Err(fail(format!("{reference} must be a reference column of {}", source.decl.name))); };
    92	                if !source.decl.immutable.iter().any(|(fields, _)| fields.iter().any(|field| field == reference)) { return Err(fail(format!("{}.{} must be immutable", source.decl.name, reference))); }
    93	                let target = tables.get(table).ok_or_else(|| fail(format!("unknown target table {table}")))?;
    94	                if source.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t| t.reference == reference && t.fields.contains_key(&format!("${reference}.{name}"))) { return Ok(()); }
    95	                let reads: Vec<_> = target.decl.rules.iter().filter(|rule| rule.effect == Effect::Read).collect();
    96	                let public = match (target.decl.public.is_some(), reads.as_slice()) {
    97	                    (true, []) => true,
    98	                    (false, [rule]) if !matches!(&rule.value, Expr::Call { name, args, .. } if name == "public" && !args.is_empty()) => {
    99	                        let catalog = super::catalog::Catalog { tables: tables.clone(), ..Default::default() };
   100	                        super::schema::bind_rule(&catalog, target, rule).is_ok_and(|rule| rule.is_uniformly_readable())
   101	                    }
   102	                    _ => false,
   103	                };
   104	                if !public { return Err(fail(format!("{table}'s effective read rule must be unconditionally public or allow; use one read policy without public(...) arguments"))); }
   105	                let mut ty = if name == "id" { ScalarType { kind: TypeKind::Id, optional: false } }
   106	                    else { target.columns.get(name).cloned().ok_or_else(|| fail(format!("unknown column {table}.{name}")))? };
   107	                if name != "id" && !target.decl.immutable.iter().any(|(fields, _)| fields.contains(name)) { return Err(fail(format!("{table}.{name} must be immutable"))); }
   108	                ty.optional = true;
   109	                out.insert(format!("{reference}.{name}"), ty);
   110	            }
   111	            Expr::Call { args, .. } => for arg in args { visit(tables, source, arg, out)?; },
   112	            Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => { visit(tables, source, left, out)?; visit(tables, source, right, out)?; }
   113	            Expr::Unary { value, .. } => visit(tables, source, value, out)?,
   114	            _ => {}
   115	        }
   116	        Ok(())
   117	    }
   118	    let mut out = BTreeMap::new();
   119	    if let Some(source) = tables.get(&maintain.source) {
   120	        for key in &maintain.keys { visit(tables, source, key, &mut out)?; }
   121	        if let Some(filter) = &maintain.filter { visit(tables, source, filter, &mut out)?; }
   122	    }
   123	    Ok(out)
   124	}
   125	
   126	fn path(expr: &Expr) -> Option<String> {
   127	    match expr {
   128	        Expr::Member { base, name, .. } => match base.as_ref() {
   129	            Expr::Focal { .. } => Some(name.clone()),
   130	            other => bare_key(other).map(|reference| format!("{reference}.{name}")),
   131	        },
   132	        _ => None,
   133	    }
   134	}
   135	
   136	pub(super) fn keys(source: &TableInfo, maintain: &MaintainDecl) -> Fallible<BTreeMap<String, CalendarKey>> {
   137	    let mut computed = BTreeMap::new();
   138	    let mut names = BTreeSet::from(["id".to_owned(), "value".to_owned()]);
   139	    for (field, key) in maintain.by.iter().zip(&maintain.keys) {
   140	        if !names.insert(field.clone()) { return Err(refusal(key.site(), format!("maintain output name {field} is duplicated or reserved"), "use distinct bare keys and at most one day, hour and bucket key; id and value are reserved")); }
   141	        match key {
   142	            Expr::Name { name, .. } if source.columns.contains_key(name) => {}
   143	            Expr::Member { .. } if path(key).is_some_and(|p| source.recorded.contains_key(&p) || source.captures.contains_key(&format!("${p}"))) => {}
   144	            Expr::Call { name, args, .. } if matches!(name.as_str(), "day" | "hour" | "bucket" | "month" | "year") => {
   145	                let (millis, offset) = calendar(name, args, key.site())?;
   146	                let column = path(&args[0]).ok_or_else(|| refusal(args[0].site(), "calendar group key needs a source time column or recorded path", "day(.at), hour(postId.at), or bucket(.at, 2m)"))?;
   147	                if !source.columns.get(&column).is_some_and(|ty| ty.kind == TypeKind::Time && (!ty.optional || source.recorded.contains_key(&column))) {
   148	                    return Err(refusal(args[0].site(), format!("{}.{} must be a required time column or recorded time path for {name}", maintain.source, column), format!("{column}: time")));
   149	                }
   150	                computed.insert(field.clone(), CalendarKey { function: name.clone(), column: column.clone(), millis, offset });
   151	            }
   152	            _ => return Err(refusal(key.site(), "maintain group keys are bare source columns or day, hour, bucket, month and year of a required time column", "by authorId, day(.at)")),
   153	        }
   154	    }
   155	    Ok(computed)
   156	}
   157	
   158	pub(super) fn calendar(name: &str, args: &[Expr], site: &Site) -> Fallible<(i64, i64)> {
   159	    // @ref LLP 3000.040#22-builtins — UTC civil periods take no duration and no offset (time zones are deferred).
   160	    if matches!(name, "month" | "year") {
   161	        if args.len() != 1 { return Err(refusal(site, format!("{name} takes one time and no offset: civil periods are UTC"), format!("{name}(.at)"))); }
   162	        return Ok((0, 0));
   163	    }
   164	    let base = if name == "bucket" { 2 } else { 1 };
   165	    let rewrite = "day(time, 8h), hour(time, 30m), or bucket(time, 1d, 8h); omit the offset for UTC epoch boundaries";
   166	    if args.len() != base && args.len() != base + 1 { return Err(refusal(site, "calendar key takes a time, a bucket duration if needed, and an optional literal offset", rewrite)); }
   167	    let literal = |expr: &Expr| match expr {
   168	        Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Number(n), site } => super::integer_literal(n, site),
   169	        _ => Err(refusal(expr.site(), "calendar durations and offsets must be literals", rewrite)),
   170	    };
   171	    let millis = match name { "day" => 86_400_000, "hour" => 3_600_000, _ => literal(&args[1])? };
   172	    if millis <= 0 { return Err(refusal(site, "bucket duration must be positive", "bucket(.at, 2m)")); }
   173	    let offset = if args.len() > base { literal(&args[base])? } else { 0 };
   174	    if offset < 0 || offset >= millis { return Err(refusal(args[base].site(), "calendar offset must be nonnegative and smaller than its period", rewrite)); }
   175	    Ok((millis, offset))
   176	}
   177	
   178	pub(super) fn predicate(source: &TableInfo, expr: &Expr) -> Fallible<Predicate> {
   179	    match expr {
   180	        Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Bool(b), .. } => Ok(Predicate::Bool(*b)),
   181	        Expr::Member { base, name, .. } if matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Focal { .. }) => {
   182	            if source.columns.get(name) == Some(&ScalarType { kind: TypeKind::Bool, optional: false }) { Ok(Predicate::Field(name.clone())) }
   183	            else { Err(refusal(expr.site(), "a bare maintain predicate needs a required boolean column", "compare .column with a scalar literal, or declare column: bool")) }
   184	        }
   185	        Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } if op == "not" => Ok(Predicate::Not(Box::new(predicate(source, value)?))),
   186	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "and" || op == "or" => {
   187	            let (a,b) = (Box::new(predicate(source, left)?), Box::new(predicate(source, right)?));
   188	            Ok(if op == "and" { Predicate::And(a,b) } else { Predicate::Or(a,b) })
   189	        }
   190	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if matches!(op.as_str(), "=" | "!=" | "<" | "<=" | ">" | ">=") => {
   191	            let (mut a, mut at) = operand(source, left)?;
   192	            let (mut b, mut bt) = operand(source, right)?;
   193	            coerce(&mut a, &bt, left)?; coerce(&mut b, &at, right)?;
   194	            if matches!(a, Operand::Literal(Value::Id(_))) && bt.required().is_identity() { at = bt.required(); }
   195	            if matches!(b, Operand::Literal(Value::Id(_))) && at.required().is_identity() { bt = at.required(); }
   196	            // @ref LLP 3000.020#3.1 — row-key comparisons preserve total ordering with null below every value.
   197	            if matches!((&a, &b), (Operand::Field(a), Operand::Field(b))
   198	                if a.starts_with("$row.") != b.starts_with("$row.")) {
   199	                at = at.required(); bt = bt.required();
   200	            }
   201	            let scalar = |ty: &Ty| matches!(ty.required(), Ty::Null | Ty::Bool | Ty::Int | Ty::Decimal { .. } | Ty::Money { .. } | Ty::Time | Ty::Text(_) | Ty::Enum(_) | Ty::Id | Ty::Ref(_) | Ty::Principal);
   202	            if !scalar(&at) || !scalar(&bt) || !at.comparable(&bt) || (!matches!(op.as_str(), "=" | "!=") && (at.is_optional() || bt.is_optional() || matches!(at, Ty::Null) || matches!(bt, Ty::Null))) {
   203	                return Err(refusal(expr.site(), format!("maintain comparison needs compatible scalar types; got {} and {}", at.name(), bt.name()), "compare like scalar types; ordered comparisons require non-optional operands"));
   204	            }
   205	            Ok(Predicate::Compare { op: op.clone(), left: a, right: b })
   206	        }
   207	        _ => Err(restricted(expr)),
   208	    }
   209	}
   210	
   211	fn coerce(value: &mut Operand, ty: &Ty, expr: &Expr) -> Fallible<()> {
   212	    if let Operand::Literal(Value::String(s)) = value {
   213	        if ty.required().is_identity() { *value = Operand::Literal(Value::Id(s.clone())); }
   214	        else if let Ty::Enum(variants) = ty.required() {
   215	            if !variants.contains(s) { return Err(refusal(expr.site(), format!("'{s}' is not an allowed variant"), format!("use one of {}", variants.join(", ")))); }
   216	        }
   217	    }
   218	    Ok(())
   219	}
   220	fn operand(source: &TableInfo, expr: &Expr) -> Fallible<(Operand, Ty)> {
   221	    match expr {
   222	        Expr::Member { .. } if path(expr).is_some() => {
   223	            let name = path(expr).unwrap();
   224	            let ty = if name == "id" { Ty::Id } else { source.columns.get(&name).map(Ty::from_scalar).ok_or_else(|| restricted(expr))? };
   225	            Ok((Operand::Field(name.clone()), ty))
   226	        }
   227	        Expr::Literal { value, site } => Ok(match value {
   228	            Literal::Str(s) => (Operand::Literal(Value::String(s.clone())), Ty::Text(s.chars().count() as u32)),
   229	            Literal::Number(n) => (Operand::Literal(Value::Int(super::integer_literal(n, site)?)), Ty::Int),
   230	            Literal::Bool(b) => (Operand::Literal(Value::Bool(*b)), Ty::Bool),
   231	            Literal::Null => (Operand::Literal(Value::Null), Ty::Null),
   232	        }),
   233	        Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } if op == "-" => {
   234	            if let Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Number(n), site } = value.as_ref() { return Ok((Operand::Literal(Value::Int(super::integer_literal(&format!("-{n}"), site)?)), Ty::Int)); }
   235	            Err(restricted(expr))
   236	        }
   237	        _ => Err(restricted(expr)),
   238	    }
   239	}

  1777	/// The id of a maintained row is a fixed-size digest of its typed group key.

  1785	#[test]
  1786	fn maintained_identity_is_fixed_size_and_domain_separated() {

  1788	    let long = maintained_id(&[Value::String("x".repeat(100_000))]);

  1791	    assert_ne!(short, long);

   311	#[test]
   312	fn email_format_is_one_shared_ascii_contract() {
   313	    let source = r#"
   314	table contacts:
   315	  email: text 3..254 format email
   316	  public
   317	  insert <- allow
   318	  online only
   319	mutation addUnchecked(id: id, email: text 3..254):
   320	  return insert contacts { id, email }
   321	query echo(email: text 3..254 format email):
   322	  return email
   323	"#;
   324	    let mut engine = engine(source);
   325	    for email in ["a@example.com", "first.last+tag@sub.example", "x@localhost"] {
   326	        let result = engine.query("echo", Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::from([("email".into(), Value::String(email.into()))]))).unwrap();
   327	        assert_eq!(result.data, Value::String(email.into()));
   328	    }
   329	    for email in ["a..b@example.com", ".a@example.com", "a@-example.com", "a@example..com", "a@пример.рф", "a@@example.com"] {
   330	        let error = engine.query("echo", Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::from([("email".into(), Value::String(email.into()))]))).unwrap_err();
   331	        assert_eq!(error.code, "E_INPUT", "{email}: {error:?}");
   332	    }
   333	    let rejected = engine.mutate("addUnchecked", None, Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::from([
   334	        ("id".into(), Value::Id("bad".into())), ("email".into(), Value::String("a..b@example.com".into())),
   335	    ]))).unwrap();
   336	    assert!(matches!(rejected.state, WriteState::Failed { ref why, .. } if why.code == "E_CONSTRAINT"), "{rejected:?}");
   337	}

   339	#[test]
   340	fn enum_columns_use_the_existing_word_search_path() {
   341	    let source = r#"
   342	table businesses:
   343	  category: enum('coffee-shop', 'book_store', 'museum')
   344	  search category
   345	  public
   346	  online only
   347	query find(q: text <=40, c: cursor ?):
   348	  return businesses matching q first 20 by .id after c
   349	"#;
   350	    let mut engine = engine(source);
   351	    engine.seed([
   352	        ("businesses".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", Value::Id("cafe".into())), ("category", Value::String("coffee-shop".into()))])),
   353	        ("businesses".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", Value::Id("books".into())), ("category", Value::String("book_store".into()))])),
   354	    ]).unwrap();
   355	    let result = engine.query("find", Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::from([
   356	        ("q".into(), Value::String("cof".into())), ("c".into(), Value::Null),
   357	    ]))).unwrap();
   358	    let rows = result.data.as_array().unwrap();
   359	    assert_eq!(rows.len(), 1);
   360	    assert_eq!(rows[0].field("id").unwrap(), &Value::Id("cafe".into()));
   361	}

   363	#[test]
   364	fn rounded_division_is_exact_signed_and_keeps_numeric_types() {
   365	    let source = r#"
   366	query rounded():
   367	  return [
   368	    divide(5, 2, 'toward-zero'), divide(5, 2, 'away-from-zero'),
   369	    divide(5, 2, 'floor'), divide(5, 2, 'ceiling'),
   370	    divide(5, 2, 'half-away-from-zero'), divide(5, 2, 'half-even'),
   371	    divide(7, 2, 'half-even'), divide(-5, 2, 'half-away-from-zero'),
   372	    divide(-5, 2, 'floor'), divide(-5, 2, 'ceiling'),
   373	    divide(5, -2, 'floor'), divide(5, -2, 'ceiling'),
   374	    divide(7, 3, 'half-even'), divide(8, 3, 'half-even')
   375	  ]
   376	query decimalValue(value: decimal 2):
   377	  return divide(value, 2, 'half-even')
   378	query moneyValue(value: money USD 2):
   379	  return divide(value, 2, 'half-even')
   380	query scaled(numerator: int, denominator: int):
   381	  return [
   382	    divide(numerator, denominator, 2, 'half-even'),
   383	    divide(numerator, denominator, 2, 'half-away-from-zero')
   384	  ]
   385	query wide(numerator: int, denominator: int):
   386	  return divide(numerator, denominator, 12, 'half-even')
   387	query scaleZero(numerator: int, denominator: int):
   388	  return divide(numerator, denominator, 0, 'half-even')
   389	query scaledOverflow():
   390	  return divide(9007199254740991, 1, 1, 'half-even')
   391	query zero():
   392	  return divide(1, 0, 'half-even')
   393	"#;
   394	    let bound = checked(source);
   395	    assert_eq!(bound.programs.iter().find(|p| p.program.name == "decimalValue").unwrap().result, Ty::Decimal(2));
   396	    assert_eq!(bound.programs.iter().find(|p| p.program.name == "moneyValue").unwrap().result, Ty::Money("USD".into(), 2));
   397	    assert_eq!(bound.programs.iter().find(|p| p.program.name == "scaled").unwrap().result,
   398	        Ty::List(Box::new(Ty::Decimal(2)), 2));
   399	    assert_eq!(bound.programs.iter().find(|p| p.program.name == "wide").unwrap().result, Ty::Decimal(12));
   400	    assert_eq!(bound.programs.iter().find(|p| p.program.name == "scaleZero").unwrap().result, Ty::Decimal(0));
   401	    let mut engine = Engine::memory(bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
   402	    let values = engine.query("rounded", Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::new())).unwrap().data;
   403	    assert_eq!(values, Value::Array([2, 3, 2, 3, 3, 2, 4, -3, -3, -2, -3, -2, 2, 3].into_iter().map(Value::Int).collect()));
   404	    for name in ["decimalValue", "moneyValue"] {
   405	        let value = engine.query(name, Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::from([("value".into(), Value::Int(5))]))).unwrap().data;
   406	        assert_eq!(value, Value::Int(2), "{name}");
   407	    }
   408	    let scaled = |engine: &mut Engine, numerator, denominator| engine.query("scaled", Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::from([
   409	        ("numerator".into(), Value::Int(numerator)), ("denominator".into(), Value::Int(denominator)),
   410	    ]))).unwrap().data;
   411	    assert_eq!(scaled(&mut engine, 101, 2), Value::Array(vec![Value::Int(5050), Value::Int(5050)]));
   412	    assert_eq!(scaled(&mut engine, -101, 2), Value::Array(vec![Value::Int(-5050), Value::Int(-5050)]));
   413	    assert_eq!(scaled(&mut engine, 1, 8), Value::Array(vec![Value::Int(12), Value::Int(13)]));
   414	    assert_eq!(scaled(&mut engine, -1, 8), Value::Array(vec![Value::Int(-12), Value::Int(-13)]));
   415	    assert_eq!(engine.query("wide", Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::from([
   416	        ("numerator".into(), Value::Int(snapback4_core::value::I53_MAX)),
   417	        ("denominator".into(), Value::Int(snapback4_core::value::I53_MAX)),
   418	    ]))).unwrap().data, Value::Int(1_000_000_000_000), "the wide scaled intermediate is not restricted to i53");
   419	    assert_eq!(engine.query("scaleZero", Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::from([
   420	        ("numerator".into(), Value::Int(5)), ("denominator".into(), Value::Int(2)),
   421	    ]))).unwrap().data, Value::Int(2));
   422	    assert_eq!(engine.query("scaledOverflow", Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::new())).unwrap_err().code, "E_OVERFLOW");
   423	    assert_eq!(engine.query("zero", Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::new())).unwrap_err().code, "E_DIV_ZERO");
   424	
   425	    use snapback4_core::ir::{Builtin, Expr, Program, ProgramKind, Rounding, Stmt};
   426	    let overflow = Program {
   427	        name: "overflow".into(), kind: ProgramKind::Query, args: BTreeMap::new(),
   428	        read_time_granularity_ms: None, native: false, returned_shapes: BTreeMap::new(),
   429	        body: vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::Divide { rounding: Rounding::TowardZero, scale: None },
   430	            args: vec![Expr::Literal(Value::Int(i64::MIN)), Expr::Literal(Value::Int(-1))] })],
   431	    };
   432	    let mut raw = Engine::memory(Default::default(), [overflow]).unwrap();
   433	    assert_eq!(raw.query("overflow", Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::new())).unwrap_err().code, "E_OVERFLOW");
   434	
   435	    for bad in ["divide(1, 2, 'nearest')", "divide(1, 2, mode)", "divide(1, divisor, 'half-even')",
   436	        "divide(1, 2, 13, 'half-even')", "divide(1, 2, scale, 'half-even')"] {
   437	        let source = if bad.contains("mode") { format!("query bad(mode: enum('half-even')):\n  return {bad}\n") }
   438	            else if bad.contains("scale") { format!("query bad(scale: int):\n  return {bad}\n") }
   439	            else if bad.contains("divisor") { format!("query bad(divisor: decimal 2):\n  return {bad}\n") }
   440	            else { format!("query bad():\n  return {bad}\n") };
   441	        let bound = compile(&[("bad.q".into(), source)]);
   442	        assert!(bound.diagnostics.iter().any(|d| d.code == "E_QUARRY_TYPE"), "{bad}: {:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
   443	    }

     1	/-!
     2	# Wave 12 semantic witnesses
     3	
     4	These executable definitions cover language contracts outside the row IR:
     5	global count visibility, capture-time count provenance, email validation, and
     6	enum values entering the existing word-search path. Rounded division lives in
     7	`Quarry.Eval` because it is an IR builtin.
     8	-/
     9	
    10	namespace Quarry.Wave12
    11	
    12	def globalCount (rows : List α) : Nat := rows.length
    13	
    14	@[simp] theorem globalCount_empty : globalCount ([] : List α) = 0 := rfl
    15	
    16	theorem positive_count_has_member {rows : List α} (h : 0 < globalCount rows) :
    17	    ∃ row, row ∈ rows := by
    18	  cases rows with
    19	  | nil => simp [globalCount] at h
    20	  | cons row rows => exact ⟨row, by simp⟩
    21	
    22	/-- A readable absent global count is zero; hidden hit and miss are both
    23	`none`, so absence never becomes a disclosure channel. -/
    24	def visibleGlobalCount (authorized : Bool) (stored : Option Nat) : Option Nat :=
    25	  if authorized then some (stored.getD 0) else none
    26	
    27	example : visibleGlobalCount true none = some 0 := rfl
    28	example : visibleGlobalCount false none = none := rfl
    29	example : visibleGlobalCount false (some 7) = none := rfl
    30	
    31	/-- Binder provenance is a capture-time domain, transaction and authority
    32	triple. A retained diagnostic origin is intentionally not part of it. -/
    33	structure CountCapture where
    34	  domain : String
    35	  transaction : Nat
    36	  authority : String
    37	  definition : Nat
    38	  deriving DecidableEq
    39	
    40	def countCaptureLive (capture : CountCapture) (domain : String)
    41	    (transaction : Nat) (authority : String) (definition : Nat) : Bool :=
    42	  capture.domain == domain && capture.transaction == transaction &&
    43	    capture.authority == authority && capture.definition == definition
    44	
    45	example : countCaptureLive ⟨"entries/owner=alice", 4, "alice", 7⟩
    46	    "entries/owner=alice" 4 "alice" 7 = true := rfl
    47	example : countCaptureLive ⟨"entries/owner=alice", 4, "alice", 7⟩
    48	    "entries/owner=alice" 5 "alice" 7 = false := rfl
    49	example : countCaptureLive ⟨"entries/owner=alice", 4, "alice", 7⟩
    50	    "entries/owner=bob" 4 "alice" 7 = false := rfl
    51	example : countCaptureLive ⟨"entries/owner=alice", 4, "alice", 7⟩
    52	    "entries/owner=alice" 4 "bob" 7 = false := rfl
    53	example : countCaptureLive ⟨"entries/owner=alice", 4, "alice", 7⟩
    54	    "entries/owner=alice" 4 "alice" 8 = false := rfl
    55	
    56	/-- A keyed maintained count becomes required only for the exact key whose
    57	role predicate was proved in the same transaction. A readable miss is zero;
    58	hidden hits and misses share `none`. -/
    59	def keyedTotalAfterRoleGuard (guardKey readKey : String)
    60	    (sameTransaction authorized : Bool) (stored : Option Nat) : Option Nat :=
    61	  if sameTransaction && authorized && guardKey == readKey
    62	    then some (stored.getD 0)
    63	    else none
    64	
    65	example : keyedTotalAfterRoleGuard "dept-a" "dept-a" true true none = some 0 := rfl
    66	example : keyedTotalAfterRoleGuard "dept-a" "dept-b" true true (some 7) = none := rfl
    67	example : keyedTotalAfterRoleGuard "dept-a" "dept-a" false true (some 7) = none := rfl
    68	example : keyedTotalAfterRoleGuard "dept-a" "dept-a" true false (some 7) = none := rfl
    69	
    70	def asciiAlphaNum (c : Char) : Bool :=
    71	  let n := c.toNat
    72	  (65 ≤ n && n ≤ 90) || (97 ≤ n && n ≤ 122) || (48 ≤ n && n ≤ 57)
    73	
    74	def asciiLower (c : Char) : Char :=
    75	  if 65 ≤ c.toNat && c.toNat ≤ 90 then Char.ofNat (c.toNat + 32) else c
    76	
    77	def splitOnce (needle : Char) : List Char → Option (List Char × List Char)
    78	  | [] => none
    79	  | c :: cs =>
    80	      if c == needle then some ([], cs) else
    81	        (splitOnce needle cs).map fun pair => (c :: pair.1, pair.2)
    82	
    83	def splitDots : List Char → List (List Char)
    84	  | [] => [[]]
    85	  | c :: cs =>
    86	      let rest := splitDots cs
    87	      if c == '.' then [] :: rest else
    88	        match rest with
    89	        | [] => [[c]]
    90	        | label :: labels => (c :: label) :: labels
    91	
    92	def noAdjacentDots : List Char → Bool
    93	  | '.' :: '.' :: _ => false
    94	  | _ :: cs => noAdjacentDots cs
    95	  | [] => true
    96	
    97	def localPunctuation : List Char :=
    98	  ['.', '!', '#', '$', '%', '&', '\'', '*', '+', '/', '=', '?', '^', '_', '`', '{', '|', '}', '~', '-']
    99	
   100	def localPartValid (part : List Char) : Bool :=
   101	  !part.isEmpty && part.length ≤ 64 && part.head? != some '.' &&
   102	    part.getLast? != some '.' && noAdjacentDots part &&
   103	    part.all fun c => asciiAlphaNum c || localPunctuation.contains c
   104	
   105	def domainLabelValid (label : List Char) : Bool :=
   106	  !label.isEmpty && label.length ≤ 63 &&
   107	    label.head?.any asciiAlphaNum && label.getLast?.any asciiAlphaNum &&
   108	    label.all fun c => asciiAlphaNum c || c == '-'
   109	
   110	def emailValid (email : String) : Bool :=
   111	  let chars := email.toList
   112	  chars.length ≤ 254 && chars.all (·.toNat ≤ 127) &&
   113	    chars.count '@' == 1 && match splitOnce '@' chars with
   114	      | some (localChars, domain) => localPartValid localChars &&
   115	          !domain.isEmpty && domain.length ≤ 253 && (splitDots domain).all domainLabelValid
   116	      | none => false
   117	
   118	example : emailValid "first.last+tag@sub.example" = true := by decide
   119	example : emailValid "x@localhost" = true := by decide
   120	example : emailValid "a..b@example.com" = false := by decide
   121	example : emailValid "a@-example.com" = false := by decide
   122	example : emailValid "a@пример.рф" = false := by decide
   123	
   124	/-- ASCII executable witness for enum spellings using the shared word-search
   125	shape: lowercase, alphanumeric token boundaries, and query-prefix matching.
   126	The Rust/device tests own NFC and the production Unicode tables. -/
   127	def asciiTokens (text : String) : List (List Char) :=
   128	  let rec go (remaining current : List Char) (tokens : List (List Char)) :=
   129	    match remaining with
   130	    | [] => (if current.isEmpty then tokens else current.reverse :: tokens).reverse
   131	    | c :: cs =>
   132	        if asciiAlphaNum c then go cs (asciiLower c :: current) tokens
   133	        else go cs [] (if current.isEmpty then tokens else current.reverse :: tokens)
   134	  go text.toList [] []
   135	
   136	def isPrefix : List Char → List Char → Bool
   137	  | [], _ => true
   138	  | _, [] => false
   139	  | p :: ps, w :: ws => p == w && isPrefix ps ws
   140	
   141	def enumMatchesAscii (value query : String) : Bool :=
   142	  let words := asciiTokens value
   143	  (asciiTokens query).all fun term => words.any fun word => isPrefix term word
   144	
   145	example : enumMatchesAscii "coffee-shop" "cof" = true := by decide
   146	example : enumMatchesAscii "book_store" "BOOK" = true := by decide
   147	example : enumMatchesAscii "museum" "music" = false := by decide
   148	
   149	end Quarry.Wave12

+/// Exact rounded division comes from its own stream, preserving every older
+/// case whose stream does not select the new expression.
+fn rounded_division(case: &mut Case) {
+    let mut rng = Rng::new(case.seed ^ ROUND_DIV_STREAM);
+    if rng.pick(6) != 0 || !matches!(case.program.body, Body::Scalar(_)) { return; }
+    let modes = [Rounding::TowardZero, Rounding::AwayFromZero, Rounding::Floor,
+        Rounding::Ceiling, Rounding::HalfAwayFromZero, Rounding::HalfEven];
+    let dividend = if rng.pick(2) == 0 { Expr::Arg("a".into(), Type::Int) }
+        else { Expr::Int([-7, -5, -1, 0, 1, 5, 7][rng.pick(7) as usize]) };
+    let divisor = if rng.pick(2) == 0 { Expr::Arg("b".into(), Type::Int) }
+        else { Expr::Int([-3, -2, -1, 0, 1, 2, 3][rng.pick(7) as usize]) };
+    let scale = match rng.pick(5) { 0 => None, 1 => Some(0), 2 => Some(2), 3 => Some(6), _ => Some(12) };
+    case.program.body = Body::Scalar(Expr::RoundedDivide(
+        modes[rng.pick(modes.len() as u64) as usize].clone(), scale, Box::new(dividend), Box::new(divisor)));
+}
+
 /// Add lower-bound rows from an independent stream. Every selected case has
 /// null-open, before-boundary, exact-boundary and future rows.
 fn timed_disclosure(case: &mut Case) {

             }

+        if let Some((mode, scale)) = rounding(&case) {

     assert!(bounds >= 300, "{bounds} bounded cases in 3,000 seeds");

+    assert_eq!(scales, [None, Some(0), Some(2), Some(6), Some(12)].into_iter().collect());

+/-- Truncating quotient/remainder followed by one exact rounding step. The
+model deliberately derives truncation from the existing Euclidean primitive,
+so its oracle is independent of the Rust implementation. -/
+def roundedDiv (rounding : Rounding) (scale : Option Nat) (x y : Int) : EM Value := do
+  if y = 0 then refuse "E_DIV_ZERO"
+  let x := x * (10 : Int) ^ scale.getD 0
+  let qe := Int.ediv x y
+  let re := Int.emod x y
+  let q := if x < 0 ∧ re ≠ 0 then qe + (if y < 0 then -1 else 1) else qe
+  let r := x - q * y
+  if r = 0 then checkI53 q else
+    let direction : Int := if (x < 0) = (y < 0) then 1 else -1
+    let away := q + direction
+    let result := match rounding with
+      | .towardZero => q
+      | .awayFromZero => away
+      | .floor => if direction < 0 then away else q
+      | .ceiling => if 0 < direction then away else q
+      | .halfAwayFromZero => if Int.natAbs r * 2 ≥ Int.natAbs y then away else q
+      | .halfEven =>
+          if Int.natAbs r * 2 > Int.natAbs y ∨
+              (Int.natAbs r * 2 = Int.natAbs y ∧ Int.emod q 2 ≠ 0)
+          then away else q
+    checkI53 result
+
 /-- A calendar builtin over unbounded integers; the caller checks i53. A
 non-positive period or an offset outside `[0, period)` is unreachable from a
 checked program. -/
@@ -930,6 +955,12 @@ def evalExpr : Nat → Ctx → View → Env → Option Value → Expr → EM (Va
         | some r => return (← checkI53 r, none)
         | none => typeErr
       | _, _ => typeErr
+    | .roundDiv rounding scale a b => do
+      let (x, _) ← ev a
+      let (y, _) ← ev b
+      match x, y with
+      | .s (.int x), .s (.int y) => return (← roundedDiv rounding scale x y, none)
+      | _, _ => typeErr
     | .arr xs => do return (.arr (← evs xs), none)
     | .record fs => do
       let vs ← fs.mapM fun p => do return (p.1, (← ev p.2).1)

   591	fn check_role_server(generated: &Generated) -> Counts {
   592	    let (mut server, _) = compile(&generated.role_source);
   593	    seed_role_engine(&mut server, generated);
   594	    let focus = generated
   595	        .entries
   596	        .first()
   597	        .map_or(generated.viewer, |entry| entry.owner.as_str());
   598	    let focus_count = generated
   599	        .entries
   600	        .iter()
   601	        .filter(|entry| entry.owner == focus)
   602	        .count();
   603	    let checks = [
   604	        (
   605	            "privateEntries",
   606	            vec![],
   607	            role_value(generated.granted, generated.entries.len()),
   608	        ),
   609	        (
   610	            "rawRole",
   611	            vec![("owner", id(focus))],
   612	            role_value(generated.granted, focus_count),
   613	        ),
   614	        (
   615	            "rawRole",
   616	            vec![("owner", id("nobody"))],
   617	            role_value(generated.granted, 0),
   618	        ),
   619	        (
   620	            "guardedRole",
   621	            vec![("owner", id(focus))],
   622	            guarded_value(generated.granted, focus_count),
   623	        ),
   624	        (
   625	            "guardedRole",
   626	            vec![("owner", id("nobody"))],
   627	            guarded_value(generated.granted, 0),
   628	        ),
   629	    ];
   630	    let mut counts = Counts::default();
   631	    for (index, (name, args, expected)) in checks.iter().enumerate() {
   632	        assert_server(
   633	            &mut server,
   634	            generated.viewer,
   635	            name,
   636	            args,
   637	            expected,
   638	            &format!("seed {} role check {index} {name}", generated.seed),
   639	            &mut counts,
   640	        );
   641	    }
   642	    assert_eq!(counts.server, EXPECTED_ROLE_OUTCOMES_PER_SOURCE);
   643	    counts
   644	}

   646	fn assert_narrowing_sources(generated: &Generated) {
   647	    let stale_count = format!(
   648	        r#"{}
   649	mutation staleCount(owner: principal, victim: entries):
   650	  n = count({domain})
   651	  delete entries[victim]
   652	  require n > 0 else EMPTY
   653	  return min({domain}, .score) + 1
   654	"#,
   655	        generated.source,
   656	        domain = generated.count_domain
   657	    );
   658	    let stale_role = format!(
   659	        r#"{}
   660	mutation staleRole(owner: principal, grant: staff):
   661	  require exists staff[principal = viewer, role = '{role}'] else NOT_FINANCE
   662	  delete staff[grant]
   663	  return totals[owner] + 1
   664	"#,
   665	        generated.role_source,
   666	        role = generated.role
   667	    );
   668	    for (name, source) in [("count", stale_count), ("role", stale_role)] {
   669	        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[(format!("stale-{name}.q"), source)]);
   670	        assert_eq!(
   671	            bound
   672	                .diagnostics
   673	                .iter()
   674	                .map(|diagnostic| diagnostic.code.as_str())
   675	                .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
   676	            ["E_QUARRY_TYPE"],
   677	            "seed {} stale {name}: {:#?}",
   678	            generated.seed,
   679	            bound.diagnostics
   680	        );
   681	        assert!(
   682	            bound.diagnostics[0].message.contains("int ?"),
   683	            "seed {} stale {name}: {:#?}",
   684	            generated.seed,
   685	            bound.diagnostics
   686	        );
   687	    }
   688	    for (name, source) in [
   689	        ("count", generated.source.clone()),
   690	        ("role", generated.role_source.clone()),
   691	    ] {
   692	        let fresh = snapback4_lang::compile(&[(format!("fresh-{name}.q"), source)]);
   693	        assert!(
   694	            fresh.diagnostics.is_empty(),
   695	            "seed {} fresh {name}: {:#?}",
   696	            generated.seed,
   697	            fresh.diagnostics
   698	        );
   699	    }
   700	}

   702	#[test]
   703	fn generated_sources_cover_wave12_semantics_on_server_and_both_devices() {
   704	    let generated = (0..GENERATED_SOURCES as u64)
   705	        .map(source)
   706	        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
   707	    for case in &generated {
   708	        assert_narrowing_sources(case);
   709	    }
   710	
   711	    let mut memory = Memory::new();
   712	    let mut sqlite = Sqlite::new("wave12-generated-semantics");
   713	    let role_counts =
   714	        generated
   715	            .iter()
   716	            .map(check_role_server)
   717	            .fold(Counts::default(), |mut total, count| {
   718	                total.server += count.server;
   719	                total
   720	            });
   721	    let memory_counts = generated
   722	        .iter()
   723	        .map(|case| check_runtime(&mut memory, case))
   724	        .fold(Counts::default(), |mut total, count| {
   725	            total.server += count.server;
   726	            total.device += count.device;
   727	            total.snapshot_pages += count.snapshot_pages;
   728	            total.stream_pages += count.stream_pages;
   729	            total
   730	        });
   731	    let sqlite_counts = generated
   732	        .iter()
   733	        .map(|case| check_runtime(&mut sqlite, case))
   734	        .fold(Counts::default(), |mut total, count| {
   735	            total.server += count.server;
   736	            total.device += count.device;
   737	            total.snapshot_pages += count.snapshot_pages;
   738	            total.stream_pages += count.stream_pages;
   739	            total
   740	        });
   741	    let expected = GENERATED_SOURCES * EXPECTED_OUTCOMES_PER_RUNTIME;
   742	    assert_eq!(
   743	        (memory_counts.server, memory_counts.device),
   744	        (expected, expected),
   745	        "{memory_counts:?}"
   746	    );
   747	    assert_eq!(
   748	        (sqlite_counts.server, sqlite_counts.device),
   749	        (expected, expected),
   750	        "{sqlite_counts:?}"
   751	    );
   752	    assert_eq!(
   753	        role_counts.server,
   754	        GENERATED_SOURCES * EXPECTED_ROLE_OUTCOMES_PER_SOURCE
   755	    );
   756	    assert_eq!(
   757	        (memory_counts.stream_pages, sqlite_counts.stream_pages),

   761	        memory_counts.snapshot_pages >= GENERATED_SOURCES

   774	    );

     1	//! Wave 12 language parity on the web-memory and SQLite device stores.
     2	mod common;
     3	use common::{Host, Memory, Sqlite, compile, id, snapshot_pages, stream_page};
     4	use serde_json::json;
     5	use snapback4_core::{Context, Value, WriteState};
     6	
     7	const SOURCE: &str = r#"
     8	table entries:
     9	  category: enum('coffee-shop', 'book_store', 'museum')
    10	  contact: text 3..254 format email
    11	  amount: decimal 2
    12	  search category
    13	  public 'wave 12 device fixture'
    14	  insert <- allow
    15	  sync public
    16	view total: count entries
    17	mutation add(id: id, category: enum('coffee-shop', 'book_store', 'museum'), contact: text 3..254, amount: decimal 2):
    18	  return insert entries { id, category, contact, amount }
    19	query totalEntries():
    20	  return total
    21	query search(q: text <=40, c: cursor ?):
    22	  return entries matching q first 20 by .id after c
    23	query extrema():
    24	  n = count(entries)
    25	  require n > 0 else EMPTY
    26	  return { low: min(entries, .amount), high: max(entries, .amount), sum: sum(entries, .amount) }
    27	query rounded(value: decimal 2):
    28	  return [divide(value, 2, 'half-even'), divide(value, 2, 'half-away-from-zero')]
    29	query average(total: int, count: int):
    30	  return divide(total, count, 2, 'half-even')
    31	query email(value: text 3..254 format email):
    32	  return value
    33	"#;
    34	
    35	fn parity<H: Host>(host: &mut H) {
    36	    let (mut server, backend) = compile(SOURCE);
    37	    server.seed([
    38	        ("entries".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([
    39	            ("id", id("a")), ("category", Value::String("coffee-shop".into())),
    40	            ("contact", Value::String("a@example.com".into())), ("amount", Value::Int(5)),
    41	        ])),
    42	        ("entries".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([
    43	            ("id", id("b")), ("category", Value::String("book_store".into())),
    44	            ("contact", Value::String("b@example.com".into())), ("amount", Value::Int(-5)),
    45	        ])),
    46	    ]).unwrap();
    47	    let mut device = host.open("alice", Some(backend)).unwrap();
    48	    let pages = snapshot_pages(&mut server, "alice", 100);
    49	    for (index, page) in pages.iter().enumerate() { device.apply(page, index == 0).unwrap(); }
    50	
    51	    assert_eq!(device.query("totalEntries", "alice", &json!({}), 10).unwrap().data, Value::Int(2));
    52	    assert_eq!(device.query("extrema", "alice", &json!({}), 10).unwrap().data,
    53	        snapback4_core::value::object([("low", Value::Int(-5)), ("high", Value::Int(5)), ("sum", Value::Int(0))]));
    54	    assert_eq!(device.query("rounded", "alice", &json!({"value":5}), 10).unwrap().data,
    55	        Value::Array(vec![Value::Int(2), Value::Int(3)]));
    56	    assert_eq!(device.query("average", "alice", &json!({"total":101,"count":2}), 10).unwrap().data, Value::Int(5050));
    57	    assert_eq!(device.query("average", "alice", &json!({"total":-1,"count":8}), 10).unwrap().data, Value::Int(-12));
    58	    assert_eq!(device.query("email", "alice", &json!({"value":"a@localhost"}), 10).unwrap().data,
    59	        Value::String("a@localhost".into()));
    60	    assert_eq!(device.query("email", "alice", &json!({"value":"a..b@example.com"}), 10).unwrap_err().code, "E_INPUT");
    61	    assert_eq!(device.predict("add", "alice", &json!({
    62	        "id":"bad", "category":"museum", "contact":"a..b@example.com", "amount":0
    63	    }), 10, vec![], 1).unwrap_err().code(), "E_CONSTRAINT", "{}", host.label());
    64	    let hits = device.query("search", "alice", &json!({"q":"cof","c":null}), 10).unwrap();
    65	    assert_eq!(hits.data.as_array().unwrap().len(), 1);
    66	    assert_eq!(hits.data.as_array().unwrap()[0].field("id").unwrap(), &id("a"));
    67	
    68	    let watermark = pages.last().unwrap().watermark;
    69	    let result = server.mutate("add", None, Context::new("alice", [
    70	        ("id".into(), id("c")), ("category".into(), Value::String("museum".into())),
    71	        ("contact".into(), Value::String("c@example.com".into())), ("amount".into(), Value::Int(9)),
    72	    ].into_iter().collect())).unwrap();
    73	    assert!(matches!(result.state, WriteState::Sent { .. }), "{result:?}");
    74	    device.apply(&stream_page(&mut server, "alice", watermark), false).unwrap();
    75	    assert_eq!(device.query("totalEntries", "alice", &json!({}), 10).unwrap().data, Value::Int(3));
    76	    host.close("alice", device);
    77	    let mut reopened = host.open("alice", None).unwrap();
    78	    assert_eq!(reopened.query("totalEntries", "alice", &json!({}), 10).unwrap().data, Value::Int(3),
    79	        "the compiler-owned global discriminator survives store reopen");
    80	    host.close("alice", reopened);
    81	}
    82	
    83	#[test]
    84	fn wave12_semantics_match_on_both_device_stores() {
    85	    parity(&mut Memory::new());
    86	    parity(&mut Sqlite::new("wave12-language"));
    87	}

    89	#[test]
    90	fn pre_wave_closed_enums_reject_email_and_rounded_division() {
    91	    #[allow(dead_code)]
    92	    #[derive(Debug, serde::Deserialize)]
    93	    enum PreWaveTextFormat { Handle, Url }
    94	    #[allow(dead_code)]
    95	    #[derive(Debug, serde::Deserialize)]
    96	    enum PreWaveBuiltin { Count, Sum }
    97	
    98	    let email = serde_json::to_value(snapback4_core::schema::TextFormat::Email).unwrap();
    99	    let divide = serde_json::to_value(snapback4_core::ir::Builtin::Divide {
   100	        rounding: snapback4_core::ir::Rounding::HalfEven,
   101	        scale: Some(2),
   102	    }).unwrap();
   103	    assert!(serde_json::from_value::<PreWaveTextFormat>(email).unwrap_err().to_string().contains("unknown variant"));
   104	    assert!(serde_json::from_value::<PreWaveBuiltin>(divide).unwrap_err().to_string().contains("unknown variant"));
   105	}

     1	//! Merge sources share the statement loop's type checking and runtime domain.
     2	use super::*;
     3	use snapback4_core::ir::LaneSource;
     4	
     5	impl Binder<'_> {
     6	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#21.6 — query lanes take statement-for domains except walk; the meter bounds discovery.
     7	    pub(super) fn lane_domain(&mut self, domain: &A, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(LaneSource, Ty)> {
     8	        if self.caller_kind != ProgramKind::Query && !matches!(domain, A::Read(_)) {
     9	            return Err(crate::diag::syntax_with(domain.site(), "range and list merge lanes belong in queries", "read the merge in a query; keep the job or mutation's existing page loop").guide("reads"));
    10	        }
    11	        let (bound, ty) = self.loop_domain(domain, out).map_err(|error| {
    12	            if matches!(error.code.as_str(), "E_UNBOUND_LOOP") || error.message.starts_with("for cannot iterate") || error.message.contains("static bound") {
    13	                crate::diag::syntax_with(domain.site(), "a merge for lane requires a prefix page, range(a, b), or a bounded list", "for h in range(hour(now) - 24, hour(now) + 1), for a in authors (authors: [principal] <=50), or for row in table[prefix] by index").guide("reads")
    14	            } else { error }
    15	        })?;
    16	        let source = match bound {
    17	            LoopDomain::Page(scan) => LaneSource::Scan(scan),
    18	            LoopDomain::LiteralRange { start, end_exclusive } => LaneSource::Range { start: ir::lit(Value::Int(start)), end_exclusive: ir::lit(Value::Int(end_exclusive)), max: Some(width(start, end_exclusive)) },
    19	            LoopDomain::ExpressionRange { start, end_exclusive } => LaneSource::Range { start, end_exclusive, max: range_width(domain) },
    20	            LoopDomain::ArgList { name, max } => LaneSource::List { value: ir::var(&name), max },
    21	            LoopDomain::DerivedArray { value, max, .. } => LaneSource::List { value, max },
    22	            LoopDomain::Recurse(_) => return Err(crate::diag::syntax_with(domain.site(), "a merge for lane requires a prefix page, range(a, b), or a bounded list; walk is not a lane domain", "for h in range(hour(now) - 24, hour(now) + 1), for a in authors (authors: [principal] <=50), or for row in table[prefix] by index").guide("reads")),
    23	        };
    24	        Ok((source, ty))
    25	    }
    26	}
    27	
    28	fn width(start: i64, end: i64) -> u64 { (i128::from(end) - i128::from(start)).max(0) as u64 }
    29	
    30	fn range_width(domain: &A) -> Option<u64> {
    31	    fn affine(value: &A) -> Option<(Option<&A>, i64)> {
    32	        if let Some(value) = literal_int(value) { return Some((None, value)); }
    33	        if let A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = value {
    34	            if matches!(op.as_str(), "+" | "-") && let Some(n) = literal_int(right) {
    35	                let (base, offset) = affine(left)?;
    36	                return Some((base, if op == "+" { offset.checked_add(n)? } else { offset.checked_sub(n)? }));
    37	            }
    38	        }
    39	        // This comparison is for stable scalar expressions only, never reads,
    40	        // helper calls or new ids whose repeated evaluation may differ.
    41	        fn stable(value: &A) -> bool {
    42	            match value {
    43	                A::Name { .. } | A::Literal { .. } => true,
    44	                A::Unary { value, .. } => stable(value),
    45	                A::Binary { left, right, .. } => stable(left) && stable(right),
    46	                A::Call { name, args, .. } if matches!(name.as_str(), "day" | "hour" | "bucket" | "month" | "year" | "monthStart" | "yearStart") => args.iter().all(stable),
    47	                _ => false,
    48	            }
    49	        }
    50	        stable(value).then_some((Some(value), 0))
    51	    }
    52	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#14 — ruling 28's dynamic widths stay meter-bounded; only structurally equal stable bases admit offset folding.
    53	    // Source locations do not matter, but precedence does: the printer is not
    54	    // an identity for expressions such as (a + b) * 2 and a + b * 2.
    55	    fn same(a: &A, b: &A) -> bool {
    56	        match (a, b) {
    57	            (A::Name { name: a, .. }, A::Name { name: b, .. }) => a == b,
    58	            (A::Literal { value: a, .. }, A::Literal { value: b, .. }) => a == b,
    59	            (A::Unary { op: a, value: av, .. }, A::Unary { op: b, value: bv, .. }) => a == b && same(av, bv),
    60	            (A::Binary { op: a, left: al, right: ar, .. }, A::Binary { op: b, left: bl, right: br, .. }) => a == b && same(al, bl) && same(ar, br),
    61	            (A::Call { name: a, args: av, .. }, A::Call { name: b, args: bv, .. }) => a == b && av.len() == bv.len() && av.iter().zip(bv).all(|(a, b)| same(a, b)),
    62	            _ => false,
    63	        }
    64	    }
    65	    let A::Call { args, .. } = domain else { return None };
    66	    let (a, start) = affine(&args[0])?;
    67	    let (b, end) = affine(&args[1])?;
    68	    match (a, b) {
    69	        (None, None) => Some(width(start, end)),
    70	        (Some(a), Some(b)) if same(a, b) => Some(width(start, end)),
    71	        _ => None,
    72	    }
    73	}

    75	// A lane-dependent predicate needs the eager binding snapshot. Plain row
    76	// predicates can keep the same head strategy as explicitly written lanes.
    77	pub(super) fn filter_uses_lane(value: &A, name: &str) -> bool {
    78	    let read_uses_lane = |read: &ast::ReadExpr| {
    79	        read.keys.iter().any(|key| filter_uses_lane(&key.value, name))
    80	            || read.seeks.iter().any(|seek| filter_uses_lane(&seek.value, name))
    81	            || read.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(|filter| filter_uses_lane(filter, name))
    82	            || read.matching.as_deref().is_some_and(|matching| filter_uses_lane(matching, name))
    83	            || read.range.as_ref().and_then(|range| range.cursor.as_deref())
    84	                .is_some_and(|cursor| filter_uses_lane(cursor, name))
    85	            || read.computed.values().any(|value| filter_uses_lane(value, name))
    86	    };
    87	    match value {
    88	        A::Name { name: used, .. } => used == name,
    89	        A::Focal { .. } | A::Literal { .. } => false,
    90	        A::Member { base, .. } | A::Unary { value: base, .. } => filter_uses_lane(base, name),
    91	        A::Binary { left, right, .. } => filter_uses_lane(left, name) || filter_uses_lane(right, name),
    92	        A::Call { args, .. } => args.iter().any(|arg| filter_uses_lane(arg, name)),
    93	        // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — an independent fixed point guard does
    94	        // not need a snapshot of the varying membership row.
    95	        A::Exists { read, .. } | A::Created { read, .. } | A::Read(read) => read_uses_lane(read),
    96	        _ => true,
    97	    }
    98	}
   520	            _ => {}
   521	        }
   522	    }

   524	    pub(super) fn field_refinement(&self, expr: &A) -> Option<Ty> {

   553	                let mut merge = self.catalog.view_reads[name].clone(); merge.site = domain.site().clone();
   554	                self.loop_domain(&A::Merge(merge), out)
